### [1] [Robustness as a Transferable Skill: Cross-Task Adversarial Defense for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18702)
*Jinquan Zhang,Run Yang,Dongfu Yin,Qiang Lai,Yufeng Yan,Pengshuai Yin,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究VLA策略的对抗视觉鲁棒性跨任务迁移：单次源任务防御更新即可提升未见任务鲁棒性，并提出SN-TRV方法组合多源防御更新，在无需目标侧对抗训练的情况下显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略对对抗性视觉扰动脆弱，而现有防御通常需要针对具体任务进行对抗微调，成本高且难以泛化。作者希望探索源操作任务上学到的鲁棒性是否能迁移保护独立训练的目标策略，从而免除目标侧的对抗训练。

Method: 首先验证单一源防御更新的跨任务迁移性；进而提出Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector（SN-TRV）：保留各源更新均值的方向，并利用源更新范数校准其幅度，形成可迁移的鲁棒性更新向量，并将其直接施加到目标视觉编码器上，无需目标对抗数据、目标侧梯度优化或额外推理模块。

Result: 在三种VLA骨干网络、固定与自适应攻击、以及多种源防御配方下，方法相比朴素平均具有更好的鲁棒性，同时揭示了攻击覆盖范围的局限性。在真实PiPER机器人上，打印对抗补丁下的平均成功率从20.3%提升至45.8%。

Conclusion: 源任务学到的鲁棒性可跨任务复用并组合，SN-TRV为VLA策略提供了一种无需目标侧对抗训练的高效防御迁移方案，但在攻击覆盖范围上仍存在局限。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to adversarial visual perturbations, while defenses often require task-specific adversarial fine-tuning. We investigate whether robustness learned on source manipulation tasks can protect independently specialized target policies without target-side adversarial training. We first show that even a single source defense update improves robustness on held-out tasks, establishing cross-task defense reuse independently of multi-source aggregation. To combine source updates, we introduce Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector (SN-TRV), which retains the direction of their mean while calibrating its magnitude using source-update norms. The resulting update is applied to the target visual encoder without target adversarial data, target-side gradient optimization, or additional inference modules. Evaluations across three VLA backbones, fixed and adaptive attacks, and different source defense recipes show improved robustness over naive averaging and identify limits in attack coverage. Physical PiPER trials increase average success under printed adversarial patches from 20.3% to 45.8%.

</details>


### [2] [Technical Report of Auriga's 1:10 Scale Autonomous Vehicle Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18855)
*Marya Sabouri Dodaran,Ali Taheri Garakani,Mandana Shabanzadeh Nasrollah-Abadi,Yousef Ashrafi,Mohammad Mahdi Azimi,Erfan Nekouei Ghahfarokhi,Amirhossein Kheiri Holighi,Seyyed Sobhan Hosseini Hajibakandeh,Matin Jamshidi,Amirhossein Gholizadeh Behbahani,Mohammad Hossein Moaiyeri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍Auriga Robotics团队在伊朗Shahid Beheshti大学研发的1:10比例自动驾驶小车平台，整合定制机械底盘、基于ESP32的分布式嵌入式系统与Jetson Orin NX计算模块，并采用ROS、模块化固件及Stanley/PID/Pure Pursuit控制算法实现结构化场景下的稳定自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自动驾驶研究提供一个可复用的模块化小型实验平台，降低真实车辆测试成本，同时验证感知—决策—控制一体化架构在类城市场景中的可行性与可靠性。

Method: 硬件上采用定制机械底盘、ESP32分布式嵌入式层（负责传感器接口、实时数据采集与执行器控制）与Jetson Orin NX（负责基于深度学习的车道线、交通标志与红绿灯检测及决策与轨迹控制）；两层通过结构化UART链路同步通信；软件上基于ROS构建，配套模块化固件，并集成Stanley、PID、Pure Pursuit三种控制算法。

Result: 系统在结构化驾驶场景中实现稳定自主导航，在类城市环境中展现出良好的鲁棒性与可靠性，感知与控制子系统能够同步协同运行。

Conclusion: 该1:10平台验证了嵌入式分布式架构加高性能边缘计算加多控制算法融合方案的可行性，可作为未来自动驾驶研究的模块化基础平台。

Abstract: This report presents the design and implementation of Auriga Robotics' 1:10 scale autonomous vehicle, developed at Shahid Beheshti University's Robotics and Intelligent Automation Laboratory. The platform integrates a custom mechanical chassis, a distributed embedded system based on ESP32 microcontrollers, and a Jetson Orin NX module for high-level perception and control. The embedded layer manages sensor interfacing, real-time data acquisition, and actuator control, while the Jetson handles environment perception through deep-learning--based lane, sign, and traffic light detection, as well as decision-making and trajectory control. System communication is achieved via structured UART links, ensuring synchronized operation between perception and control subsystems. The vehicle's software stack incorporates ROS, modular firmware design, and multiple control algorithms---Stanley, PID, and Pure Pursuit---to achieve stable autonomous navigation in structured driving scenarios. The overall system demonstrates robust performance and reliability in urban-like environments, serving as a modular research platform for future autonomous driving studies.

</details>


### [3] [Passage-Focused Soft Coordination for Hierarchical Multi-Robot Navigation in Bottleneck Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18877)
*Haoze Dong,Stepan Dergachev,Meng Guo,Konstantin Yakovlev,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以"通道（passage）"为核心的多机器人导航框架，将软性全局协调与灵活局部执行结合，在1200个实例与8机器人硬件实验中实现近乎完美的成功率并优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性全局调度对执行偏差敏感，而纯局部方法缺乏长时程协调能力，需要在两者之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建由凸集组成的通道诱导图，支持带优先级的规划与两级软协调：通道感知的路径选择考虑通道占用与通行方向，路径受限的连续时空规划对时间冲突施加惩罚；生成参考轨迹交由模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器在线避碰，并采用自适应时间与几何跟踪应对执行偏差，配合选择性重规划维持协调而不做刚性轨迹预留。

Result: 在4种环境、5–30个机器人共1200个实例中取得近乎完美的成功率，makespan与flowtime优于所评估基线；8机器人硬件实验全部无碰撞完成。

Conclusion: 以通道为中心的协调方式能有效兼顾长时程协作与灵活执行。

Abstract: In multi-robot navigation, rigid global schedules are sensitive to execution deviations, while local methods lack long-horizon coordination. We propose a passage-focused framework combining soft global coordination with flexible local execution. A passage-induced graph of convex sets supports priority-ordered planning with two levels of soft coordination. Passage-aware route selection accounts for passage usage and traversal directions, while route-restricted continuous space-time planning penalizes temporal conflicts. The resulting references guide a model predictive path integral controller that avoids collisions online. Adaptive temporal and geometric tracking accommodates execution deviations, while selective replanning maintains coordination without rigid trajectory reservations. Across 1,200 instances in 4 different environments with 5-30 robots, the framework achieves a near-perfect success rate and outperforms evaluated baselines in makespan and flowtime. Hardware experiments with 8 robots demonstrate successful completion of all trials without collisions. These results highlight the effectiveness of passage-focused coordination in balancing long-horizon cooperation with flexible execution.

</details>


### [4] [Correcting Persistent Failures in Point-Cloud Grasping Policies using Human Demonstrations in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18919)
*Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Karthik Rengarajan,Bharath Narasimhan,Chris von Csefalvay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出人在回路的失败挖掘与示范回路：RL点云抓取策略在未见物体上成功率由Contact-GraspNet的55%提升到83%，再通过58条VR人类示范将RL持续失败的困难抓取子集成功率从24%提升到45%，并对未示范的相似情形有泛化恢复效果，且不损害总体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭、仓储和农场等场景的机器人需要抓取从未见过、无3D模型也无类别标签的物体，因此希望策略只依赖深度传感器点云的几何信息。主流离线抓取生成器（如Contact-GraspNet）虽具备这种泛化性，但按学习的合理性排序，而非按"是否真正抓起物体"排序；RL可直接优化成功提升，但总会残留个别持续失败的抓取情形。

Method: 构建人在回路的失败闭环：自动挖掘RL策略反复失败的情境，将其送入虚拟现实应用，由人用手亲自完成抓取，再以两个抓取点（抓取位置）的形式回流训练。人类只提供"抓哪里"，机器人的接近轨迹仍由自身策略决定。仿真中与Contact-GraspNet基线及纯RL策略对比，并做未示范相似用例的迁移评估。

Result: 仿真中RL将未见物体成功率从55%（Contact-GraspNet）提升至83%；仅增加58条人类示范，使RL持续失败的困难用例成功率由24%提升至45%（近似翻倍），并恢复了纯RL在相似但从未示范的用例上损失的性能，同时未见物体上的成功率未下降。

Conclusion: 少量、低成本的人类抓取点示范可作为RL点云抓取策略的精准补丁：RL负责平均水平，人在回路负责补齐系统性失败模式，且这种补丁具有向相似情形的泛化能力，是一种可扩展的迭代改进范式。

Abstract: Robots in homes, warehouses and farms must pick up objects they have never seen, usually without a 3D model or a category label. A grasping policy that works directly on the point cloud from a depth sensor needs only the geometry the robot observes, so it applies to any new object. Offline grasp generators such as Contact-GraspNet work this way, but they rank grasps by learned plausibility rather than by whether the grasp actually lifts the object. Reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize for that directly and make a point-cloud grasping policy much better on average, yet it leaves specific grasps persistently unsolved. We present a human-in-the-loop failure loop that targets those grasps: situations an RL policy keeps failing are mined automatically, sent to a virtual-reality application where a person picks the object up with their own hands, and returned to training as two grasp points. The person contributes only where to grasp; the robot chooses its own approach. In simulation, RL raises success on unseen objects from 55% with Contact-GraspNet to 83%. Adding 58 human demonstrations nearly doubles success on the hard cases RL kept failing, from 24% to 45%, and recovers performance that RL alone loses on similar cases that were never demonstrated, without reducing success on unseen objects.

</details>


### [5] [Optimal Operating-Point Selection for Single-Linearization Trajectory Planning of Nonlinear Robot Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18935)
*Bhanuka Dayawansa,Rohan Munasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将单点线性化的线性化点作为优化变量，通过网格搜索与有界细化离线选取最优操作点，在UR5三自由度机械臂上使非线性轨迹目标下降7%–38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点线性化计算高效但规划性能高度依赖所选取的线性化点，而传统做法通常直接取初始点、中点或终点，缺乏系统性选择依据。

Method: 从统一的立方种子轨迹中采样候选操作点；对每个候选点构造对应仿射模型并求解同一个带约束的有限时域二次规划；将所得控制序列在原非线性动力学上执行，以非线性轨迹目标为评价指标，通过网格搜索加有界细化确定最优操作点。

Result: 在UR5三自由度机械臂的四个点到点运动任务中，最优归一化操作点位置落在0.325–0.935之间，体现任务依赖性；相比表现最好的传统初始/中点/终点线性化，非线性轨迹目标分别降低17–18%、21–22%、37–38%和7–8%；在带相同PID反馈修正的非线性执行中优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 系统化的操作点选择能在保持固定仿射规划模型的前提下显著提升单点线性化轨迹规划性能，是一种低成本的离线改进方案。

Abstract: Single-point linearization provides a computationally efficient basis for optimization-based trajectory planning of nonlinear robot manipulators. However, planning performance depends on the selected linearization point. This paper presents an offline performance-driven approach that treats the location of a single linearization as an optimization variable. Candidate operating points are sampled from a common cubic seed trajectory, and each corresponding affine model is used to solve an identical constrained finite-horizon quadratic program (QP). The resulting control sequences are executed on the original nonlinear dynamics, and the operating point minimizing the nonlinear trajectory objective is selected through grid search and bounded refinement. Evaluation on four point-to-point motions of a three-degree-of-freedom UR5 manipulator yields optimal normalized operating-point locations between 0.325 and 0.935, demonstrating trajectory-dependent selection. Compared with the best-performing conventional initial, midpoint, or terminal linearization, the proposed approach reduces the nonlinear trajectory objective by 17-18%, 21-22%, 37-38%, and 7-8% across the four tasks, respectively. Additional nonlinear execution with identical PID feedback correction confirms that the performance advantage persists for a representative task. These results demonstrate that systematic operating-point selection can substantially improve single-linearization trajectory planning while preserving a fixed affine planning model.

</details>


### [6] [TMA: Sim-to-Real Forceful Humanoid Manipulation via Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18976)
*Yiting Chen,Kelin Yu,Sarthak Kaingade,Bryan Trinh,Kaiyu Hang,Roberto Martin-martin,Nima Fazeli,X. Alice Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出触觉驱动的运动自适应方法TMA及全身系统，实现人形机器人零样本仿真到真实的力控多指操作，以圆形门把手开门为基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人移动操作多集中于几何主导的交互，而真实世界中需应对接触与交互动力学的不确定性，多指施力操作亟需触觉驱动的自适应能力。

Method: 以触觉可观测的交互为中心组织策略学习，用锥体几何作为多指接触表示的归纳偏置，基于触觉历史在线自适应运动行为；上身操作策略与预训练通用运动控制器联合训练，协调灵巧交互与全身稳定。

Result: 仅在仿真中训练即可零样本迁移至搭载多指手的Unitree G1人形机器人，在四个商用圆形门把手上显著优于标准RMA与反应式触觉策略基线。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的在线运动自适应结合全身协调，可实现零样本仿真到真实的力控人形操作，圆形门把手开启可作为有效基准任务。

Abstract: Recent humanoid loco-manipulation in articulated environments has largely focused on geometry-dominant interactions, where success is primarily determined by geometric alignment and motion execution. In contrast, forceful multi-finger manipulation requires the robot to adapt to real-world uncertainties in contact and interaction dynamics. We present Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation (TMA) and an integrated whole-body system for zero-shot sim-to-real forceful humanoid manipulation, and study round-doorknob opening as a challenging benchmark requiring stable multi-finger frictional grasping, sufficient torque generation without slip, force-regulated pushing, and whole-body stability. TMA structures policy learning around interactions observable through tactile feedback, uses cone geometry as an inductive bias for multi-finger contact representation, and adapts motor behavior online from tactile history. An upper-body manipulation policy is trained alongside a pretrained general locomotion controller to coordinate dexterous interaction and whole-body stabilization. Trained entirely in simulation, TMA transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 humanoid with BrainCo Revo2 multi-fingered hands and substantially outperforms standard Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) and reactive tactile policy baselines across four commercial round doorknobs in the real world.

</details>


### [7] [Robot Demonstration Videos Should Include Transparency Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18982)
*Nozomi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文呼吁机器人学与AI领域为演示视频建立“披露标准”，并通过六国大规模预注册实验证明：公众难以辨别视频的视觉真实性（真实拍摄 vs. CGI）与行为真实性（自主 vs. 遥操作），而清晰的披露干预能显著改善公众的辨别力与能力认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于实体与资金限制，公众难以直接接触具身AI技术，其对机器人进展的认知主要依赖经过剪辑与筛选的演示视频。若视频隐去拍摄方式与操控方式等关键信息，会系统性扭曲公众感知，使其高估机器人能力，因此需要研究公众的辨别力并设计干预措施。

Method: 开展跨六国、大规模（N=9,341）的预注册实验，测量公众对机器人演示视频视觉真实性（真实拍摄 vs. CGI）和行为真实性（自主运行 vs. 遥操作）的辨别能力；随后基于行为科学证据设计披露干预，检验在视频中加入清晰披露的效果。

Result: 公众在辨别两类真实性上普遍存在困难，信息缺失导致其高估机器人在真实环境中的任务执行与导航能力；加入清晰披露后，公众对视觉与行为真实性的辨别显著提升，对机器人能力的理解也更准确。

Conclusion: 建议机器人研究社区采纳演示视频披露标准，以校准公众预期、提升研究透明度，并让公众对具身AI进展形成更准确的认识。

Abstract: This paper argues that the robotics and AI community should adopt disclosure standards for demonstration videos. As physical and financial constraints limit direct public access to embodied AI, public dissemination of progress in robotics is mainly through curated demonstration videos rather than direct experience with the technology. In a large-scale (N=9,341), pre-registered experiment across six countries, we find that the general public struggles to discern both the visual authenticity (e.g., distinguishing real footage from CGI) and behavioral authenticity (e.g., distinguishing autonomy from teleoperation) of robot demonstration videos. The omission of these details distorts public perception, leading people to overestimate the ability of robots to perform tasks and navigate real world environments. Using evidence from behavioral science, we design a disclosure intervention and demonstrate that the inclusion of clear disclosures in robot demonstration videos significantly improves discernment of visual and behavioral authenticity, as well as understanding robots' capabilities. We propose the adoption of disclosure standards for robot demonstration videos to accurately calibrate public expectations and promote transparency in robotics research.

</details>


### [8] [Refinement vs. Replanning: Inference Compute Allocation in Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19034)
*Alba Maria Tellez Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在冻结的SmolVLA模型上，论文研究部署期如何联合分配"单次调用内的细化算力（去噪步数d）"与"重规划频率（执行步数h）"，提出同时报告标称算力率与完整尝试成本的评估流程，并在LIBERO仿真中给出案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的分块机器人策略可在部署期以两种耦合方式消耗推理算力：增加单次调用内的工作量以细化动作块，或提高调用频率以基于新观测重规划。已有工作缺乏在不更新权重的前提下，对二者联合分配进行规范评估的方法。

Method: 提出一套评估流程：以标称算力率和完整尝试成本双指标报告成功率；在发现任务上选择比较，并在留出任务上检验预设比较；仅在检查留出结果后新增的比较只作描述性总结。用一个冻结SmolVLA检查点在同步LIBERO仿真中演示，固定块长K=50，以去噪步数作为细化控制量，对比不同(d, h)组合。

Result: 算术量与时延对成本的刻画不同：d=10时82.1%的计数算术与去噪无关，而固定输入主机时间的描述性仿射拟合仅有17.7%归于截距。h=2、d=2时成功139/160，优于d=5的122/160（+10.6个百分点，任务条件95%区间+4.4至+16.9），且每控制步算术量少5.9%。d=2下h=5与h=2成功数为138/160对139/160，每次尝试算术量低60.7%，但未确立等价性。

Conclusion: 该案例研究说明部署期自适应须联合评估生成努力与反馈频率，算术成本与时延成本不可互换；结果仅为一例，不构成通用最优配置。

Abstract: A frozen chunked robot policy can spend deployment-time inference compute in two coupled ways: more work within each policy call to refine a predicted action chunk, or more frequent policy calls to replan from fresh observations. We study this allocation without updating model weights. Our evaluation procedure reports success with both nominal compute rate and complete attempt cost, selects comparisons on discovery tasks, and tests prespecified comparisons on held-out tasks. Comparisons added after inspecting the held-out results only summarize observed patterns. We demonstrate the procedure with one frozen SmolVLA checkpoint in synchronous LIBERO simulation, using denoising count as the refinement control and fixing chunk length at K=50. Arithmetic and latency characterize cost differently: at d=10, 82.1% of counted arithmetic is independent of denoising, whereas a descriptive affine fit to fixed-input host time assigns 17.7% to its intercept. At h=2, d=2 succeeds on 139/160 attempts versus 122/160 for d=5 (+10.6 percentage points; task-conditional 95% interval: +4.4 to +16.9), while using 5.9% less arithmetic per control step. Executing h=5 rather than h=2 at d=2 yields 138/160 versus 139/160 successes and 60.7% lower arithmetic per attempt; equivalence is not established. The case study shows why deployment adaptation must evaluate generation effort and feedback frequency jointly; it does not define a universal setting.

</details>


### [9] [Certify, Then Select: Viability-Aware Runtime Steering of Frozen Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19047)
*Angelo Moroncelli,Loris Roveda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CAST：在测试时从冻结扩散策略采样若干动作块，用障碍条件逐一认证，再由学习到的价值集成挑选排序最高的已认证动作块执行；并给出安全性、最优性间隙与闭环分解的理论保证。进一步提出 CAST-V/CAST-VL 通过策略条件化的可行性（viability）避免"现在安全、之后无解"的死胡同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略擅长从示教中学习机器人技能，但部署时会遇到示教中未包含的约束，且为每种约束重新训练不现实。现有安全过滤器直接投影/修改动作以满足约束，存在两个问题：修改忽略了任务目标；大幅修改可能把策略带到无法继续执行的区域（死胡同）。此外"当前动作安全"不足以保证后续仍安全——生成式控制器可能现在有安全动作，之后却完全没有。

Method: 1) CAST：从冻结策略采样少量动作块，对每块做障碍（barrier）条件认证，仅对认证通过的块用价值集成（value ensemble）排序，执行排名最高者。2) 理论：证明已认证步骤的安全性不依赖 critic 的好坏；证明只要存在认证样本，执行块与"以可行性为条件的策略"的分布距离有 log N nat 的界，而安全过滤器投影可能与之奇异（差距无界）；给出精确闭环恒等式，把排序带来的收益分解为可用性、分散度与 critic 目标三项。3) 安全性权限分析：刻画有限采样下冻结策略的安全控制权限。4) CAST-V：用模型在有限样本保证下估计策略条件化的 viability，从而保持安全控制权限；CAST-VL 则从控制器自身日志中学习 viability。

Result: 在 robomimic 操作任务、平面四旋翼、MuJoCo 人形与独轮车上，均使用冻结策略：CAST 在几乎所有设置下把"任务+约束"联合成功率提升到高于输出过滤器；当过滤器修改幅度大时，"先认证后排序"起决定性作用；viability 机制消除了大部分一步认证看不到的死胡同。

Conclusion: 测试时应把"认证"放在"排序"之前，并引入策略条件化的多步可行性，才能在保持冻结策略能力的前提下实现有保证的约束满足；CAST 系列在多种机器人与约束设置中优于安全过滤式基线。

Abstract: Diffusion policies, which generate short action sequences by denoising random noise, are a leading way to learn robot skills from demonstrations. Once deployed, they meet constraints the demonstrations never contained, and retraining for each is impractical. A safety filter edits the policy's action to satisfy them, but the edit ignores the task, and a large edit can leave the policy where it cannot continue. We propose CAST (Certified Action Selection at Test time): draw a few action chunks from the frozen policy, certify each with a barrier condition, and execute the certified chunk that a learned value ensemble ranks highest. We prove that a certified step is safe whatever the critic does; that, whenever a certified sample exists, the executed chunk stays within log N nats of the policy conditioned on feasibility, while a safety-filter projection can be singular to it; and that an exact closed-loop identity splits the gain of ranking into availability, spread and the critic's target. Certifying the next chunk is not enough: a generative controller can have safe actions available now and none later. We characterise the safe control authority of a frozen policy under finite sampling, and CAST-V preserves it through a policy-conditioned viability, estimated with a model under a finite-sample guarantee or learned from the controller's own logs (CAST-VL). On robomimic manipulation, a planar quadrotor, a MuJoCo humanoid and a unicycle, with frozen policies, CAST raises joint task-and-constraint success above the output filter in nearly every setting; certifying before ranking is decisive where a filter's edit is large, and viability removes most dead ends that one-step certification cannot see. Project page: https://clmoro.github.io/cast.

</details>


### [10] [RoboCoEvo: Expanding Robot Capabilities through Agent-Policy Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19081)
*Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Dawei Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Xuan Zhao,Yintao Ma,Yangzheng Wu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboCoEvo，一个由失败引导的协同进化框架，将 MLLM 智能体可执行知识的迭代精炼与机器人基础模型（如 VLA）的参数学习相耦合，使二者相互促进、共同提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将 MLLM 智能体与机器人基础模型集成时，两者能力的提升基本相互解耦：智能体组件的进步未必惠及模型组件，反之亦然，从而严重限制了整体系统能力。作者认为需要"协同进化"，即智能体与机器人基础模型之间形成相互强化的迭代过程。

Method: 采用"代码即策略"（code-as-policy）形式，让智能体开发并精炼可执行的失败检测与恢复程序，产生针对性的纠正经验；再通过后训练把这些纠正行为固化进机器人模型的端到端策略中；更新后策略的失败分布变化又反过来指导智能体程序的下一轮修订。

Result: 在 BEHAVIOR-1K、RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 三个基准上的实验表明，随着协同进化轮次推进，自主 VLA 任务性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 失败引导的协同进化机制能够有效打通智能体与机器人基础模型之间的改进闭环，实现可持续的自主任务性能增益。

Abstract: Integrating MLLM-based agents with robotic foundation models has attracted increasing attention as a means of enhancing robotic capabilities. However, the improvement of such integrated systems remains largely decoupled across their agent and model components, with advances in one component not necessarily benefiting the other, substantially limiting the capabilities of the combined system. As both MLLMs and robotic foundation models become increasingly capable, fully realizing their combined potential calls for co-evolution, an iterative process in which improvements in agents and robotic foundation models reinforce one another. We propose RoboCoEvo, a failure-guided co-evolution framework that couples the refinement of the agent's explicit and reusable executable knowledge with parameter learning in robotic foundation models, such as vision-language-action (VLA) models. Through a code-as-policy formulation, the agent develops and refines executable failure-detection and recovery programs, generating targeted corrective experience. Post-training consolidates the resulting corrective behaviors into the robotic model's end-to-end policy, improving autonomous task execution. Changes in the updated policy's failure distribution, in turn, guide subsequent revisions to the agent's programs. Experiments on BEHAVIOR-1K, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin demonstrate sustained improvements in autonomous VLA task performance across successive co-evolution rounds.

</details>


### [11] [Leaning Over to Reach Far-Away Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19100)
*Allen Devaraj,William Xie,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用采样式全身MPC，仅靠局部感知与基准标记，实现人形机器人"撑扶环境"的支撑式抓取，将可达范围外扩约50 cm，34 Hz笔记本实时运行并成功sim-to-real。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规全身人形控制倾向于避免或被动适应接触，但当目标物放在远超正常臂展的表面上时，必须主动利用与环境的接触来稳定身体；作者希望以简单模型和局部感知实现这一能力。

Method: 在Unitree H1-2上采用采样式（如CEM）全身模型预测控制，统一处理倾身、撑扶、伸手与恢复；仅使用机载局部感知和fiducial标记做位姿估计，控制器以34 Hz在笔记本上实时运行并完成sim-to-real迁移；共54次试验，涵盖6个目标与3种桌面高度，并分析采样预算为何足够小。

Result: 可达范围较无支撑站立 workspace 外扩约50 cm；负载姿态可维持4分钟以上（之后位姿估计漂移）；89%的试验成功触及指令目标，81%完成完整"伸手—恢复"循环；实验表明采样动作的时间持续性与探索尺度是成功的关键因素，CEM在规划频率与模型失配下具有最宽的工作区间。

Conclusion: 该工作为在线、富接触的人形操作建立了真实世界基线，说明简单的采样式全身MPC配合局部感知即可实现可靠的撑扶式抓取与恢复，并为采样预算的充分性提供了实证解释。

Abstract: We study supported reaching to extend a humanoid robot's workspace by bracing against the environment for stabilization using a straightforward yet capable whole-body model predictive control (MPC) approach, exclusively with local sensing and fiducial markers. While typical solutions for whole body humanoid control aim at avoiding or accommodating contact, contact is explicitly required to reach objects placed on surfaces far out of normal reach. We employ sampling-based MPC for whole-body leaning, bracing, reaching, and recovery on a Unitree H1-2 humanoid. The controller runs in real-time at 34 Hz on a laptop, transfers sim-to-real, extends reach by about 50 cm beyond the unbraced standing workspace, and sustains the loaded posture for over four minutes, before pose estimation drifts. The controller reaches the commanded target in 89% of trials and completes the full reach-and-recovery cycle in 81%, across 54 trials spanning six targets and three table heights. We further characterize why such a small real-time sampling budget is sufficient, finding that temporal persistence of sampled actions and exploration scale strongly govern success, while CEM provides the widest operating region over planning rate and model mismatch. These results establish a real-world baseline for online, contact-rich humanoid manipulation. Code, videos, and full hardware details are available at https://h12leaner.github.io/

</details>


### [12] [Wireless Edge-Assisted Adaptive Latent Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models in Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19131)
*Fatih Temiz,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无线边缘辅助的自适应隐式推理框架WEALR，用拉格朗日约束深度强化学习（TRPO-Lag）联合决定VLA模型隐式推理的执行位置与收敛阈值，在任务成功率、时延和能耗间取得平衡，可行性加权任务成功率最高提升23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉观测与语言指令映射为机器人动作，但其计算需求对资源受限的端侧实时控制构成挑战。隐式推理（latent reasoning）的测试时扩展可在不生成显式思维链token的情况下精化隐表示，但带来任务成功、推理时延与能耗之间的权衡；因此需要在高通信不确定性下进行高效的协同推理。

Method: 构建无线边缘辅助的自适应隐式推理框架（WEALR），由决策智能体联合选择执行位置（端侧/边缘）与隐式推理的收敛阈值；将该联合自适应问题建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），并采用拉格朗日约束深度强化学习TRPO-Lag求解，以在变化的无线信道条件与任务需求下平衡任务性能与资源消耗。实验基于LIBERO操作任务、实测画像化的VLA推理开销以及射线追踪无线信道进行评测。

Result: 实验结果表明，TRPO-Lag相比基准CDRL方法，可行性加权任务成功率最高提升23%。

Conclusion: WEALR框架验证了在真实无线通信条件下对VLA隐式推理进行执行位置与计算预算联合自适应的有效性，能够在保证实时性约束的同时提升任务完成质量，为物理AI中的高效协同推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable embodied artificial intelligence (AI) by translating visual observations and language instructions into robotic actions. Emerging use cases in physical AI are expected to drive the need for efficient inference and communication, as the computational demands of the VLA models pose challenges for real-time control with limited onboard resources. Test-time scaling through latent reasoning allows these models to refine hidden representations without generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens, but introduces a trade-off between task success, inference latency, and energy consumption. In this paper, we address the efficient collaborative inference under a realistic communication scenario. We propose a wireless edge-assisted framework for adaptive latent reasoning (WEALR) that augments onboard computation with edge computing. A decision-making agent jointly selects the execution location and latent-reasoning convergence threshold. We formulate this joint adaptation as a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). A Lagrangian-constrained deep reinforcement learning (CDRL) approach, namely trust region policy optimization Lagrangian (TRPO-Lag), learns to balance task performance and resource consumption under varying wireless conditions and task requirements. We evaluate the framework using LIBERO manipulation tasks, profiled VLA inference costs, and ray-traced wireless channels. Experimental results demonstrate that TRPO-Lag achieves up to 23% higher feasibility-weighted task success rate compared to benchmark CDRL methods.

</details>


### [13] [SuperGlance: Driving at a Glance with a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19138)
*Jieyuan Pei,Xin Yan,Mingwei Xu,Haiyi Zhang,Siyu Zou,Zhangyi Hu,Zhuoxuan Li,Yuguang Yang,Rongjunchen Zhang,Cheng Chi,Hui Xiong,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperGlance 利用 VLM 首个 token 之前的隐藏状态，将 decoder-only VLM 转为 encoder 式驾驶策略，在 84 ms 内完成规划（比 Qwen-Drive-1.0 快约六倍），并保持视觉问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 逐步解码规划，每步一次前向，导致规划耗时超过一秒，难以支撑具身智能所需的快速 System 1。常见做法是把 System 1 放在 VLM 之外，但这样的规划器丧失了 VLM 的感知与语言能力。作者注意到 VLM 在写出第一个 token 之前，已把场景读入一个隐藏状态，因此希望直接利用这一隐藏状态来驾驶与问答。

Method: 冻结候选轨迹集合（5,273 条），用线性头对隐藏状态打分选轨迹；因候选集固定，奖励可离线计算，从而支持奖励加权的监督目标，并用强化学习最大化精确期望奖励。策略训练前后穿插多选题训练，以保持 VLM 的视觉能力（题目需在两种选项顺序下都答对以规避捷径）；同时用 KL 正则约束各阶段模型不偏离初始模型。

Result: 视觉多选题表现与基座 VLM 解码相当；规划仅需 84 ms GPU 时间，比 Qwen-Drive-1.0 快六倍，在 NAVSIM、PAI-AV、WOD-E2E 上至少同等水平。AlpaSim 闭环中，两规划器在 300 ms 延迟内每责任事故公里数保持稳定，之后下降；把 GPU 时间计为延迟时，领先优势由 2.8 倍扩大到 3.9 倍。延迟 1.2 s 时，本文模型低于 Qwen-Drive-1.0 在其自身 518 ms 时的表现。

Conclusion: 单次前向即可同时完成规划与视觉问答，为 VLA 走向 System 1 提供了更可行的路径；但性能对延迟敏感，超过一定阈值后优势消失。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is turning to vision-language-action models (VLAs). They answer and plan by decoding, one step at a time. Each step is another forward pass, so a plan can take over a second of GPU time. Embodied AI often pairs such a slow, reasoning System 2 with a fast System 1 that acts. Human drivers plan at a glance, with System 1. Faster designs usually put System 1 outside the vision-language model (VLM), in a planner without its abilities. Yet before a VLM writes its first token, it has already read the scene into one hidden state. We show that, once trained, this hidden state is enough to drive and to answer questions about what the model sees. SuperGlance reads it to turn a decoder-only VLM into an encoder-style driving policy. Its linear head scores a fixed set of 5,273 candidate trajectories. With the set fixed, rewards are computed offline, so supervised targets can be reward-weighted and reinforcement learning maximizes the exact expected reward. Before and during policy training, multiple-choice training keeps the VLM's visual abilities in one pass, so the policy still answers questions about driving scenes. Training questions are built against shortcuts. Each must be answered right in two option orders. Against drift, a KL term keeps each stage near its starting model. One pass offers VLAs a more plausible path to System 1. SuperGlance answers visual multiple-choice questions as well as its base VLM does by decoding. It plans in 84 ms of GPU time, six times faster than Qwen-Drive-1.0, and at least as well on NAVSIM, PAI-AV and WOD-E2E. In AlpaSim closed loop, km per at-fault incident holds for both planners up to a 300-ms delay and drops beyond. With GPU time charged as delay, SuperGlance's lead over Qwen-Drive-1.0 grows from 2.8 to 3.9 times. Delayed by 1.2 s, our model falls below Qwen-Drive-1.0 at its own 518 ms.

</details>


### [14] [ImpedanceMimic: Learned Impedance as Action Space for Force-Accurate Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19153)
*Matthias Jammot,Pembe Gizem Özdil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ImpedanceMimic——从运动与力测量中学习逐关节刚度、阻尼与位置设定点的强化学习策略，仅推理时需运动数据，在129名被试6项活动中于运动学与力保真度上超越固定增益PD、直接力矩、肌肉驱动及现有模仿控制器，并可零样本迁移到未见人体与人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从运动反推力是病态问题：不同控制器可复现同一动作却产生不同接触力。基于物理的运动模仿虽通过仿真消歧，但依赖固定增益PD控制。固定的刚度与阻尼增益在接触与摆动阶段面临冲突需求，从而在运动跟踪与力跟踪之间形成权衡。

Method: ImpedanceMimic为强化学习策略，根据运动与力的测量调节逐关节的刚度、阻尼和位置设定点，但推理阶段仅需运动输入。将增益表示为惯性缩放先验的倍数，使单一策略可控制被试特异的生物力学模型而无需显式人体测量输入。

Result: 在129名被试、6项活动中，ImpedanceMimic在运动学与力保真度上优于各积分方案下的匹配固定增益PD、直接力矩控制、肌肉驱动控制及现有模仿控制器，并接近专用的运动到力回归器；零样本迁移到未见人体与人形机器人成功。学得的阻抗构成跨身体共享的低维运动状态表示，可泛化到未参与训练的活动：刚度随载荷增大，阻尼预示卸载。

Conclusion: 可变阻抗的可学习控制优于固定增益PD模仿，能统一运动与力跟踪，并揭示一种跨身体、跨任务的低维运动状态（阻抗）表示，兼具生物力学建模与机器人迁移价值。

Abstract: Recovering the forces behind a motion is ill-posed: different controllers can reproduce the same movement while generating different contact forces. Physics-based motion imitation resolves this ambiguity through simulation, but relies on fixed-gain proportional-derivative (PD) control. We show that fixed gains impose a trade-off between motion and force tracking, as contact and swing place conflicting demands on joint stiffness and damping. We introduce ImpedanceMimic, a reinforcement-learning policy that modulates per-joint stiffness, damping, and position setpoints from motion and force measurements, but requires only motion at inference. Across 129 participants and six activities, ImpedanceMimic outperforms matched fixed-gain PD under every integration scheme, direct-torque, muscle-actuated and existing imitation controllers in kinematic and force fidelity, approaching a dedicated motion-to-force regressor. By expressing gains as multiples of inertia-scaled priors, a single policy controls subject-specific biomechanical models without explicit anthropometric input and transfers zero-shot to unseen humans and a humanoid robot. The learned impedance forms a low-dimensional representation of motor state, shared across bodies, that generalizes to withheld activities: stiffness scales with load, and damping anticipates unloading.

</details>


### [15] [Distilling Safety Filters for Filter-free Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19190)
*Srikar Babu Gadipudi,Somayeh Sojoudi,Claire J. Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将CBF安全过滤器蒸馏进RL策略的方法，使机器人团队在无过滤器部署时实现零碰撞，并在多智能体与真实四旋翼上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人共享空间导航中，强化学习依赖试错会产生真实机器人无法承受的碰撞；CBF安全过滤器虽可纠正不安全动作，但在团队场景中退化为约束数量随团队规模二次增长的集中式优化，难以扩展。

Method: 把过滤器安全性内化到策略中：用隐式Q学习仅从已执行的安全动作中学习（避免过滤器掩盖不安全动作代价、导致actor-critic高估）；在合成的近碰撞状态上把过滤器蒸馏进策略，并从理论上证明蒸馏损失可界定策略的约束违反程度，进而限制跨越安全边界的幅度，使误差不累积；当蒸馏状态覆盖部署状态时，损失还可界定每一步的碰撞概率。

Result: 无过滤器、仅用局部观测时，多智能体1,800个测试回合零碰撞；三智能体训练的策略以恒定单智能体计算量零样本迁移到二十智能体团队，并在最多八智能体时保持无碰撞；在Crazyflie四旋翼上最多五架同时飞行完成验证。

Conclusion: 将安全过滤器蒸馏进策略可在去除集中式CBF过滤器的同时保留安全性，实现可扩展、跨团队规模泛化且可上真机的多机器人无碰撞导航。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can teach robot teams to navigate shared spaces, but trial and error causes collisions that real robots cannot afford. Control barrier function (CBF) safety filters prevent these collisions by correcting unsafe actions, yet in a team the filter becomes a centralized optimization whose number of constraints grows quadratically with the team size. We propose an approach that builds the filter's safety into the RL policy and examine how such a system behaves during filter-free deployment. However, the filter that keeps training safe also hides what the policy must learn: it masks the cost of unsafe actions, which actor-critic methods then overvalue, and it keeps near-collision states out of the training data. Our approach therefore learns only from executed safe actions with implicit Q-learning and distills the filter into the policy at synthetic near-collision states. We prove that filter distillation loss bounds the policy's constraint violation and hence how far agents can cross their safety margin, so errors never accumulate. When distillation states cover deployed states, the loss also bounds the collision probability at every step. Without a filter and acting only on local observations, our policies have no collisions in 1,800 test episodes with multiple agents. A policy trained with three agents transfers zero-shot to teams of twenty at constant per-agent computation and stays collision-free with up to eight agents. We test our policies on Crazyflie quadrotors with up to five flying at the same time.

</details>


### [16] [Ego2Sim2Humanoid: Agentic Real-to-Sim for Learning Loco-Manipulation VLAs without Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19196)
*Chen Liu,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 \method{} 框架，将第一视角（egocentric）眼镜录制的人类操作视频自动转换为经物理验证的机器人训练轨迹，从而在不使用真实机器人遥操作演示的前提下训练出可自主执行移动操作任务的人形机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略的模仿学习通常依赖遥操作机器人演示，每条轨迹都需要受训操作员实时操控实体机器人并佩戴体感追踪硬件，成本高昂。第一视角眼镜虽可直接采集人类演示且无需机器人或手持夹爪，但只能追踪头部与视野内的人手，且人类与机器人在身高、臂展、手部形态上的差异导致直接复制人类动作无法产生可行接触。

Method: 方法包含四个核心环节：1）Agentic 实到虚（real-to-sim）阶段：从 RGB-D 与头部流重建碰撞体与带纹理视觉资产，必要时补全未观测表面，并预测被操作物体上的目标接触带；2）运动适配：抓取规划器将接触带转换为可行的机器人腕部位姿与手指闭合量，双层站立姿态优化规划出保持手部目标可达的无碰撞站姿，全身运动生成器合成下肢移动与视野外手臂运动；3）两阶段运动修正：在可见性切换处做运动学混合，并在物理仿真中依据智能体设计的语义奖励训练强化学习跟踪策略，使物体离开相机视野后仍能完成操作阶段；4）沿优化轨迹通过机器人机载相机渲染重建场景，得到同步的机器人视角图像、本体感觉与经验证的动作标签。

Result: 仅在该转换生成的数据集上微调、不使用任何真实机器人演示，GR00T N1.7 在 Unitree G1 上自主完成了移动操作（loco-manipulation）任务。

Conclusion: 该工作表明第一视角人类演示可经"实到虚"重构与物理验证转换为高质量的机器人训练数据，替代昂贵的遥操作采集流程，并成功迁移至真实人形机器人执行全身移动操作，为可扩展的机器人模仿学习数据来源提供了新路径。

Abstract: Imitation learning for visuomotor policies typically relies on teleoperated robot demonstrations, which are costly because every episode requires a trained operator controlling a physical robot in real time through teleoperation interfaces and wearable body-tracking hardware. Egocentric glasses capture human demonstrations without a robot or handheld grippers, yet they track only the head and in-view human hands, and directly copying human motion to a robot with different stature, arm reach and hand morphology fails to produce feasible contacts. \method{} converts egocentric glasses recordings into physically verified robot training trajectories. An agentic real-to-sim stage reconstructs collision and textured visual assets from the recorded RGB-D and head streams, completes unseen surfaces when needed, and predicts target contact bands on the manipulated objects. During motion adaptation, a grasp planner turns each contact band into a feasible robot wrist pose and finger closure, bilevel stance optimization plans collision-free standing postures that keep hand targets reachable, and a whole-body motion generator synthesizes lower-body locomotion and out-of-view arm motion. Two-stage motion correction then blends visibility transitions kinematically and trains a reinforcement-learning tracking policy in physics simulation under agent-designed semantic rewards, completing manipulation phases even when the object leaves the camera view. Rendering the reconstructed scene through the robot's onboard camera along the optimized rollout yields synchronized robot-view images, proprioception and verified action labels. Fine-tuned exclusively on this converted dataset, without real-robot demonstrations, GR00T N1.7 performed loco-manipulation tasks autonomously on a Unitree G1. Project page: https://robonana.github.io/ego2sim2humanoid/

</details>


### [17] [Self-Calibrating Vision-Agent-Action: Deploying Pick-and-Place to New Robot Cells without Demonstrations or Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19198)
*Jae Hun Hwang,Yechan Seo,Hong-In Won*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Vision-Agent-Action (VAA)：无需机器人演示或微调，靠预训练视觉语言模型+自标定几何，在夹爪/基座坐标系变化的机器人单元中完成抓取放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的视觉-语言-动作模型 (VLA) 在机器人硬件、相机几何、外观、基座坐标系改变后性能大幅下降，而重新采集演示并微调代价高；作者希望无需演示与微调即能适应这些变化。

Method: 用预训练视觉-语言模型与语言模型做指令解析；通过自身运动与指尖测量命令映射与相机几何；估计镜头畸变；基于实测物体深度规划抓取。并在 8 个公开 LIBERO 微调 VLA 上做受控实验，改变硬件、相机几何、外观与基座坐标系（分数析因设计），并做配对消融。

Result: 夹爪与坐标系变化是 VLA 性能损失的主要来源。18 个含此类变化的单元中：冻结 VAA 230/360 (64%)，精化 VAA 268/360 (74%)，最强公开 VLA 仅 76/360 (21%)；配对消融支持自标定组合、复合变化下的镜头估计与实测深度流程。两步 LIBERO-Goal 链任务中 VAA 完成 54/60，四个 VLA 全部失败。

Conclusion: VAA 表明，结合预训练视觉语言模型与自标定测量，可在不训练、不微调的情况下显著提升跨硬件与坐标系变化的操作鲁棒性，优于现有微调 VLA。

Abstract: In simulation, Vision-Agent-Action (VAA) performs pick-and-place in robot cells with gripper or base-frame changes without robot demonstrations or fine-tuning. It uses pre-trained vision-language and language models for interpretation, measures the command map and camera geometry from its own motion and fingertips, estimates lens distortion, and plans grasps from measured object depth. A controlled study of eight public LIBERO-fine-tuned vision-language-action models (VLAs) varies robot hardware, camera geometry, appearance, and base frame. Gripper and frame changes dominate their losses in the tested fractional design. Across 18 cells with these changes, frozen VAA achieves 230/360 successes (64%), the refined VAA 268/360 (74%), and the strongest released VLA on this subset 76/360 (21%). Paired ablations support the self-calibration bundle, lens estimation under compounded changes, and the measured-depth pipeline. On a two-step LIBERO-Goal chain with object state carried between instructions, VAA completes 54/60 chains in changed cells, while four evaluated VLAs complete none.

</details>


### [18] [Continuous Lower-Limb Joint-Angle Estimation from sEMG and IMU: The Conditional Value of Cross-Activity and Cross-Subject Data under Update-Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19238)
*Jiaying Xu,Guangyuan Chen,Buyun Gao,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将"借用数据"拆分为信息价值与优化更新量两个效应，通过对等更新重放控制，系统量化了自身其他活动数据与他人同活动数据对外骨骼关节角度估计器的真实价值，发现数据价值具有层级性、状态依赖性和方向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图驱动外骨骼控制需要连续因果的用户关节运动学估计，而新用户训练数据来源选择缺乏原则。以往研究把"借用他人或自身其他活动记录"简化为"多数据vs少数据"，混淆了数据源所携带的信息量与优化器更新次数带来的效应，并用平均值掩盖了负迁移现象。

Method: 基于公开数据集全部十名受试者、六种运动活动的高密度sEMG与IMU，估计髋、膝、踝角度。冻结一个紧凑的残差因果TCN-GRU解码器，采用"更新匹配重放对照"：每个新增数据源的增益都与用户自身数据上相同更新次数的效果对标，从而分离信息贡献与更新贡献；并进行留一参与者门控实验。

Result: 用户自身其他活动使R²从0.722提升至0.788，其中+0.048来自重放之外的信息；下楼是可靠的有害来源，坐-站-走是可靠的有益来源。他人同活动数据在用户仅有目标活动时增益为G_T=+0.053，拥有自身其他活动数据后减半为G_W=+0.028；完全校准后，一两段目标记录即可使供体价值崩溃。两种来源的逐目标增益相关；留一参与者门控仅在低校准条件下能恢复大部分跨被试价值。

Conclusion: 数据价值是层级化、状态依赖且有方向性的；真正的瓶颈在于无测试标签条件下决定何时迁移。

Abstract: Intent-driven exoskeleton control needs a continuous, causal estimate of the wearer's joint kinematics and an answer to a second question: whose data should train the estimator for a new user? Borrowing the user's recordings of other activities, or other users' recordings of the same activity, is usually evaluated as "more data" versus "less data," which confounds the information a source carries with the optimizer updates it adds and hides negative transfer behind averages. We separate these effects for hip, knee, and ankle angle estimation from high-density sEMG and an IMU across six locomotion activities on all ten participants of a public dataset. A compact residual causal TCN-GRU decoder is frozen, and an update-matched replay control prices every added source against the same number of updates on the user's own data. A user's own other activities raise $R^2$ from 0.722 to 0.788, of which +0.048 is information beyond replay; stair descent is a reliably harmful donor and sit-stand-walk a reliably beneficial one. Other users' same-activity data help while the user has only the target activity ($G_\mathrm{T} = +0.053$) and half as much once own other-activity data exist ($G_\mathrm{W} = +0.028$); at full calibration, donor value collapses after one to two target records. Per-target gains from the two sources correlate, and leave-one-participant-out gates recover most of the cross-subject value only at low calibration. Data value is hierarchical, state-dependent, and directional; the bottleneck is deciding, without test labels, when to transfer.

</details>


### [19] [Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning for Nonstationary Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19251)
*Jungjin Lee,Se Jin Kim,Sung Kwon On,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PICR 将“从近期交互中推断的上下文”与“固定的物理信息网络”结合，仅更新上下文即可让机器人在不重新训练、无人工建模的条件下适应变化的物理条件；在 Isaac Sim 的 Franka Panda 上对未知负载的预测误差降低 1.6–3.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测动作后果需要内化系统行为，而条件很少保持固定，因此必须在少数几次交互内完成适应。快照中看起来相同的系统可能因快照隐藏的物理条件而对同一动作产生不同响应。现有上下文类模型只把近期转移汇总为潜在上下文，却未说明上下文应如何改变运动方程；物理信息神经网络（PINN）虽将方程写入模型，但只描述单一系统，无法识别系统已发生变化。

Method: 提出 Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning（PICR），桥接上述两类方法：仅更新从近期交互中重新推断的上下文，该上下文用于调制一个固定的物理信息网络，由该网络确定机器人的惯量与势能，从而在无重新训练、无手工指定机器人模型的条件下从自身经验中适应新的物理条件。在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中的 Franka Panda 机械臂上评估，涵盖变化负载与未见负载，并扩展到替代力矩下的反事实查询。

Result: 在 Isaac Sim 的 Franka Panda 上，PICR 在变化与未见负载下预测运动，对未见负载的误差比基线降低 1.6–3.8 倍，并能扩展到替代力矩下的反事实查询。分析表明：仅从交互推断的上下文编码了负载的物理属性、跟踪其变化并支配机械臂响应；上下文诱导的重力项与惯性项各自在单个拉格朗日分量的层面与模拟器一致，显示其物理有效性。

Conclusion: 通过只更新上下文、保持物理信息网络固定，PICR 实现了少样本的物理条件自适应，并具备可解释的物理意义（上下文可对应负载属性并驱动正确的重力/惯性项），为跨系统变化与反事实推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reasoning about what an action will cause requires internalizing how a system behaves. As conditions seldom stay fixed, this must often happen within a few interactions. Yet systems that look identical in a snapshot can respond differently to the same action, depending on physical conditions the snapshot hides. Context-based models capture this separation by summarizing recent transitions into a latent context, but do not specify how that context should change the equations of motion. Physics-informed neural networks build those equations into the model, but describe only a single system and cannot recognize when it has changed. We present Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning (PICR), which bridges the two. Only a context, re-inferred from recent interactions, is updated; it conditions a fixed physics-informed network that determines the robot's inertia and potential energy, so the robot adapts to new physical conditions from its own experience without retraining or a hand-specified robot model. On a Franka Panda arm in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, PICR predicts motion under changing and unseen payloads, reducing error on the latter by 1.6-3.8x over baselines, and extends to counterfactual queries under alternative torques. Our analysis shows that the context, inferred solely from interaction, encodes payload physical properties, tracks their changes, and governs the arm's response. The gravity and inertia terms induced by the context further demonstrate its physical validity, each agreeing with the simulator at the level of individual Lagrangian components.

</details>


### [20] [HospitalBench: Diagnosing Where and Why Embodied Agents Fail on Long Hospital Task Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19274)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Vinija Jain,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HospitalBench 是面向长时程具身任务的诊断性基准：在 Isaac Sim 中构建十项医院杂务，每项由 2 步不计分预热和 5 个计分子任务组成因果链，用视觉语言智能体通过技能级工具操作，并从轨迹中读取链条断裂位置、失败调用后的行为与位姿估计漂移。本文仅报告首批结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务失败的方式是终任务成功率无法反映的：子任务之间存在因果依赖，前面步骤留下的状态决定后续步骤该做什么。现有基准把子任务并列摆放，掩盖了链条断裂的具体位置与失败后的恢复行为，因此需要一个能诊断"在哪里断、断后怎么办、自身状态估计漂移多少"的基准。

Method: 在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中构建十项医院杂务，每项为单一连续仿真：2 步不计分预热后接 5 个计分子任务，子任务间状态耦合（物体位姿、一次性的选择或仅见过一次的事实）。视觉语言智能体通过技能级工具从相机图像行动。用仅能调用模型可用接口的脚本机器人作为可解性验证。评估时从每条轨迹提取三项诊断：链条断裂点、失败调用后的下一步行为、智能体自身位姿估计相对真值的漂移。文中仅给出首批结果（60 次计划运行中的 15 次：三个模型高投入设置，在 T1、T10 两个起始状态各一次及 T3 一次；脚本机器人在十项杂务中的三项上完成验证）。

Result: 15 次运行中仅 2 次通过全部 5 个子任务；跑完子任务 1 至 5 的运行数依次为 15、8、4、2、2，衰减近似逐级减半。189 次失败调用中，168 次后接不同调用，111 次后接的调用没有失败，说明智能体具备一定试错调整能力，但多数仍无法累积成链。作者明确声明这是首批结果，并非完整基准。

Conclusion: 终任务成功率掩盖了长时程任务的真实失败结构，按子任务链逐级诊断能定位断裂点并刻画失败后的恢复行为。初步结果显示失败随链条加深快速累积，而智能体的错误后调整虽常见却不足以保证链条延续。场景、任务链、脚本机器人、评估框架和轨迹将全部开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks fail in ways that end-task success cannot see. HospitalBench is a diagnostic benchmark of ten hospital chores in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, each two unscored warm-up steps followed by a causal chain of five scored sub-tasks in one continuous simulation: the state one step leaves (an object pose, a choice, a fact seen once) determines what the next step must do, unlike benchmarks that place sub-tasks side by side. Vision-language agents act through skill-level tools from camera images. A scripted robot that makes only the calls a model gets is the solvability check of a chore. From each trajectory the benchmark reads where a chain breaks, what the agent did after a failed call, and how far its own position estimate drifts from the true one. This version reports first results only: 15 of 60 planned runs (three models at high effort, one run each on chores T1 and T10 from both start states and T3 from one), with scripted-robot proofs on three of the ten chores. Of the 15 runs, 2 passed all five sub-tasks. Runs still going after sub-tasks 1 to 5 were 15, 8, 4, 2, 2. Of 189 failed calls, 168 were followed by a different call and 111 by a call that did not fail. These are first results and not a full benchmark. We will release the scenes, task chains, scripted robot, evaluation harness and trajectories.

</details>


### [21] [VLN-Flash: Parallel Action Decoding with Adaptive Execution for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19287)
*Ruizhe Xie,Honghua Chen,Binglin Wang,Tong Guo,Xu Tang,Puti Yan,Shaoxiong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-Flash 是一种免训练解码器，单次前向并行评估完整动作前缀树，并以分数间隔自适应决定执行步数，实现约 3 倍解码加速并提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航策略自回归生成动作时，机器人必须等待一串相互依赖的预测，推理延迟高、逐动作等待明显，影响实际部署中的响应速度。

Method: 利用小规模离散动作词表可并行打分所有短时程规划；通过分支专属注意力与对齐的 token 位置，使单次前向即可精确复现原策略对每个前缀的条件动作分布；在每个深度上计算逐步贪心前缀与其最强备选之间的分数间隔，据此决定执行多少个动作，并在所选深度执行联合分数最高的规划后再重新观测。

Result: 在匹配输入下，Uni-NaVid 与 StreamVLN 上动作解码约提速 3 倍；相比原生自回归解码器，在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上成功率与 SPL 均有提升，成功率最高提升 2.7 个百分点；在 Unitree GO2 实机上提升导航成功率，扣除网络传输后相邻动作平均等待减少约 160 ms。

Conclusion: 将依赖式逐动作解码替换为单次前向的树状并行解码并配合自适应执行深度，可在保持策略分布一致性的同时兼顾效率与导航表现，且无需额外训练即可用于现有 VLN 模型与真实机器人部署。

Abstract: When a vision-and-language navigation policy generates an action sequence autoregressively, the robot waits for a chain of dependent predictions. A small discrete action vocabulary makes it possible to score all short-horizon plans in parallel. We introduce VLN-Flash, a training-free decoder that evaluates the complete action-prefix tree in a single forward pass. Branch-specific attention and matched token positions preserve the original policy's conditional action distribution for each prefix in exact arithmetic. We use the score margin between the stepwise-greedy prefix and its strongest alternative at each depth to decide how many actions to execute. The robot then executes the plan with the highest joint score at the chosen depth before observing again. On matched inputs, VLN-Flash achieves a nearly $3\times$ speedup in action decoding over native autoregression on Uni-NaVid and StreamVLN. Compared with the native autoregressive decoders, it improves success rate and success weighted by path length (SPL) on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, with success gains up to 2.7 percentage points. On a Unitree GO2, VLN-Flash improves navigation success and cuts the mean wait between actions by about 160 ms after subtracting network transmission time.

</details>


### [22] [Residual Reinforcement Learning for Visual Servo Control with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19318)
*Mahdi Nobar,Alisa Rupenyan,John Lygeros,Jürg Keller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用SAC在数字孪生中学习残差策略，增强环路逆雅可比PI控制器，并结合延迟补偿卡尔曼滤波，实现不确定/噪声条件下传送带上移动目标的精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生不精确、测量误差与噪声导致基于模型的控制器难以在传送带目标跟踪中达到高精度，需要量化模型失配带来的性能下限并突破该下限。

Method: 提出在环路逆雅可比PI控制器上叠加由Soft Actor-Critic在数字孪生中学习的残差策略；用带延迟补偿的卡尔曼滤波融合乱序相机测量以获得低延迟目标位置估计；并通过频域灵敏度分析推导该类基于模型控制器的性能下界。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明残差策略使跟踪误差低于理论下界；在27.9–41.8 mm/s传送带速度下，平均跟踪误差由基线1.1–1.4 mm降至0.3–0.7 mm。

Conclusion: 在有限保真度仿真器中学习残差修正并结合延迟补偿，可显著提升模型不确定性下的基于模型控制性能，同时保持可靠的sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: We address tracking a moving object on a conveyor with a Franka Emika Panda robot arm, despite an imprecise digital twin, measurement errors, and noise. Our novel approach augments a loop-shaped inverse-Jacobian PI controller with a residual policy learned via Soft Actor-Critic in the digital twin. A delay-compensated Kalman filter fuses out-of-sequence camera measurements to provide latency-aware moving target position estimates. We compare the achieved performance to a performance bound for a class of model-based controllers derived from a frequency-domain sensitivity analysis, quantifying the minimum achievable error imposed by uncertain kinematic model mismatch. Simulation and hardware experiments indicate that our residual policy drives tracking error below the bound. Across conveyor speeds (27.9-41.8 mm/s), our method reduces mean tracking errors from 1.1-1.4 mm (baseline inverse-Jacobian PI) to 0.3-0.7 mm. These results show that learning residual corrections in a limited-fidelity simulator, combined with delay compensation, can substantially improve model-based control under modeling and measurement uncertainties while maintaining reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [23] [MARS: Dynamic Routing of Reinforcement-Learned Atomic Skills for Multi-Stage Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19324)
*Zequan Zeng,Junqin Yang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS：将冻结的通用VLA策略与多个独立训练的原子技能RL策略结合，用共享RL Token状态表示与VLA参考动作优化各关键阶段专用策略，并用基于Mamba时序编码与技能语义注意力的动态路由器实现多阶段操作中的通用-专用策略协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在多任务机器人操作中能力较强，但在抓取、对齐、插入等关键操作阶段的控制精度与执行稳定性不足；需要在保留通用操作能力的同时引入任务特定的细粒度控制，并在多阶段任务中有效协调通用策略与专用策略。

Method: 提出MARS（模块化原子技能路由系统）动态路由框架：集成冻结的通用VLA策略与多个独立训练的原子技能策略；以共享RL Token状态表示和VLA参考动作作为基础，针对不同关键操作阶段用强化学习优化专用控制策略；设计动态路由器，采用Mamba循环编码与历史特征窗口捕捉操作状态演化，将候选策略的技能描述编码为语义查询，通过注意力机制建立技能语义与时序状态特征的对应关系，实现高层策略选择。

Result: 固定轨迹评测下，在四个LIBERO任务套件上平均Macro-F1达90.47%，较MLP路由器提升7.08个百分点；在仿真Tube Transport任务中，全任务成功率由20.04%提升至85.67%。

Conclusion: 阶段特定的强化学习与具备时序状态感知的动态策略路由，能够改善多阶段机器人操作中的多策略协同与整体任务执行性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in multi-task robotic manipulation. However, they still face limitations in control precision and execution stability during critical manipulation stages, such as object grasping, alignment and insertion. To combine general-purpose manipulation capabilities with task-specific fine-grained control, while enabling effective coordination between generalist and specialized policies throughout multi-stage manipulation tasks, this paper proposes MARS (Modular Atomic-skill Routing System), a dynamic routing framework for reinforcement learning-based atomic skills. MARS integrates a frozen generalist VLA policy with multiple independently trained atomic skill policies. Using a shared RL Token state representation and the reference action of VLA, specialized control policies are optimized through reinforcement learning for different cr`itical manipulation stages. For high-level policy selection, we develop a dynamic router that integrates temporal context and skill semantics. The router employs Mamba-based recurrent encoding and a historical feature window to capture state evolution during manipulation. Skill descriptions of candidate policies are encoded into semantic queries, and attention mechanisms are used to establish correspondences between skill semantics and temporal state features. Experimental results show that MARS achieves an average Macro-F1 score of 90.47% across four LIBERO task suites under fixed-trajectory evaluation, outperforming an MLP-based router by 7.08 percentage points. In the simulated Tube Transport task, the full-task success rate increases from 20.04% to 85.67%. These results demonstrate that stage-specific reinforcement learning and temporal state-aware dynamic policy routing can improve multi-policy coordination and overall task execution performance in multi-stage robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [24] [Long-Horizon Causal Inertial Odometry in Multistory Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19387)
*Tianzi Zhang,Yan Wang,Hao Qiao,Jian Kuang,Xiaoji Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出局部Token因果注意力（LTCA）长时程架构，将1秒惯性窗口压缩为1Hz运动Token，在60秒因果缓存内维持事件起始时刻的运动状态，并引入可插拔气压计分支与在线/回退联合条件训练，解决电梯/扶梯匀速段垂直位移被系统性低估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层建筑室内3D定位中，电梯或扶梯加速数秒后以恒定速度巡航数十秒，步态激励消失、比力退化为恒定重力反作用，瞬时速度在1–2秒窗口内不可观测。短窗口学习式惯性里程计输出因此退化到训练集条件均值，垂直位移被系统性低估。

Method: 1）LTCA：长时程架构，将每个1秒惯性窗口压缩为一个1Hz运动Token，在有限的60秒因果缓存上保持起始事件所建立的���动状态；2）可插拔气压计分支：在线时通过局部差分高度观测为垂直通道提供直接高程约束；3）配对在线/回退条件训练，使气压计在训练时同时正则化两模式共享的表征；回退时同一权重可退化为纯惯性推理。

Result: 在自建MSIO九栋建筑基准上，气压计在线时RoNIN与TLIO实例的ATE-3D在Seen/Unseen划分分别为2.83/4.18 m与2.44/5.12 m；推理时禁用气压计，同一权重回退到纯惯性推理达到3.24/4.28 m与2.64/5.23 m，仍优于所有原生短窗口基线。并在公开RoNIN与TLIO基准上进一步验证。

Conclusion: 长时程Token化因果注意力结合可插拔气压计约束，可有效克服恒速段瞬时速度不可观测导致的垂直漂移，且无气压计时仍保持对短窗口基线的优势，具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: In indoor 3D localization inside multistory buildings, an elevator or an escalator accelerates for a few seconds and then cruises at constant velocity for tens of seconds. Gait excitation disappears, the specific force degenerates into a constant gravity reaction, and instantaneous velocity becomes unobservable within a 1-2 s window. The output of short-window learned inertial odometry consequently degrades toward the conditional mean of the training set, and vertical displacement is systematically underestimated. We propose Local-Token Causal Attention (LTCA), a long-horizon architecture that compresses each 1 s inertial window into a 1 Hz motion token and sustains, over a bounded 60 s causal cache, the motion state established at the onset event. We further introduce a pluggable barometer branch that, when online, supplies a direct elevation constraint to the vertical channel through local differential height observations, and we train it with paired online/fallback conditional training so that the barometer also regularizes the representation shared by the two modes during training. On MSIO, our benchmark of nine buildings, the RoNIN and TLIO instances reach 3D trajectory errors (ATE-3D) of 2.83/4.18 m and 2.44/5.12 m on the Seen and Unseen splits, respectively, with the barometer online. With the barometer disabled at inference, the same weights fall back to pure inertial inference and reach 3.24/4.28 m and 2.64/5.23 m, still below every native short-window baseline. We further validate the framework on the public RoNIN and TLIO benchmarks.

</details>


### [25] [Semantics First, Pixels Later: Mission-Driven Event Streams for Drone Video over Bandwidth-Starved Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19463)
*Cong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 BPS（行为原语流），在带宽极端受限的链路（1 Mbps 至 1 kbps）上优先传输语义事件而非像素：机载检测与跟踪产出多帧事件短记录（平均 25 字节），以地面侧语言模型的问答准确率作为评价指标。开发集上事件流以 0.13 kbps 答对 74.3% 的问题，远超视频方案；但留出集上使用机载感知时优势消失，且 Jetson Orin Nano 上仅 4.5 fps 会漏掉多数事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在带宽严重受限的链路上无法回传操作员所需的视频，而操作员真正关心的问题往往不是像素，而是"三个人是否汇合、何时、何地"这类语义问题。因此需要研究在 1 Mbps 甚至 1 kbps 链路上"应该传什么、以什么顺序传"，而不是继续压缩视频。

Method: 提出 BPS 行为原语流：机载运行检测器与跟踪器，由 16–125 字节的任务卡筛选多帧事件，生成平均 25 字节的短记录，先发语义、后发位置与图像。评价采用预注册协议与留出集，以地面侧语言模型回答问题的正确率为度量，对比对象包括：同一模型视觉微调读取的 H.265、地面自行运行感知的 H.265、量化轨迹流、机载字幕，以及"什么都不接收"的读题基线；并测量各流在模拟链路上的最低可用码率与 Jetson Orin Nano 上的运行帧率。

Result: 开发集（84% 问题关于汇合，且多为单一地点）：事件流以 0.13 kbps 答对 74.3%；空输入基线 57.4%；视频读题在 100 kbps 为 59.6%，源帧上为 64.7%。地面运行同一感知时，视频需 100 kbps（约 800 倍码率）才能追平事件流，64 kbps 及以下落后；轨迹流需 96 倍字节追平，17 倍字节时低 0.03。留出集上结果变弱：使用标注轨迹（oracle）时事件流在 100 kbps 下比 H.265 高 0.13；但用机载感知时仅 +0.01 [-0.04, +0.06]，且不优于空输入。单独经模拟链路发送：事件记录在 1 Mbps 为 0.739、0.3 kbps 仍为 0.724；逐帧轨迹流可低至 1 kbps，视频配地面感知约需 100 kbps。Jetson Orin Nano 上未改动流水线为 4.5 fps，漏检多数事件。

Conclusion: 在开发集上，行为原语事件流在极低码率下显著优于视频与轨迹方案，说明"语义先行"的传输顺序在带宽受限场景下具有很高的码率效率。但该优势在留出集上依赖感知质量：oracle 轨迹仍占优，机载感知则收益消失；同时嵌入式平台实时性不足（4.5 fps）导致大量事件漏检。因此结论应谨慎——语义流的价值成立，但需以更强/更轻的机载感知与跨场景泛化为前提。

Abstract: A surveillance drone on a bandwidth-starved link cannot send the video its operator would need, yet the operator's questions are often not about pixels: did three people converge, when, and where? We study what should cross a link of 1 Mbps down to 1 kbps instead, and in what order. BPS, a behavior-primitive stream, sends semantics first and pixels later: it runs a detector and a tracker onboard and sends short records (25 bytes on average) of multi-frame events selected by a mission card of 16-125 bytes, ahead of positions and pictures. We measure it by what a ground-side language model answers from it, under a preregistered protocol with a held-out set, against H.265 read by the vision fine-tune of the same model, H.265 on which the ground runs our own perception, a stream of quantized tracks, and onboard captions. On the development set (84% of questions about convergence, mostly one location) the event stream answers 74.3% of questions at 0.13 kbps. A reader that receives nothing answers 57.4%, and the video reader 59.6% at 100 kbps and 64.7% on the source frames. When the ground instead runs our perception on the video, video matches the stream at 100 kbps, about 800 times the stream's rate, and falls behind at 64 kbps and below. A track stream matches it at 96 times the bytes and loses 0.03 at 17 times. On held-out data the result is weaker: with events taken from annotated tracks, an oracle, the stream beats H.265 at 100 kbps (+0.13), but with onboard perception it does not (+0.01 [-0.04, +0.06]) and is no better than receiving nothing. Sent alone through emulated links the event records answer 0.739 at 1 Mbps and still 0.724 at 0.3 kbps; an every-frame track stream holds down to 1 kbps and video with ground-side perception down to about 100 kbps. On a Jetson Orin Nano the unchanged pipeline runs at 4.5 fps and misses most events.

</details>


### [26] [Depth-Aware Path Planning and Collision Avoidance for Robotic Nursing Assistants Using Real-Time 3D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19534)
*Peeyush Thakur,Dharmendra Sharma,Archit Sharma,Sandeep Gupta,Anuj Nandanwar,Laxmidhar Behera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向护理机器人的深度感知路径规划与避障框架，融合RGB-D深度传感、规则避障与NavfnROS全局规划器，在Husky A200+Kinova Gen3平台上实现，仿真成功率提升40%、最小间隙提升35%，并完成真实环境验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 护理机器人在含低矮障碍物的杂乱环境中需安全导航。传统LiDAR方案难以检测透明或低矮障碍，纯视觉方案易受噪声与遮挡影响，因此需要多传感融合的感知与规划方案。

Method: 集成RGB-D深度传感、基于规则的避障与NavfnROS全局路径规划器；采用全局规划、感知融合、实时控制的多层架构；在ROS-Gazebo中实现，硬件为Husky A200底盘配Kinova Gen3机械臂。

Result: 仿真杂乱环境下相比单一传感器基线，成功率最高提升40%，最小间隙提升35%；真实环境实验验证其计算高效且具安全感知能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架可作为自主医疗护理机器人计算高效、安全可靠的导航方案，弥补单一传感器在低矮与透明障碍检测上的不足。

Abstract: This paper presents a depth-aware path planning and collision avoidance framework for the safe navigation of robotic nursing assistants in cluttered environments with low-height obstacles. Conventional LiDAR-based navigation systems, while accurate for mapping and localization, fail to detect transparent or low-height obstacles, whereas vision-based systems are prone to noise and occlusion. The proposed framework integrates RGB-D depth sensing, rule-based obstacle avoidance, and the NavfnROS global path planner to drive the robot from an initial point to a goal point. The proposed approach is implemented on the Husky A200 robot, equipped with a Kinova Gen3 arm, to form a complete setup for a robotic nursing assistant. This system features a multi-layer architecture for global planning, perception fusion, and real-time control in the ROS-Gazebo environment. Experimental evaluations in a simulated cluttered environment demonstrate up to 40\% higher success rate and 35\% improvement in minimum clearance compared with single-sensor baselines. Furthermore, real-world experimental results validate the proposed approach as a computationally efficient and safety-aware navigation solution for autonomous healthcare robots.

</details>


### [27] [Cooperative Gradient Estimation for Multi-Robot Source Seeking with Heteroskedastic Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19535)
*Alessandro Renzaglia,Lara Briñón-Arranz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多异构机器人协同定位三维标量场源的方法：在球面上构造以传感器噪声方差为参数的加权重心Voronoi剖分编队，结合加权最小二乘估计编队中心处的场梯度，并从理论上证明该构造近乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人测量含有噪声，且不同机器人的传感精度不同导致异方差数据；现有方法往往忽略传感器异构性，难以在噪声条件下获得稳健的梯度估计与源定位。

Method: 各机器人测量所在位置的场强度并在队内共享；将加权重心Voronoi剖分编队策略（定义于球面、由传感器噪声方差参数化）与加权最小二乘估计器结合，计算编队中心处的场梯度；推导梯度估计协方差的闭式表达，给出球面任意编队可达到估计方差的下界；再利用估计梯度以梯度上升策略引导团队趋向源位置。

Result: 解析证明该剖分构造渐近满足达到估计方差下界的条件，即近乎最优；仿真结果表明该方法优于对比策略，并能在机器人失效时在线重构编队，体现良好适应性。

Conclusion: 在编队设计与估计中同时显式考虑传感器异构性，可显著提升噪声环境下梯度估计的稳健性与源定位效果，并为多机器人协同源搜索提供理论保证与自适应能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach for localizing the source of a three-dimensional scalar field using a team of heterogeneous cooperative mobile robots. Each robot measures the field intensity at its location and shares the information within the team. Measurement noise is explicitly considered, and heterogeneity arises from robot-dependent sensing accuracies, leading to heteroskedastic data. The proposed framework combines a weighted centroidal Voronoi tessellation-based formation strategy, defined on a spherical surface and parameterized by sensor noise variances, with a Weighted Least Squares estimator to compute the field gradient at the formation center. By explicitly accounting for sensor heterogeneity in both formation design and estimation, the method achieves robust gradient estimation under noisy measurements. In particular, we show analytically that this construction is nearly optimal. The covariance of the gradient estimate is derived in closed form, providing a lower bound on the attainable estimation variance for any formation on the sphere, and the proposed tessellation is shown to asymptotically satisfy the conditions under which this bound is attained. The estimated gradient is then exploited within a gradient-ascent strategy to guide the team toward the source location. Simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach compared to alternative strategies and highlight its intrinsic ability to reconfigure online in response to robot failures, thereby enhancing its adaptability.

</details>


### [28] [ResWAM: Learning What Changes, Not What Stays, for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19559)
*Chongyu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella,Jason Corso,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉残差学习（visual residual learning）与 ResWAM 模型：在冻结 DINOv3 特征空间中只监督“当前帧→未来帧”的特征变化量，并用 0.5B 双分支 DiT 共享训练动作分支与残差分支，从而在低训练/推理算力下提升视觉-语言-动作策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）监督完整未来观测，但一个动作块内观测变化很小，未来预测损失被“复制静态背景”主导，难以学到任务相关的机器人与物体运动；同时现有 WAM 依赖大型视频生成模型或独立世界模型，训练与推理开销大，不利于闭环部署。

Method: 1) 视觉残差学习：在冻结的 DINOv3 特征空间中，仅监督当前帧到未来帧的特征残差，而非完整未来特征；2) ResWAM：总参数 2.3B 的残差世界-动作模型，含轻量 0.5B 双分支 DiT，动作分支与残差分支通过条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）联合训练。

Result: 分析表明残差目标比完整未来特征具有更高特征秩、表征更丰富多样，且空间上集中于机械臂与被操作物体而非背景。无机器人预训练条件下，ResWAM 在 LIBERO-Plus 平均成功率 81.1%、RoboTwin 2.0 达 93.8%、RoboDojo 达 9.40% 成功率 / 13.41 分，训练算力仅 2.7–4.0 TFLOPs、推理算力 1.0–1.4 TFLOPs；真机 Open/Put/Stack 任务平均 80.0%（较 π_{0.5} 提升 10.0%），延迟 92.7 ms。

Conclusion: 以特征残差替代完整未来观测作为世界建模目标，可同时提升策略学习的有效性与计算效率，使世界-动作模型在低算力、低延迟条件下实现领先的多基准与真机表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment vision-language-action policies (VLAs) with future prediction so that the policy can anticipate how its actions will affect the physical world. Existing WAMs supervise complete future observations. However, observations typically change little over an action chunk, so the future loss is dominated by copying static background content rather than learning task-relevant robot and object motion, limiting policy learning. Moreover, existing WAMs rely on large video generation models or separate world models, incurring heavy training and inference compute that hinders closed-loop deployment. To improve policy performance, we propose visual residual learning, supervising only the feature change from the current frame to the future frame in a frozen DINOv3 space. Compared with complete future features, this residual learning target yields a higher feature rank with richer and more diverse representations of scene changes, and spatially concentrates on the robot arm and manipulated objects rather than the background. To further improve computational efficiency, we present ResWAM, a residual world-action model with 2.3B parameters in total, including a lightweight 0.5B dual-branch DiT that jointly trains an action branch and a residual branch via conditional flow matching. Without robot pretraining (i.e., pretraining on external robot action trajectories beyond the benchmark demonstrations), ResWAM achieves top average success rates on LIBERO-Plus (81.1%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.8%), and RoboDojo (9.40% success, 13.41 score) at the lowest training (2.7-4.0 TFLOPs) and inference compute (1.0-1.4 TFLOPs), and leads real-robot Open, Put, and Stack (80.0% average, +10.0% over $π_{0.5}$) at 92.7 ms latency.

</details>


### [29] [Towards Probabilistically Safe Reinforcement Learning: Theory and Deployment on Humanoid and Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19616)
*Pol Mestres,Varun Gabbita,Jason H. K. Siu,Sergio A. Esteban,Hampus Serneke,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将鲁棒安全梯度流强化学习框架（RSGF-RL）推广到一般轨迹级安全约束（含机会约束），允许有偏的值与梯度估计，并在理论上给出训练全程约束违反界与收敛到近似KKT点的保证，最终用PPO风格估计实现人形机器人与无人机的零样本仿真到现实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习不能保证学到的策略满足安全约束，限制了其在安全关键场景中的应用；而现有带可证保证的安全RL方法通常只支持受限的约束类别，且需要目标与约束函数的无偏估计，因而无法直接与PPO等先进算法结合。

Method: 扩展robust safe gradient flow-based reinforcement learning（RSGF-RL）框架：使公式支持一般类别的轨迹级安全约束（包括以指定概率避碰的机会约束）；理论分析允许值与梯度估计存在偏差，并在此基础上推导训练过程中的约束违反上界与迭代点收敛性。

Result: 证明以高概率成立：训练全程的约束违反量被估计偏差加上一个随rollout数量增加而消失的项所界定；若初始策略不安全，违反量会衰减至该水平。同时建立迭代点收敛到原安全RL问题近似KKT点的性质。实验上，用PPO风格估计实现的RSGF-RL在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现全身控制策略的零样本仿真到现实部署，并在Crazyflie无人机上实现无碰撞航点导航。

Conclusion: RSGF-RL经扩展后可兼容有偏估计与一般安全约束，从而能与PPO等主流算法结合，并在理论上保持安全性与收敛保证，在实际机器人平台上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire complex skills, but standard RL formulations do not guarantee that learned policies satisfy safety constraints, which limits their use in safety-critical settings. Existing safe RL methods with provable guarantees typically support only restricted classes of constraints and require unbiased estimators of the objective and constraint functions, which prevents their direct integration with state-of-the-art algorithms such as proximal policy optimization (PPO). In this paper, we extend the recently introduced robust safe gradient flow-based reinforcement learning (RSGF-RL) framework to overcome these limitations. Our formulation accommodates a general class of trajectory-level safety constraints, including chance constraints such as collision avoidance with a prescribed probability, and our analysis allows for biased value and gradient estimators. We show that, with high probability, the constraint violation throughout training is bounded by the estimator bias plus a term that vanishes as the number of rollouts grows, and decays to this level if the initial policy is unsafe. We also establish the convergence properties of the iterates to approximate KKT points of the original safe RL problem. Finally, we show that RSGF-RL implemented with PPO-style estimates enables zero-shot sim-to-real deployment of whole-body control policies on a Unitree G1 humanoid and collision-free waypoint navigation on a Crazyflie drone.

</details>


### [30] [PSK: A Matrix-Free Single-Loop Method for K-Level Physics-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19660)
*Zherong Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单循环惩罚式K层方法PSK，仅用梯度与投影即可并行求解多步物理控制中的隐式物理优化问题，并借助"平滑墙"处理无约束规范（CQ）失效的接触约束，保持O(ε^{-3})复杂度，还给出无统一CQ时的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的仿真步需在大量接触与碰撞约束下最小化能量，对其做隐式微分通常要解Hessian或KKT线性系统；作者追问：仅用梯度步是否足够？同时接触丰富场景常违反约束规范（CQ），标准隐式微分假设不成立。

Method: 无约束情形下把K步控制视为K层强凸下层的优化并重构为单一min-max问题，提出PSK（Penalized Single-loop K-level）：只用梯度与投影、所有层并行更新。对接触约束，用带宽恒定的平滑墙替换每个约束，在不需要CQ的温和假设下分析带化下层解。

Result: PSK达到ε-稳定控制需O(ε^{-3})次迭代，指数与K无关、常数依赖K；带化情形下精确解可行且满足banded Fritz-John，复杂度维持O(ε^{-3})。但若无随带宽一致CQ，收缩带宽会丢失K无关指数：宽度ε、固定K≥2时，确定性zero-respecting方法在控制维度随1/ε增长的困难实例上需Ω(ε^{-(K+3)/4})次调用。已在平滑运动学与动力学多机器人避障导航中验证假设；实验中PSK逐例调参后16层可达全部目标，同等时间下18例中17例损失低于调参unrolling，10例高于隐式微分。

Conclusion: 梯度步足以支撑隐式物理控制，无需Hessian/KKT求解；平滑墙使无CQ的接触约束得到可行且复杂度可控的解，但一致CQ对保持K无关复杂度不可或缺。

Abstract: Physics-based control is central to robot learning, but differentiating through implicit physics steps typically requires Hessian or Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) linear solves; we ask whether gradient steps suffice. Each simulation step minimizes an energy under many contact and collision constraints. Without constraints, we view control over $K$ steps as $K$-level optimization with strongly convex lower levels, recast as one min--max problem. Our Penalized Single-loop $K$-level method (PSK) uses only gradients and a projection, updates all levels in parallel, and reaches an $ε$-stationary control in $O(ε^{-3})$ iterations, with a $K$-independent exponent but a $K$-dependent constant. Contact-rich constraints, however, typically violate a constraint qualification (CQ). We replace each constraint by a smooth wall that acts on a band of constant width. Under mild assumptions that need no CQ, every exact solution of the banded lower levels is then feasible and banded Fritz--John, and PSK keeps the $O(ε^{-3})$ complexity. Without a CQ that is uniform in the band width, however, a shrinking band loses the $K$-independent exponent: with width $ε$ and fixed $K\geq2$, deterministic zero-respecting methods need $Ω(ε^{-(K+3)/4})$ calls on a hard instance whose control dimension grows with $1/ε$. We prove our assumptions for smoothed kinematic and dynamic multi-robot navigation among segment obstacles. In navigation experiments, PSK, tuned per case, reaches every goal with sixteen levels and, at equal time, reaches a lower loss than tuned unrolling in 17 of 18 cases, while implicit differentiation is lower in 10.

</details>


### [31] [Early Readability of Trajectory Quality in Diffusion Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19684)
*Xiang Zhang,Ziyang Leng,Yuxin Bao,Zhihao Zhao,Bolei Zhou,Xugui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散式驾驶规划器的中间状态能否在去噪完成前预测候选轨迹质量，发现第一次去噪器评估后的状态已足以进行有效选择，并据此提出"早选后补"方法，降低计算量并提升轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式驾驶规划器能为同一场景生成多条候选轨迹，但候选质量参差不齐。若能在去噪完成前就判断哪条候选更优，既可节省对全部候选完整去噪的计算开销，又能提升最终输出轨迹质量。作者希望系统性地检验"中间状态是否携带可用于候选选择的信号"这一问题。

Method: 在 Alpamayo、ReCogDrive-IL 与 HDP 三个预训练规划器上开展系统研究：用线性探针从中间状态预测最终轨迹质量，在留出场景上评估候选排序与选择效果；进一步分析读出效果对表示形式和读取器（reader）的依赖；并据此提出早选方法——在第一次去噪器评估后即完成选择，仅对选中候选继续完整去噪。

Result: 三个规划器上，来自第一次去噪器评估的状态已能支持有信息量的选择，而继续去噪并不能稳定提升选择质量；选择能力可与有限的整体排序精度并存，且读出效果依赖表示与读取器。早选方法相较对每个候选完整去噪降低了计算量，并在三个规划器上均优于随机选择；其中 Alpamayo 的单相机与四相机配置下平均位移误差分别降低 17.8% 与 13.4%。闭环评估也显示平均驾驶分数与行驶进度更高。

Conclusion: 扩散规划器的早期中间状态已包含足够的候选质量信号，可实现有效早选。该发现既揭示了去噪过程信息演化的特性，也提供了兼顾效率与质量的实用选择策略，并在开环与闭环评测中均获验证。

Abstract: Diffusion-based driving planners can generate multiple trajectories for the same scene, but these candidates vary in quality. Can their internal states reveal which candidate will be better before denoising is complete? We systematically study this question in three pretrained planners: Alpamayo, ReCogDrive-IL, and HDP, using linear probes to predict final trajectory quality from intermediate states and evaluating candidate ranking and selection on held-out scenes. Across all three planners, we observe that states from the first denoiser evaluation already support informative selection, while further denoising does not consistently improve selection quality. Further analyses show that readout depends on the representation and reader, and that selection can coexist with limited overall ranking accuracy. These findings motivate an early-selection method that selects after the first denoiser evaluation and completes only the chosen candidate. This reduces computation relative to fully denoising every candidate and improves open-loop trajectory quality over random selection across all three planners. In particular, it reduces Alpamayo's average displacement error by 17.8% and 13.4% in one- and four-camera configurations, respectively. Closed-loop evaluations also show higher mean driving scores and progress.

</details>


### [32] [Physics-Grounded STL Monitoring from Image Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19688)
*Xinpei Ni,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从多视角图像学习共享潜在表示并解码物理语义边距的视觉STL监控器，结合共训练与保形预测校准，实现无需重训即可对任意STL公式给出满足/违反认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务需满足可用信号时序逻辑（STL）表达的时序与条件约束。用相机图像监控这些约束存在困难：STL鲁棒性依赖距离、间隙等物理边距，而这些量无法从图像直接观测。同时，为每个公式单独训练视觉监控器成本高、难以泛化到新公式。

Method: 从多视角图像学习共享潜在表示，为各个谓词解码物理意义明确的边距；将编码器与谓词解码器共训练（对比冻结编码器）；利用STL语义把边距组合为任意有界公式的鲁棒性估计，无需重训；再用保形预测校准所选STL子句的解码误差，得到双侧区间，并通过区间STL传播到由这些子句组成的任意公式。

Result: 共训练使认证率从58.7%提升至96.3%（谓词符号准确率几乎不变）。在LIBERO模拟厨房任务中，对18个未见公式的4662次评估，监控器正确预测满足或违反的比例达98.9%；对最长17分钟的任务，校准区间在96.9%的任务上能判定满足或违反且无错误结论；单次校准在8个子句的全部255个合取式上提供91.0%的同时覆盖率，支持无需重新校准的新公式。

Conclusion: 学习可解码物理边距的共享视觉表示，可使STL监控具备组合性；结合保形校准可给出双向、可传播且覆盖有保证的认证结论，并泛化到未见公式与新任务，显著降低部署时的重训与重标定成本。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation missions must satisfy timed and conditional requirements that can be expressed in signal temporal logic (STL). While a practical means of monitoring these requirements is from camera images, this is challenging because STL robustness depends on physical margins, such as distances and clearances, that are not directly observed. Rather than learning a separate visual monitor for each formula, we learn a shared latent representation from multi-view images and decode physically meaningful margins for individual predicates. Co-training the encoder with predicate decoders increases the certification rate from 58.7% to 96.3% compared with a frozen encoder, despite nearly unchanged predicate sign accuracy. STL semantics then compose these margins into robustness estimates for any bounded formula over the trained predicates, without retraining. We further calibrate the decoding errors of selected STL clauses using conformal prediction, yielding two-sided intervals that certify both satisfaction and violation and propagate through interval STL to any formula composed from the clauses. In simulated kitchen missions (LIBERO), our monitor correctly predicts satisfaction or violation in 98.9% of 4662 evaluations across 18 unseen formulas. For missions lasting up to 17 min, calibrated bounds establish satisfaction or violation for 96.9% of missions without incorrect conclusions. A single calibration also provides simultaneous 91.0% coverage across all 255 conjunctions of eight clauses, allowing new formulas without recalibration.

</details>


### [33] [Real-Time Air Pollution Monitoring and Tracking via Adaptive Density-Driven Multi-UAV Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19703)
*Julian Martinez,Ruchika Singh,Mohammad Afrazi,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应密度驱动控制（D²C）框架，用多无人机协同跟踪动态演化的污染羽流，通过传感器驱动的参考样本更新与集中协调分配实现精准跟踪，在强弱风场下均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染的准确、及时监测对公共健康与环境管理至关重要，但污染物分布动态演化，传统固定监测域方法在强风、时变风场下难以维持跟踪，需要多无人机自适应协同跟踪与建图。

Method: 将D²C策略扩展至基于预测性污染扩散与动态更新参考密度的无人机轨迹引导；采用传感器驱动的代理羽流更新机制，参考样本经梯度吸引、风平流、样本间排斥与随机扩散传播；并采用集中协调结构，各无人机共享局部测量以维护统一参考样本集，实现目标分配与轨迹规划。

Result: 在温和与强风条件下的仿真表明，方法在Sinkhorn距离指标上显著优于先进基线；在高速时变风场使羽流漂出固定监测域的场景中，基线方法失去跟踪，而D²C成功适应演化分布。

Conclusion: D²C是动态环境下高效且具韧性的空气监测方案，能够适应污染分布漂移并保持稳健跟踪。

Abstract: Accurate and timely air pollution monitoring and tracking are critical for protecting public health and supporting effective environmental management. This paper presents an adaptive Density-Driven Control (D$^2$C) framework for coordinating multiple unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to track and map dynamically evolving pollutant distributions. The proposed framework extends the D$^2$C strategy to guide UAV trajectories based on predictive pollutant propagation and dynamically updated reference densities. A sensor-driven surrogate plume update mechanism is employed, in which reference samples are propagated using locally sensed concentration gradients and wind estimates through gradient attraction, wind advection, inter-sample repulsion, and stochastic diffusion, enabling UAVs to maintain an accurate spatio-temporal representation of the pollution field. The framework utilizes a coordinated structure where individual UAVs share their local measurements with a central coordinator to maintain a unified set of reference samples, facilitating systematic target allocation and trajectory planning. Simulation results under both mild and strong wind conditions demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Sinkhorn distance. Specifically, in scenarios where the plume drifts beyond a fixed monitoring domain under high-velocity, time-varying wind fields, the tested baseline methods fail to maintain tracking, whereas the proposed D$^2$C framework successfully adapts to the evolving distribution. These findings highlight D$^2$C as an efficient and resilient solution for atmospheric monitoring in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [34] [Cache or Denoise Less? Residual Reuse in a Flow-Matching World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19709)
*Abhinav Gupta,Nissan Pow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在6B参数的Fast-WAM世界动作模型上，两个无缓存去噪步骤即可保持十步的成功率（500个初始状态上差异不超过2个百分点），并将重规划调用耗时从202 ms降至92 ms；相比之下跨周期缓存仅能节省11–14%且更脆弱，因此作者主张以"少步数"作为基线，并在部署所用的采样器与噪声设置下检验缓存复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配机器人策略每个动作块需多次网络评估逐步去噪，跨周期缓存（如C³ache）试图复用先前重规划调用的计算，但其相对"直接减少步数"是否真正更优并不清楚，缺乏在真实部署条件下的对比证据。
