Method: 以Fast-WAM（6B参数世界动作模型）为平台，在LIBERO-Goal上进行对比实验：(1) 比较无缓存的少步数与十步基线的成功率与延迟；(2) 用代价模型分析缓存可带来的理论上限；(3) 在Fast-WAM当前时间偏移下考察C³ache八步缓存所覆盖的流路径比例及其成功率，并在每次调用重采样噪声以测试缓存稳健性。

Result: 两个无缓存步骤在全部500个初始状态上与十步相比非劣（差距2个百分点内，单一检查点与噪声种子）；PyTorch默认编译模式下RTX A6000上单次重规划由202 ms降至92 ms。保留两个新鲜步骤的缓存不可能快于两个无缓存步骤；缓存两步中的第一步（每四次调用刷新）按代价模型至多节省11–14%。缓存策略脆弱：C³ache八步在当前时间偏移下覆盖80%而非44%的路径，成功率63%对95%；每次调用重采样噪声使覆盖60%以上路径的缓存下降14–40个百分点。

Conclusion: 少步数基线在效率与成功率上已接近十步，留给缓存的提升空间很小，且缓存对采样器时间偏移与噪声设置高度敏感。作者建议以少步数作为默认基线，并在实际部署的采样器与噪声条件下评估复用方法，同时发布了评估工具、日志与检查清单。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies generate each action chunk through repeated network evaluations, one per denoising step. Cross-cycle caches such as C$^3$ache reuse computation from earlier replanning calls, but whether they beat simply taking fewer steps is unclear. We compare the two in Fast-WAM, a 6B-parameter world action model, on LIBERO-Goal. Two uncached steps keep the success of ten: on all 500 initial states they are non-inferior within two percentage points, for one checkpoint and noise seed. Under PyTorch's default compile mode, they also cut a replanning call on an RTX A6000 from 202 to 92 ms. This leaves little room for caching. A cache that keeps two fresh steps cannot be faster than two uncached steps: every call still encodes the images, runs the video expert and computes those two steps. Caching the first of two steps, refreshed every fourth call, saves at most 11--14% of a call by our cost model. Cached policies are also fragile. Their losses tracked the share of the flow path the cached steps cover: under Fast-WAM's current time shift, C$^3$ache's eight cached steps cover 80% of the path instead of 44%, and the cached policy succeeds on 63% of states against 95% uncached. Resampling the noise on every call cost caches covering 60% or more of the path 14--40 points. These results argue for fewer-step baselines and for testing reuse under the deployed sampler and noise settings. We release our evaluation harness, logs and a checklist.

</details>


### [35] [Sim-to-Real Room-Mediated Object-Goal Navigation with ROS 2 and Nav2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19720)
*Matthew Chua,Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将基于房间概率向量（RPV）的ObjectNav策略通过机器人无关的ROS 2桥接部署到Spot与TurtleBot 4实体平台，在真实研究设施中进行66次试验，原始成功率为0.455，比仿真中报告的0.539低8.4个百分点，并分析了造成差距的现实因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav方法多在仿真中评估，可扩展且可重复，但真实部署往往只做可行性演示，缺乏系统检验；作者希望考察语义先验（房间共现）在物理环境中的泛化能力，并明确仿真到真机迁移所需的适配。

Method: 部署Room-Mediated Co-occurrence策略（对房间概率向量RPV进行推理），借助机器人无关的ROS 2桥接运行于Boston Dynamics Spot和Clearpath TurtleBot 4两种平台；描述将RPV从仿真迁移到硬件时在传感、定位和动作接口方面的改造；在高校研究生科研设施中执行66次试验，覆盖11类目标物体、2个起始位置。

Result: Spot上66次试验的原始成功率为0.455。分析指出未探测到的玻璃墙、进入禁入区域、可用深度范围受限、目标尺寸偏小以及房间词表与物理布局不匹配，是真实成功率与仿真成功率0.539之间8.4个百分点差距的可能原因。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 真实世界部署可复现语义先验的有效性，但存在明显性能落差；玻璃等传感器透明物体、安全边界、深度感知范围与语义词表对齐是RPV类方法落地的主要瓶颈，需在系统层面加以解决。

Abstract: Although object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) approaches are usually evaluated in simulation, which offers scale and repeatable environments, real-world deployments are rarely examined beyond feasibility demonstrations. This paper presents the deployment of Room-Mediated Co-occurrence, an ObjectNav policy that reasons over Room Probability Vectors (RPVs), on two robotic platforms (Boston Dynamics Spot and Clearpath Robotics TurtleBot 4) through a robot-agnostic ROS 2 bridge. We study how these semantic priors generalise to physical environments and describe the adaptations required to transfer RPV from simulation to hardware, covering sensing, localisation, and the action interface. Across 66 trials on Spot, searching for 11 object categories from two starting positions in a university postgraduate research facility, RPV achieves a raw Success Rate (SR) of 0.455. Our analysis identifies undetected glass walls, entry into off-limits areas, limited usable depth range, target size, and mismatch between the room lexicon and physical layout as plausible contributors to the 8.4 percentage-point gap between the real-world SR and the reported simulated SR of 0.539. Code is available at https://github.com/Matthew-K-Chua/rpv-ros2-bridge.

</details>


### [36] [AlphaTruck: A Platform for High-Speed Off-Road Autonomy on Unstructured Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19730)
*Rwik Rana,Adam J. Thorpe,Jesse Quattrociocchi,William Ward,Goktug Poyrazoglu,Adam Uccello,Christian Ellis,Rahul Moorthy Mahesh,Yukang Cao,Nathan Tsoi,Garrett Warnell,Volkan Isler,Ufuk Topcu,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlphaTruck 是一个基于 1/5 比例 RC 车辆底盘、集成多模态感知、嵌入式 CPU-GPU 计算与车载自主导航栈的越野自主导航平台，可在仅有车载传感与计算条件下以 6–10 m/s 自主行驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主导航研究缺少合适的物理平台：全尺寸车辆能力强但昂贵、后勤负担重、超限损耗大；RC 小型车便宜但地形通过性和载荷（传感器、计算）能力受限。作者希望在两者之间取得平衡。

Method: 利用小型化传感与计算的最新进展，在 1/5 比例大型 RC 车辆底盘上集成多模态感知、嵌入式 CPU-GPU 计算平台和车载自主导航栈，并在质量、功率与散热预算受限条件下平衡五项需求：崎岖地形机动性、丰富感知、车载计算、载荷容量（容纳自主栈并留出任务载荷空间）、可维护与可修复性。

Result: 平台仅依靠车载感知与计算即可在越野环境中以 6–10 m/s 速度自主导航；同时可搭载任务专用载荷，定位于研究测试床及商用/国防场景（勘测、搜救、农业等）的现场部署平台。

Conclusion: AlphaTruck 表明 1/5 比例平台可作为兼具能力与成本效益的越野自主导航研究与应用载体，并强调了在受限质量、功率、散热预算下多目标权衡设计的重要性。

Abstract: Progress in autonomous navigation in off-road environments requires not only advances in algorithms, but also robust and capable physical platforms suited to both development and deployment. Today, off-road autonomy is typically developed and tested at one of two extremes of vehicle scale, each with different drawbacks. Full-size vehicles are capable but expensive, require large logistic infrastructure for deployment and maintenance, and expensive when pushed beyond their limits. RC-scale vehicles are small and inexpensive, but limited in the terrain they can traverse and the payload that they can carry, which includes their sensors and compute. We present the AlphaTruck platform, which leverages recent advances in compact sensing and compute to integrate multimodal sensing, embedded CPU-GPU compute, and an onboard autonomy stack for high-speed, off-road navigation, all integrated into a large-scale, 1/5th RC vehicle chassis. AlphaTruck is designed as both a research testbed and a field-deployable platform for commercial and defense missions such as survey, search and rescue, and agriculture, with the capacity to carry task-specific payloads. At 1/5 scale, the autonomy stack, sensors, and batteries all compete for the same mass, power, and cooling budget, so the platform must balance five needs: mobility over rough terrain, rich sensing, onboard computation, payload capacity to carry the autonomy stack while leaving room for task-specific equipment, and practical operation and repair. Thus, AlphaTruck navigates off-road environments autonomously at 6-10 m/s using only onboard sensing and compute.

</details>


### [37] [Cooperative Transport of Unknown Objects by Distributed Effort Regulation in Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19731)
*Tamzidul Mina,Shyam S. Kannan,Wonse Jo,Shaocheng Luo,Galen B. King,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式多机器人搬运策略，在无物体先验信息、无机器人数量先验的条件下，通过沿物体边界动态选取推点并结合"无物体模型"的航向误差驱动推力调节，实现高效、鲁棒的协同搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同搬运方法通常依赖对物体形状、尺寸、质量或质量分布的先验知识，且假设机器人数量已知，难以适应真实场景中未知物体与动态任务规模。

Method: 机器人沿物体边界移动并动态确定可行推点；提出无物体模型的航向误差驱动推力调节方法，利用个体与集体航向误差调节各自推力；当推点失效或所需推力超出机器人纠正航向能力时，机器人动态重定位。实验涵盖简单与复杂（凸/非凸）形状、动态变化的重量分布，并分析机器人分布与初始位姿的影响。

Result: 在多种形状与动态质量分布下获得高效物体路径，多次试验搬运时间一致；对非均匀摩擦、轮子打滑等外部扰动具有鲁棒性；可扩展性分析表明搬运时间取决于可用推动面数量，而该数量由物体尺寸、形状及机器人间安全距离决定。

Conclusion: 所提分布式策略无需物体与机器人数量先验即可完成搬运任务，性能稳定、路径高效、对扰动鲁棒，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: A distributed multi-robot object transportation strategy is proposed for objects of unknown shape, size, geometry, mass and mass distribution without any prior knowledge about the object or number of robots involved in the transportation process. Robots follow the boundary of the object and dynamically determine viable pushing points. A novel object-model-free heading-error-driven pushing effort regulation method is proposed to promote goal-directed object motion; robots regulate their pushing effort using individual and collective heading errors. Robots dynamically relocate around the object boundary if a pushing point is lost or the required pushing effort exceeds its capability to correct its heading. The proposed method is validated using simple and complex shapes with convex and non-convex boundary regions and dynamically changing weight distribution of objects. Detailed analysis on the effects of robot distribution and initial object pose on the transportation performance in terms of required time and path efficiency is also presented. Scalability analysis revealed that object transportation time is dependent on the number of pushing surfaces available which in turn depends on the object size, shape, and set inter-robot safety distance. The proposed system yielded highly efficient object paths with consistent elapsed transport times over several trials robust to external disturbances such as non-uniform friction between contacting surfaces and robot wheel slip.

</details>


### [38] [M-CROSS: A Multisensor Simulation Dataset for SLAM across Water--Land Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19765)
*Yan Dong,Tianyi Wu,Junyu Yang,Shaoqiang Qiu,Zefeng Yan,Tao Zhang,Bin Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 M-CROSS——首个面向连续水陆跨域过渡场景的多传感器仿真SLAM数据集与基准，并提供过渡感知评测协议，揭示现有SLAM系统在跨介质过渡时普遍失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水陆跨域机器人需在陆地、过渡与水下阶段持续定位，但传感器特性与可用性在过渡时突变；现有数据集与传统评测指标无法刻画过渡引起的性能退化与失效，缺乏针对跨介质连续过渡的系统性评测手段。

Method: 构建 M-CROSS 多传感器仿真数据集，提供同步的LiDAR、声呐、双目相机、IMU、DVL与深度测量，6DoF位姿真值、逐传感器介质状态标注以及随介质变化的传感器退化模型；提出过渡感知评测协议，包含轨迹完成率(TCR)、分段绝对位姿误差(PW-APE)、过渡终点误差(TEE)与过渡存活时长(TSP)四项指标，分别衡量轨迹完成度、单域精度、过渡诱导致误差以及长时间部分浸没下的定位存活能力。

Result: 在6个代表性SLAM系统上的实验表明，现有方法无法在完整的水陆过渡集合上同时保持定位精度与轨迹连续性，表现出过渡诱导致漂移、轨迹不连续与跟踪失败等问题。

Conclusion: M-CROSS 填补了跨介质连续过渡SLAM数据与评测的空白，其过渡感知指标可显式量化过渡退化与失效；该数据集已公开，为后续跨域鲁棒定位研究提供基准平台。

Abstract: Water--land cross-domain robots require continuous localization across land, transition and underwater phases, where sensor characteristics and availability change abruptly. Existing datasets and conventional metrics are not designed to capture transition-induced degradation and failures. We present \textbf{M-CROSS}, to the best of our knowledge, the first multisensor simulation dataset and benchmark for SLAM across continuous water--land transitions. It provides synchronized LiDAR, sonar, stereo camera, IMU, DVL, and depth measurements; 6DoF pose ground truth; sensor-wise medium-state annotations; and medium-dependent sensor degradation models. We establish a transition-aware protocol comprising the Trajectory Completion Rate (TCR), Piecewise Absolute Pose Error (PW-APE), Transition End Error (TEE), and Transition Survival Period (TSP), which measure trajectory completion, single-domain accuracy, transition-induced error, and localization survival under prolonged partial immersion, respectively. Experiments with 6 representative SLAM systems show that existing methods fail to maintain both localization accuracy and trajectory continuity across the full set of water--land transitions, exhibiting transition-induced drift, trajectory discontinuities, and tracking failures. M-CROSS is publicly available https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MCross-Dataset

</details>


### [39] [JEPA4R: Embodied JEPA World Models with 4D Point Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19771)
*Jiarui Dong,Zhiheng Liang,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Heejeong Nam,Haoyu Chen,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA4R 是一种基于单目深度相机点云的 JEPA 世界模型，在4D点云潜空间中进行预测与规划，并通过冻结潜特征上的策略头执行动作，实现了可解释的4D想象解码与跨仿真到真机的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 世界模型存在两点不足：一是2D图像使操控所依赖的度量几何隐含且与外观纠缠；二是模型"想象"的内容不可见，通常仅通过短时程潜空间规划的仿真表现来评判。

Method: 以单目深度相机获取的点云为观测，构建 JEPA 世界模型，在其潜空间中做预测与规划（称为4D点规划）；动作由挂在冻结潜特征上的策略头输出；并在冻结世界模型上附加解码器，将想象结果可视化为4D点云。

Result: 解码器在200步内平均点误差低于1.1 cm；在OGBench 的 Cube-Single、Cube-Double、Cube-Triple 与 Scene 任务上优于像素 JEPA 模型；在 MetaWorld 与 Adroit Relocate 上分别取得98.4%和88.0%，排名第一，超过第二名 R3M 的96.0%与65.3%。仅在数字孪生中训练，配合夹爪与灵巧手可在真实机械臂上零样本运行。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的观测与规划空间提升到带度量几何的点云潜空间，既能保持想象的物理可解释性，又能提升策略学习性能并实现仿真到真实的直接迁移。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models predict future embeddings instead of pixels and plan in latent space. Two challenges remain: 2D images leave the metric geometry that manipulation acts on implicit and entangled with appearance, and what these models imagine stays hidden, as they are mostly judged in simulation by short-horizon latent planning. We present JEPA4R, a JEPA world model that observes single-depth-camera point clouds, predicts and plans in their latent space, and acts through a policy head on its frozen latents; we call this 4D point planning. A decoder on the frozen world model shows what it imagines as 4D point clouds, with a mean point error within 1.1 cm over 200 steps. Under the policy-learning protocols of OGBench and CortexBench, JEPA4R leads pixel JEPA models on OGBench Cube-Single, Cube-Double, Cube-Triple and Scene, and ranks first on MetaWorld and Adroit Relocate with 98.4% and 88.0%, above the runner-up, R3M, at 96.0% and 65.3%. Trained only in a digital twin, JEPA4R and its policy heads run zero-shot on the real arm with both a gripper and a dexterous hand.

</details>


### [40] [Recovery-Guided Model-Based Diffusion for Safety-Critical Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19775)
*Lei Zheng,Shivam Sood,Mithun Vanniasinghe,Guillaume Sartoretti,Armin Lederer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出恢复引导的基于模型扩散（recovery-guided MBD）框架，将学习到的终端可恢复性评价器嵌入短时域轨迹优化，在保持低计算量的同时实现高安全裕度的四足高速运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的高速足式运动要求规划器同时兼顾安全前瞻性与在线计算效率。短预测时域虽能降低采样（rollout）成本，但安全前瞻有限；而严格的安全约束又会极大压缩可行轨迹空间，因此需要一种在短视域下仍能保证安全的方法。

Method: 1）在短时域轨迹优化中引入学习到的终端恢复critic，该critic沿着指定恢复策略的rollout近似最坏情况下的未来间隙裕度（clearance margin）；2）提出可行性引导的精细化采样策略，在每个采样阶段同时考虑时域内约束与终端可恢复性；3）保留可行精英样本，以缓解采样支撑丢失问题。

Result: 在50组配对的随机Unitree Go2避障试验中，方法实现零碰撞、98%的严格间隙成功率，且计算量显著低于长时域MBD基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模终端可恢复性，可将长期安全信息压缩进短视域规划，从而在计算效率与安全前瞻之间取得良好折中，为安全关键足式高速运动提供可行方案。

Abstract: Safety-critical high-speed legged locomotion requires motion planners to balance safety foresight with online computational efficiency. Although short prediction horizons reduce rollout costs, they provide limited safety foresight, and strict safety constraints severely restrict the feasible trajectory space. We propose a recovery-guided model-based diffusion (MBD) framework for safety-critical legged locomotion. It incorporates a learned terminal recovery critic into short-horizon trajectory optimization. Specifically, the critic approximates the worst-case future clearance margin along rollouts of a designated recovery policy. We introduce a feasibility-guided refinement strategy that combines within-horizon constraints and terminal recoverability at each sampling stage, while retaining feasible elites to mitigate sampling-support loss. Across 50 paired randomized Unitree Go2 obstacle-avoidance trials, our approach achieves zero collisions and 98% strict-clearance success, while requiring substantially less computation than long-horizon MBD baselines. A project page is available at https://zack4417.github.io/rg-mbd/.

</details>


### [41] [DynLimit: A Simulation Benchmark for Dynamics-Critical Planning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19785)
*Yang Xiao,Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Kang Wang,Guodong Yin,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仿真平台 DynLimit，将"操控需求—闭环执行—任务完成"三问分离，支持场景组合、共享观测/控制接口、可选车辆动力学适配器、同步记录与任务导向评测；在8类任务、3种摩擦、3条基线链共450次运行上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接近车辆操控极限的实验容易混淆三类问题：机动指令请求了什么、闭环系统实际执行了什么、任务是否完成。现有工具缺乏对这三者的端到端组织与可追溯评测。

Method: 构建仿真平台：场景由道路、摩擦、障碍与参考对象组合而成；规划器与控制器通过共享观测与控制接口接入；车辆适配器可选免许可四轮模型或外部动力学求解器；同步记录用于任务评测、回放与响应检查。并提出任务导向评测方法，将给定参考需求与采样通过集配对，并进行事件对齐诊断。实验设置：8类任务族×3种摩擦环境×3条参考辅助基线链，共450次运行（360标称、18局部细化、72预设初始偏移）。

Result: 方法间差异依赖于任务族；相同的总体通过计数来源于不同的通过单元；局部细化收窄了两处采样转移但未找到连续边界；两组案例比较表明侧偏角或轮胎利用率峰值不能替代任务约束。

Conclusion: 在所有报告的运行中，三种视角始终相互区分：结果可从给定需求出发，经由实际执行响应，追溯到做出裁决的任务规则，从而保证评测的可追溯性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Experiments near vehicle handling limits must keep three questions apart: what a maneuver requests, what the closed-loop system executes, and whether the task is completed. We present DynLimit, a simulation platform that organizes this process end to end. Scenarios are composed from road, friction, obstacle, and reference objects; planners and controllers attach through a shared observation and control interface; a vehicle adapter runs either a license-free four-wheel model or an external dynamics solver; and synchronized records feed task evaluation, replay, and response inspection. The platform carries a task-oriented evaluation method that pairs a prescribed reference demand with a sampled passing set and event-aligned diagnosis. Across eight task families, three friction environments, and three reference-assisted baseline chains we executed 450 runs: 360 nominal, 18 locally refined, and 72 with prescribed initial offsets. Method differences depend on the task family, equal aggregate pass counts arise from different passing cells, and local refinement narrows two sampled transitions without locating a continuous boundary. Two case comparisons show why sideslip or tire-utilization peaks cannot replace task constraints. The three views therefore stay distinct in every reported run: an outcome can be traced from the prescribed demand, through the executed response, to the task rule that decided it. Code is available at https://github.com/Yang2581/dynlimit

</details>


### [42] [Drone Navigation for Fast, Safe Emergency Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19818)
*Pranav Nedunghat,Jeffrey Mao,Praveen Merumugumala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可实时计算最小时间轨迹并自带避障保证的方法，用于无人机在灾区执行自主导航与救援任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年全球有358起与天气相关的灾害影响数百万人，无人机若要自主投送物资、快速定位幸存者，需在复杂障碍环境中飞行。已有方法在求解时间最优轨迹时不考虑避障，因此需要一种同时兼顾避障约束与整体任务时间的方法。

Method: 在轨迹优化中引入避障约束，实时求解最小时间轨迹，并对轨迹的避障性给出保证；同时以"任务时间"（规划时间与遍历时间之和）为关键指标进行优化，示例中四旋翼穿越峡谷状障碍时大部分轨迹接近最大推力飞行。

Result: 方法可在实时条件下生成满足避障保证的时间最优轨迹，并改善导航的整体任务时间；文中给出四旋翼穿越多重峡谷状障碍投送物资的示例任务。

Conclusion: 将避障约束纳入时间最优轨迹规划，并以规划时间加遍历时间为优化指标，有助于无人机在时间敏感的紧急救援场景中更快抵达目标。

Abstract: In 2025, there was a total of 358 weather-related disasters worldwide affecting millions of people. For unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously aid these people, they need to be able to navigate around complex obstacles and reach their destinations to deliver supplies or quickly locate survivors in disaster-stricken areas. Our research addresses this need by providing a method to compute minimum-time trajectories in real time while providing guarantees of obstacle avoidance along the trajectory. Previous approaches treat the problem of computing time-optimal trajectories without considering obstacle avoidance. Our work not only includes obstacle avoidance constraints, but also improves overall mission time for navigation. We define mission time as the sum of planning time and traversal time. In time-sensitive emergencies, rapid trajectory planning is essential to ensure the prompt delivery of critical medical aid. As such, we consider the combined planning and traversal time as the key metric for how quickly the vehicle can reach a survivor. We show a sample mission in Fig. 1 where the quadrotor navigates through multiple canyon-like obstacles to deliver aid; during most of this trajectory, it is applying the near-maximum thrust.

</details>


### [43] [EasyNav: A Modular Architecture for Real-Time Navigation Across Heterogeneous Robot Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19894)
*Francisco Martín Rico,Francisco Miguel Moreno,Juan Sebastián Cely Gutiérrez,Esther Aguado,Miguel Ángel De Miguel Paraíso,José Miguel Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以显式共享导航状态为核心交互接口的模块化导航架构，并实现为开源ROS 2框架EasyNav，在真实机器人实验中相比Nav2显著降低反应延迟与资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航系统把建图、定位、规划与控制隐式绑定到特定表示和数据流，导致架构难以扩展、难以做实验对比、难以端到端分析时序；在多种空间表示、传感模态与坐标系并存且以不同频率演化的异构场景中尤为突出。

Method: 以显式、共享的导航状态作为各模块之间唯一的交互接口，将建图、定位、规划与控制约束在该共享状态上运行，从而支持可互换、与表示无关的流水线，并通过显式的兼容性与时序契约加以保障；系统实现为开源ROS 2框架EasyNav。

Result: 开展真实机器人实验，包括激光传感的室内导航，以及GPS与3D LiDAR结合、跨多层路线的大规模室内外导航。与条件匹配的Nav2相比，室内反应延迟由377 ms降至33 ms，室外由71 ms降至8 ms，同时CPU与内存占用更低。

Conclusion: 以共享导航状态为中心的架构能够有效解耦表示与数据流，提升可扩展性与时序可控性，并在真实异构场景中同时获得更低延迟和更低资源开销。

Abstract: Most robot navigation stacks implicitly bind mapping, localization, planning, and control to specific representations and data flows, yielding architectures hard to extend, compare experimentally, or reason about end-to-end timing. This is critical in heterogeneous scenarios, where multiple spatial representations, sensing modalities, and coordinate frames coexist and evolve at different rates. This paper presents a navigation architecture centered on an explicit, shared navigation state that serves as the sole interaction interface between modular components. Constraining mapping, localization, planning, and control to operate over this shared state enables interchangeable, representation-agnostic pipelines under explicit compatibility and timing contracts. We realize this architecture as EasyNav, an open source ROS 2 framework, and validate it through real-robot experiments, including indoor navigation with laser sensing and large-scale indoor-outdoor navigation with GPS and 3D LiDAR across multi-level routes. Compared with Nav2 under matched conditions, EasyNav reduces reaction latency from 377 to 33 ms indoors and from 71 to 8 ms outdoors, while also using less CPU and memory.

</details>


### [44] [DiffWAM: Learning World Action Model from Consecutive Visual Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19904)
*Xinheng Zhang,Haoyu Xiong,Chao Jing,Xiaohan Zhang,Linshan Li,Hongbang Fan,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffWAM：在World Action Model中加入"连续视觉差异预测"作为显式时序变化信号，用MoE结构分离World/Action/Diff三个专家，推理时可去掉World与Diff专家、零额外开销，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0与真机任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Model同时预测未来场景状态与动作，但未来状态中任务相关变化与大量任务无关内容（如静态背景）纠缠在一起，削弱了模型捕捉任务执行相关变化的能力。

Method: 提出DiffWAM，一个Mixture-of-Experts框架，联合学习未来状态预测与连续视觉差异预测；连续视觉差异定义为相邻观测隐表示之间的差异，作为时序变化的显式学习信号。框架包含World Expert（未来帧预测）、Action Expert（动作生成）、Diff Expert（轨迹上隐差异预测）；采用结构化单向注意力掩码，使三个专家仅以当前帧表示为条件、专家之间互不注意力。World与Diff专家可在推理阶段移除，不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在LIBERO与RoboTwin 2.0基准上达到SOTA；在真实世界操作任务上显著优于FastWAM。平均成功率分别为98.8%、92.65%、88.3%，相比SOTA基线FastWAM分别提升1.75、1.55和15.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 以显式视觉差异信号增强世界表示、并用MoE+单向注意力实现训练期增益与推理期零开销的结合，是提升机器人动作学习效果的有效方案，尤其在真机场景中提升幅度最大。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as an important paradigm in robot learning, jointly predicting future scene states and robot actions from current observations and instructions. However, future states entangle task-related changes with large amounts of task-irrelevant content, such as static backgrounds, which weakens the model's ability to capture changes relevant to task execution. To address this, we propose Difference World Action Model (DiffWAM), a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that enhances world representations by jointly learning future-state prediction and consecutive visual difference prediction. We define consecutive visual differences as differences between the latent representations of consecutive observations, providing an explicit learning signal for temporal variation. The framework comprises a World Expert for future frame prediction, an Action Expert for action generation, and a Diff Expert for predicting these latent differences along the trajectory. A structured unidirectional attention mask conditions the Diff, World, and Action Experts solely on the current-frame representation while blocking attention between experts. This design allows both the World and Diff Experts to be removed during inference, introducing no additional inference overhead. DiffWAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks and substantially improves over FastWAM on real-world manipulation tasks. Specifically, it attains average success rates of 98.8%, 92.65%, and 88.3%, outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline (i.e., FastWAM) by 1.75, 1.55, and 15.0 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [45] [AgenticJevNav: Agentic Object Search and Navigation via Event-Driven Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19912)
*Shuning Zhang,Shuli Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yihang Kang,Tao Wang,Xiangyu Chen,Yixiao Feng,Kai Sheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticJevNav：面向执行失败与推理延迟的智能体导航架构，通过目标主导规划、证据记忆、事件触发核验与有界执行四者联动，在6类仿真任务上达73.33%成功率（较最优Agent-only基线高21.67个百分点），并在真实四足机器人上取得70.0%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体导航需在证据不完整、执行失败及推理延迟的条件下保持目标并调整动作。现有方法往往缺乏对失败/阻塞的显式核验机制、目标所有权与动作授权的分离，以及过期决策的撤销管理，因而难以在长时程中稳定维持目标一致性。

Method: 提出AgenticJevNav，将目标导向规划、证据记忆、事件触发核验与有界执行相连接。Agent基于RGB、里程计、记忆与反馈进行规划，保留目标所有权但仅被授权有界动作集；Jev在执行阻塞/失败或连续两段低进展后审查结构化证据。采用版本、新鲜度与一次性检查约束决策应用；由单一执行器加独立RGB反射约束运动；目标修订时先撤销过期请求与动作再重新规划。

Result: 在六类仿真任务族、60次试验中达到73.33%成功率，在历史协议不同的条件下比次优的Agent-only聚合控制高21.67个百分点。在真实GENISOM L1四足机器人上部署，30次试验成功率70.0%，并包含有界搜索报告。

Conclusion: 仿真与实体机器人结果共同验证了该架构的有效性；剩余失败案例揭示了在地面定位（grounding）、接近规划与运行时效率方面的改进空间。

Abstract: Agentic navigation must maintain goals and adapt actions from incomplete evidence despite execution failures and delayed inference. We present AgenticJevNav, which links goal-directed planning, evidence memory, event-triggered verification, and bounded execution. The Agent plans from RGB, odometry, memory, and feedback, retaining goal ownership while authorizing a bounded action set. Jev reviews structured evidence after blocked/failed execution or two consecutive low-progress segments. Version, freshness, and once-only checks govern decision application. A single executor and independent RGB reflex constrain motion. Goal revisions revoke obsolete requests and actions before replanning. Across six simulated task families, AgenticJevNav achieves 73.33% SR over 60 trials, 21.67 percentage points above the next-best Agent-only aggregate control under differing historical protocols. A real-world GENISOM L1 quadruped deployment achieves 70.0% success over 30 trials, including bounded search reporting. Together, the simulation and physical-robot results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture, while remaining failures highlight opportunities to improve grounding, approach planning, and runtime.

</details>


### [46] [Lao Siji: Adapting openpilot to Closed-Loop End-to-End Driving in Neural Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19922)
*Chengzhi Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Jinchang Xu,Weichao Zhuang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lao Siji 通过微调开源 L2 量产模型 openpilot（冻结视觉编码器，加入自车状态适配器与路线记忆）并配合特权地图教师、车身范围损失与蒸馏，在 2026 NVIDIA AlpaSim 端到端驾驶挑战赛中排名第三，训练仅需 1 GPU 小时、权重偏移 3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数参赛方案依赖通用视觉基础模型从头训练，成本高且效果不一定占优；作者希望验证从量产级开源驾驶模型出发做轻量适配，能否以极低算力取得有竞争力的闭环驾驶表现，并弄清哪些训练信号真正带来增益。

Method: 以 openpilot 为基座：冻结视觉编码器，仅微调策略层，并加入自车状态适配器与路线记忆；在神经重建场景的闭环 rollout 中，用特权地图教师监督横向自校正，用车身范围损失约束道路与障碍物间距，同时蒸馏基座模型其余输出以保留跟车行为；纵向安全依赖基座前车估计加规则化速度层。并做了消融对比（更长训练、模仿人类日志、人类日志速度目标）。

Result: AlpaSim 挑战赛第三名，优于多数基于基础模型的参赛方案；消融显示自校正样本贡献主要增益，无教师时车身范围损失可替代其作用，而更长训练、模仿人类日志与人类日志速度目标无收益甚至有害；未经额外训练即在 HUGSIM 与 Odyssey 上进入前列；最终训练仅耗 1 GPU 小时，权重相对发布模型仅偏移 3.9%。

Conclusion: 从量产级开源驾驶模型做最小化适配，比从头训练基础模型更具性价比；闭环性能的关键在于特权教师驱动的自校正与几何间隙约束，而非更多数据或模仿人类速度；该方法具备良好的跨仿真器泛化性，代码将在评审后开源。

Abstract: We present Lao Siji, an end-to-end driving policy that ranks third in the 2026 NVIDIA AlpaSim E2E Driving Challenge, ahead of most entries built on foundation models. Instead of training from scratch on a general-purpose vision backbone, we adapt openpilot, an open-source L2 production model. We freeze its vision encoder and fine-tune the policy layers with an ego-state adapter and a route memory. On closed-loop rollouts in neural-reconstructed scenes, we supervise the policy with a privileged map teacher for lateral self-correction and a body-extent loss for road and obstacle clearance, while distilling the base model's remaining outputs to retain car following. Ablations show that self-correction samples carry the gain, while the clearance loss substitutes for them where no teacher exists. Longitudinal safety instead rests on the base model's lead-vehicle estimate behind a rule-based speed layer. Longer training, imitating human logs, and human-log speed targets yield either no measurable gain or adverse effects. Without further training, Lao Siji also places among the leaders on HUGSIM and Odyssey. The final training takes only one GPU-hour and shifts the weights by 3.9% from the shipped model. Code will be released after review.

</details>


### [47] [From Companion to Guardian: Designing an Emergency Support System Using a Quadruped Robot for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19932)
*Chunyang Meng,Eduardo B. Sandoval,Josh Andres,Francisco Cruz,Mari Velonaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一套基于Unitree Go1四足平台的居家陪伴机器人系统，将日常陪伴与紧急感知响应结合，采用"陪伴—危险感知—紧急"三阶段交互模型和基于规则的升级策略，并通过105次自动化试验与25次实景试验验证了决策规则与升级时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独居老年人增多使紧急支持成为居家陪伴机器人的重要功能；同时照护人力需求预计在2020-21至2049-50年间近乎翻倍，机器人狗等服务平台可在无人类即时支持时兼顾陪伴与求助。

Method: 在Unitree Go1四足机器人上构建居家陪伴系统：设计陪伴、危险感知、紧急三阶段交互模型；采用基于规则的升级策略，融合视觉与语音输入检测到的迹象判定风险等级，并动态调整机器人等待用户确认安全的时间；支持用户确认安全、请求立即帮助、停止运动与系统重置。

Result: 通过105次自动化试验和25次分阶段物理实景试验验证了决策规则与升级时序；额外视觉证据可缩短等待时间，若等待结束时未获确认，系统进入紧急模式。

Conclusion: 该三阶段、可解释的规则式升级设计可在四足陪伴机器人上实现陪伴与紧急响应的统一，验证结果支持其在实际居家场景中的可行性，后续需更大规模与真实用户验证。

Abstract: As older adults increasingly live alone, emergency support becomes an important function for home companion robots. In Australia, demand for the care and support workforce is projected to nearly double between 2020-21 and 2049-50. Service robots such as robot dogs may support older adults by combining everyday companionship with assistance when human support is not immediately available. We present the design and implementation of a domestic companion-robot system on a Unitree Go1 quadruped platform that combines companionship with emergency awareness and response. The system uses a three-stage interaction model consisting of companion, danger-awareness, and emergency modes. A rule-based escalation policy combines signs detected from visual and speech input to determine the current risk level and adjust how long the robot waits for the user to confirm safety before entering emergency mode. Additional detected signs can shorten this waiting time. Users can confirm safety, request immediate help, stop robot motion, or reset the system. We verified the implemented decision rules and escalation timing through 105 automated trials and 25 staged physical trials. Additional visual evidence shortened the waiting time, and the system entered emergency mode when the waiting time ended without confirmation.

</details>


### [48] [CRAFT: A Robot-Agent Harness with Scope-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19952)
*Peng Zhang,Jiawen Wang,Cheng Xue,Jochen Renz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出CRAFT框架，在冻结机器人技能的前提下学习局部放置要求；通过"范围阶梯"结合记忆、显式规则与学习器，权衡修正的适用范围。仿真显示该方法在效用上优于纯纠错记忆，提问更少，但对要求变化的可靠适应仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人虽能移动物体，却需要学习物体"该放在哪里"；当人类拒绝某次放置时，机器人必须判断该纠正是仅适用于当前情境，还是表达了一条更广泛的规则。现有做法若为每个新要求重新训练技能代价高，且难以区分局部修正与通用规则。

Method: 提出CRAFT（Contextual Requirement Adaptation with Frozen Tools）框架：将物理执行成功与"是否满足要求"分开记录；构建范围阶梯（scope ladder）——若近乎相同情境下已有判定则直接用记忆，否则融合记忆、显式规则和针对规则之外要求的学习器，并按各来源对既往反馈的预测准确度加权其输出。评估使用效用指标（奖励可接受动作，惩罚错误与提问），并保留全部执行失败与超时。

Result: 受控实验显示，在效用指标上优于仅使用纠错记忆的基线。在相机接地的机器人仿真中，范围阶梯比具备持久记忆的语言模型智能体提问更少，但部分此类智能体错误动作更少、在要求变化后成功率更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持将具体记忆与更广泛的规则相结合，但对不断变化要求的可靠适应仍未解决。所有证据均来自使用生成要求和模拟反馈的仿真环境，尚需真实世界验证。

Abstract: A robot may know how to move an object yet still need to learn where it belongs. When a person rejects a placement, the robot must decide whether the correction applies only to that situation or expresses a broader rule. We study this problem in CRAFT (Contextual Requirement Adaptation with Frozen Tools), a framework for learning local requirements without retraining robot skills. It records physical success separately from whether an action meets the requirement. Our scope ladder uses memory when an action has already been judged in a nearly identical situation. Elsewhere, it combines memory, explicit rules and a learner for requirements beyond those rules, weighting their predictions by how well they predicted earlier feedback. Controlled studies show gains over correction memory alone in utility, which rewards acceptable actions and charges for mistakes and questions. In camera-grounded robot simulations, the ladder asks fewer questions than language-model agents with persistent memory, but some of those agents take fewer wrong actions and succeed more often after requirements change. All execution failures and timeouts remain in the evaluation. These results support combining specific memories with broader rules, while leaving reliable adaptation to changing requirements unresolved. All evidence comes from simulation with generated requirements and simulated feedback.

</details>


### [49] [Value and Policy Calibration from Execution Feedback for Socially Compatible Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19969)
*Sen Shen,Ning Liu,Xiangrui Kong,Yingwei Li,Dongkun Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种世界模型强化学习框架，将想象式策略改进与基于真实交互的价值/策略校准相结合，在无信号交叉口实现100%通行成功率，并显著降低跟驰人类驾驶车辆的加速度波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在交叉口的决策会影响周围人类驾驶车辆的延误、制动与运动平顺性；而学习"社会兼容"行为需要把想象交互结果与真实执行动作的反馈联系起来，现有世界模型方法缺乏这种从最终交互中校准价值与策略的机制。

Method: 构建交互感知的潜空间世界模型以支持想象推演；采用离策略校正与回放更新，使观测到的奖励与状态转移同时监督critic与actor，从而在想象策略改进之外实现价值校准与策略校准的双重机制，平衡AV通行效率与HDV交互负担。

Result: 在无信号交叉口仿真中达到100%成功率，相较PIWM与DreamerV3，后车HDV加速度变化分别降低65.5%和44.0%；消融实验表明价值校准主要降低HDV负担、策略校准主要提升通行效率；在分布外交通变化测试下，同等通行时间下自车jerk与HDV加速度变化均低于PIWM。

Conclusion: 将想象式规划与真实交互驱动的价值/策略校准相结合，可有效提升自动驾驶决策的社会兼容性与鲁棒性，两类校准机制具有互补作用。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicle (AV) decisions at intersections affect the delay, braking, and motion smoothness of surrounding human-driven vehicles (HDVs). Learning socially compatible behavior requires connecting imagined interaction outcomes with feedback from executed actions. We propose a world-model reinforcement learning framework combines imagined policy improvement with direct value and policy calibration from finalized interactions, balancing AV progress with HDV interaction burden. An interaction-aware latent model supports imagination, while off-policy correction and replay updates enable observed rewards and transitions to supervise both the critic and actor. Simulation experiments at an unsignalized intersection achieve a 100% success and reductions in following-HDV acceleration variation of 65.5% and 44.0% relative to PIWM and DreamerV3, respectively. Ablations identify complementary roles for value calibration in reducing HDV burden and policy calibration in improving passage efficiency. Tests under out-of-distribution traffic shifts further show lower ego jerk and HDV acceleration variation than PIWM at comparable passage times.

</details>


### [50] [Practice Is Not Free: Measuring the Embodied Cost of Reset-Free Robot Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19979)
*Vedant Telrandhe,Sushil Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源低成本的机器人自主练习测试平台（自建 SO-101 机械臂 + 两个互相重置的 ACT 策略 + 视觉成功检测 + 成本账本），并形式化"成本感知的练习调度"问题：以扣除实体成本后的能力增益作为任务选择奖励；硬件上 51 分钟的初步实验表明固定重试上限并不能降低失败导致的执行时间损失（36% vs 27%，不显著）且未节省机器人时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人无人工重置的自主练习中，每次尝试都消耗真实时间、执行器磨损甚至人力，但现有调度器把尝试视为"免费"资源，缺少对实体成本的度量与建模手段。

Method: 搭建低成本的 SO-101 机械臂测试台，运行两个由示教训练的 ACT 策略互相重置；用视觉成功检测器判定成败，用账本记录每次尝试的耗时、关节行程和人工干预；提出成本感知的练习调度框架（奖励 = 实测能力增益 − 实体成本）并预先注册对比实验；在硬件上完成前两个条件的 51 分钟实验，并对日志做离线重放分析。

Result: 固定重试上限未减少失败所占执行时间比例（36% vs 可行性规则的 27%，差异不显著），也未节省机器人时间；日志显示失败无聚集迹象，失败后立即重试的成功率与其他尝试无异；重试规则额外的人力时间主要来自方块被打出可达范围而非规则本身；成本感知规则在日志重放中无需手工阈值即恢复出合理选择。此外报告构建经验，包括覆盖效应对应的现象：把示教集中在更小工作空间后，该区域成功率由 8/20 提升到 10/10。

Conclusion: 实体成本可以被显式测量并纳入练习调度，"失败即重试"这类直觉规则缺乏实证支持；成本账本式基础设施为成本感知调度提供了可行且低成本的验证途径，并在日志重放中显示出替代手工阈值的潜力。

Abstract: When a robot practices without human resets, every attempt still costs wall-clock time, actuator wear, and sometimes a person's attention, yet the schedulers that decide what to practice next treat attempts as free. We present an open, low-cost testbed for measuring these costs: a self-built SO-101 arm runs two demonstration-trained ACT policies that reset each other, with a vision-based success detector and a ledger that records the time, joint travel, and human interventions of every attempt. We formulate cost-aware practice scheduling, in which task selection is rewarded by measured competence gain net of embodied cost, and specify in advance the comparison that will test it. Its first two conditions, run on hardware for 51 minutes of robot time, show that a fixed retry limit did not reduce the share of execution time lost to failures (36%, against 27% for a feasibility rule; not significant) and saved no robot time. The logs suggest why: failures showed no sign of clustering, so a retry right after a failure was as likely to succeed as any other attempt. The ledger also shows that most of the retry rule's extra human time came from cubes knocked out of reach rather than from the rule itself, and a cost-aware rule replayed on the logs recovers the sensible choice without a hand-set limit. We also report lessons from building the system, including a coverage effect in which concentrating demonstrations in a smaller workspace raised success there from 8/20 to 10/10.

</details>


### [51] [MoViC: Real-Time Humanoid Whole-Body Control from Monocular Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19998)
*Yimin Han,Jiahui Zhang,Wanyue Li,Zeren Luo,Yeke Chen,Liu Zhao,Peizhuo Li,Xiaoyu Ji,Shihao Dong,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoViC 是一个无需穿戴设备的框架，以实时单目 RGB 视频作为人形机器人的控制信号，结合单目人体运动重建、机器人重定向和基于 Transformer 的行为基础模型，实现全身实时模仿与无设备遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全身模仿需要同时具备鲁棒的通用跟踪器与高质量实时重建，而现有方案通常依赖穿戴式跟踪设备，限制了部署便利性；同时视觉估计频率（10 Hz）与控制器频率（50 Hz）不匹配，且堆叠未来参考帧会使输入维度与网络规模过大。

Method: 框架包含三部分：单目人体运动重建、机器人重定向、基于 Transformer 的行为基础模型策略。策略输入为自身本体感知历史、单个显式的下一步参考，以及紧凑的频域运动表示，用频域表示提供时序上下文而无需显式堆叠未来参考帧，从而降低输入维度与网络规模；部署时提出外推方法桥接 10 Hz 视觉估计与 50 Hz 控制器，实现视觉处理与策略执行的异步运行。

Result: 硬件实验表明，系统可从实时单目 RGB 输入实现高动态运动跟踪，并完成双臂与单臂操作任务的无设备遥操作。

Conclusion: MoViC 证明仅凭单目 RGB 视频即可驱动人形机器人进行实时全身模仿与遥操作，频域运动表示与频率外推机制是兼顾实时性与模型效率的关键设计。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body imitation from monocular video requires both a robust universal tracker and high-quality real-time reconstruction. We present MoViC, a framework that uses live RGB video as a control signal for humanoid robots without wearable tracking devices. The framework combines monocular human motion reconstruction, robot retargeting, and a transformer-based behavior foundation model. The policy receives proprioceptive history, a single explicit next-step reference, and a compact frequency-domain motion representation that supplies temporal context without explicitly stacking future reference frames, which reduces the input dimension and network size. During deployment, our extrapolation method bridges the 10-Hz visual estimation with 50-Hz controller, enabling asynchronous visual processing and policy execution. Hardware experiments further demonstrate highly dynamic motion tracking and device-free teleoperation of bimanual and single-arm manipulation tasks from live monocular RGB input. Project page: https://movicproject.github.io/ .

</details>


### [52] [PlanDiv: Test-Time Scaling with Latent Plans for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20010)
*Hanbo Zhang,Lianlei Shan,Liuhaichen Yang,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlanDiv通过让候选动作在"计划"层面而非噪声层面产生差异，使测试时扩展在VLA策略上持续有效，CALVIN五任务链成功率从56.9%提升至69.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（采样多个动作候选并执行最优者）对VLA策略有效，但当候选来自同一解码器时收益快速衰减：候选之间只是噪声不同而非计划不同。在CALVIN上，三种重采样源从4个候选增加到8个仅带来0.06-0.07的平均完成任务数提升，说明单纯增加采样数量无法获得有效多样性。

Method: 提出PlanDiv动作头：由轻量规划器提出K个潜在计划假设；共享的掩码扩散执行器在单次骨干网络前向中为每个计划解码一个动作块；策略依据自身似然选择排名最高的动作块执行。计划仅从示教数据中学习，无需计划标签或奖励信号。

Result: 在骨干、数据和训练配方固定的条件下，8个计划条件候选将CALVIN ABC→D五任务链成功率从56.9%提升至69.9%，而自回归、无计划或同计划重采样的8个候选最高仅64.9%（同计划基线即为PlanDiv自身检查点但固定计划，故差距确实来自计划而非采样量）。LIBERO-Long上4个计划消除40%的单候选失败，匹配重采样最多21%。真实Franka机械臂上，单解任务与单输出OpenVLA-OFT+基线持平，双解任务54次试验成功51次（基线40次）。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的瓶颈在于候选多样性来源而非采样数量；通过显式引入潜在计划假设，可使候选在策略层面真正分化，从而在仿真与真实机器人任务上稳定获得增益，且增益集中于存在多解的任务。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies by drawing several action candidates and executing the best one. We find that its returns diminish quickly when the candidates are resampled from one decoder: they differ by noise rather than by plan, and for each of three resampling sources, going from four to eight candidates adds only 0.06-0.07 to the average number of completed tasks on CALVIN. We introduce PlanDiv, an action head that makes the candidates differ by plan: a lightweight planner proposes K latent plan hypotheses, a shared masked-diffusion executor decodes one action chunk per plan from a single backbone pass, and the policy executes the chunk that its own likelihood ranks highest. The plans are learned from demonstrations alone, without plan labels or rewards. With the backbone, data and training recipe held fixed, eight plan-conditioned candidates raise five-task chain success on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D from 56.9% to 69.9%, while eight candidates from autoregressive, plan-free or same-plan resampling reach at most 64.9%. The same-plan baseline is PlanDiv's own checkpoint with the plan held fixed, so this gap comes from the plans, not from drawing more samples. On LIBERO-Long, four plans remove 40% of single-candidate failures, against at most 21% for matched resampling. On a real Franka arm the gain appears where a task admits more than one solution: PlanDiv is on par with a single-output OpenVLA-OFT+ baseline on a one-solution task and succeeds in 51 of 54 trials against 40 of 54 on two tasks with two solutions.

</details>


### [53] [Prompt Re-Specification and Target-Mask Availability in Mask-Conditioned Visual Reinforcement Learning for Moving-Platform Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20075)
*Ho Tim Tsui,Jongyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用一个冻结权重的掩码输入DrQ-v2策略，只改动SAM 3的文本提示，就可在仿真中把无人机在"香蕉主题"新降落平台上的着陆成功率从12.0%恢复到68.4%；结果表明"掩码能否送达策略"是该类方法的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉强化学习的无人机在移动平台上着陆策略，当降落平台外观与训练时不同就会失效。作者希望去掉像素级外观信息、只保留目标的二值空间掩码，并用文本提示指定目标，从而提升跨外观的泛化性。

Method: 仿真实验：以二值目标掩码为视觉输入，采用冻结（固定权重）的、经微调的SAM 3检测器生成掩码，DrQ-v2策略本身不做任何权重更新，仅更换文本提示。另设独立实验，在策略用真值掩码训练的情况下，比较补全"平台可见期间缺失的掩码"与掩码质心修正、面积修正两种方案的效果；并测试跨时间跟踪平台以获取更多掩码。

Result: 香蕉主题平台使着陆成功率由75.6%降至12.0%，原提示下SAM 3极少检测到该平台；更换提示后（在若干候选中挑选）成功率恢复至68.4%，且无需任何权重更新。在真值掩码策略实验中，补全缺失掩码对成功率的提升远大于掩码质心修正与面积修正。跨时间跟踪能更频繁地提供掩码，甚至在平台被确认不在渲染图像中之后仍持续输出掩码，但未能建立可靠的着陆成功率提升。

Conclusion: 决定该方法成败的关键因素可能在于"掩码是否真的被送入策略"；在保持策略冻结的前提下，仅靠调整提示词即可显著恢复泛化性能，而单纯的掩码几何修正与跟踪收益有限。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning (RL) policies that land a drone on a moving platform can fail when the landing pad looks different from the one used in training. Using binary target masks as the visual input removes most pixel-level appearance information while preserving the spatial representation of the target, and lets a text prompt specify the target. In simulation, we test whether a frozen (fixed-weight) mask-based DrQ-v2 policy can handle a new pad when only the prompt of its fine-tuned SAM 3 detector changes. A banana-themed pad lowered landing success from 75.6% to 12.0%, and SAM 3 rarely detected it under the original prompt. A revised prompt, chosen after testing several candidates, restored success to 68.4% with no weight updates. In a separate experiment with a policy trained on ground-truth masks, filling in masks that were missing while the pad was visible helped landing far more than the tested mask-centroid and area corrections. Tracking the pad over time supplied masks more often and also kept producing them after the pad was confirmed absent from the rendered image, but it did not establish a reliable improvement in landing success. Whether a usable mask reaches the policy at all thus appears to be an important limiting factor for this approach.

</details>


### [54] [EgoSwarm: Learning Decentralized Robot Swarm Intelligence from Human Swarm Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20100)
*Ziqiang Liu,Xitong Chen,Min Li,Yiqun Li,Zhuoping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoSwarm 是首个从人类群体示范中学习去中心化多机器人协作的机器人学习框架，通过 SIP/SIT 从分布式观测恢复跨智能体交互关系并转化为局部监督，再用 SwarmVLA 迁移为去中心化策略，在三类多机器人协作任务上平均成功率达 72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单体具身智能迈向群体具身智能是实现可扩展通用具身智能的关键一步，复杂任务常需多智能体分工、交接与协同操作共享物体。但现有机器人学习方法主要依赖个体示范，对智能体动作间依赖关系的监督信号有限，而大规模采集多机器人示范又十分困难。人类第一人称示范提供了可扩展的替代方案，然而多参与者的分布式观测无法直接监督去中心化策略。

Method: 该工作构建了无需机器人的多人第一人称数据采集系统，收集超过50小时协作经验；提出群体交互数据包（SIP）与群体交互令牌（SIT），从分布式观测中恢复跨智能体交互关系，并将其转化为与部署时信息约束一致的局部监督；进一步提出 SwarmVLA，将群体行为知识迁移到以局部观测、局部历史与有限邻居消息为条件的去中心化策略。

Result: SwarmVLA 在三类多机器人协作任务上取得 72.2% 的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 人类群体第一人称示范可作为可扩展监督来源，配合 SIP/SIT 的交互关系恢复与局部监督构造，能够有效训练去中心化多机器人协作策略，为实现群体具身智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Advancing from individual embodied intelligence to swarm embodied intelligence is a key step toward scalable, general-purpose embodied intelligence. Complex tasks often require multiple agents to divide responsibilities, coordinate handovers, and manipulate shared objects collaboratively. However, existing robot learning methods rely primarily on individual demonstrations, which provide limited supervision for modeling dependencies among agent actions, while collecting multi-robot demonstrations at scale remains difficult. Human egocentric demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, yet distributed observations from multiple participants cannot directly supervise decentralized policies. We introduce EgoSwarm, the first robot learning framework for learning decentralized multi-robot collaboration from human swarm demonstrations. EgoSwarm collects more than 50 hours of collaborative experience using a robot-free multi-person egocentric system. We introduce the Swarm Interaction Packet (SIP) and Swarm Interaction Token (SIT) to recover cross-agent interaction relations from distributed observations and transform this experience into local supervision consistent with deployment-time information constraints. We further propose SwarmVLA, which transfers knowledge of swarm behavior to decentralized policies conditioned on local observations, local history, and limited neighbor messages. SwarmVLA achieves a mean success rate of 72.2% across three categories of multi-robot collaborative tasks. Project page:https://xitong-c.github.io/EgoSwarm_Page/.

</details>


### [55] [SAFER: Navigating the Exploration-Exploitation Dilemma in World-Model-Based Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20107)
*Yansong Qu,Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen,Samuel Labi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFER：离线学习他人的失败（专家标注），在线持续精炼世界模型，分块潜空间世界模型实时预测后果，风险感知采样规划器以回报减碰撞惩罚评估候选动作，在CARLA与MetaDrive上取得最高路线完成率与奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要预判行为后果，尤其在高风险的罕见场景。世界模型只能在训练数据覆盖范围内可靠，形成探索—利用困境：覆盖内规划难以超越数据，覆盖外模型误差会误导规划。而正常驾驶日志缺少失败数据，交互学习又只能靠自身失败获取且得不到正确示范。

Method: SAFER框架分两部分。离线阶段：用部分训练的智能体与脚本化可恢复扰动在仿真中制造失败，由专家为每个访问状态标注动作与价值，使规划探索区域在行驶前被覆盖；规划时用分块潜空间世界模型快速预测后果，并用风险感知采样规划器以回报减有界碰撞惩罚评估候选机动。在线阶段：持续在行驶超出离线数据覆盖处精炼模型，形成闭环。

Result: 闭环对比实验中，SAFER在CARLA Leaderboard路线上取得最高路线完成率0.95与最高每步奖励0.77（特权鸟瞰视角）；在MetaDrive中取得最高回合奖励，常规场景53.4，安全关键交通场景49.0。

Conclusion: 通过离线借鉴他人失败（含专家标注）与在线自我精炼相结合，SAFER缓解了世界模型的探索—利用困境，实现了实时风险感知规划，并在多个仿真基准上优于对比方法。

Abstract: An autonomous vehicle must foresee the consequences of its actions, especially in the rare, time-critical situations where crashes happen. A world model offers this foresight, but only within the coverage of its training data, which poses an exploration-exploitation dilemma. Within this coverage a planner can hardly improve on the data, and beyond it the model's errors mislead it. Logs of competent driving rarely contain the failures that would widen the coverage, and agents that learn from interaction gain them only by failing themselves, without being shown what to do instead. We propose SAFER, a framework that learns offline from failures that others make, judged by an expert, and keeps learning online from its own driving. Offline, partially trained agents and scripted, recoverable perturbations Stage failures in simulation, and an expert Annotates every visited state with its action and value, so that much of what the planner explores is covered before it drives. In planning, a chunked latent world model Foresees the consequences fast enough for real-time use, and a risk-aware sampling-based planner Evaluates candidate maneuvers by their return minus a bounded collision penalty. Online, a loop continuously Refines the model wherever this driving leaves the coverage of the offline data. In closed-loop evaluation against the compared methods, SAFER reaches the highest route completion and reward per step on the CARLA Leaderboard routes, 0.95 and 0.77 from a privileged bird's-eye view, and the highest episode reward in MetaDrive, 53.4 in regular and 49.0 in safety-critical traffic. Project page: https://ys-qu.github.io/safer-website.

</details>


### [56] [Cleanly-DINO: Efficiently Learning Visual Task Spaces for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20137)
*Adrian Röfer,Melika Shekarriz,José Arce y de la Borbolla,Iman Nematollahi,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Cleanly-DINO，利用掩码与冻结ViT特征拟合投影以构建视觉任务空间，并用进展度量刻画清洁任务的完成度，在多样清洁样本上训练，评估其不完整区域定位与跨视觉域迁移能力，并用于RL的观测编码与奖励信号及真实机器人擦拭流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量示范学习操作任务需要能捕捉任务完成相关变化的抽象表示，如刚体操作中的物体位姿；但清洁等任务由视觉状态变化而非物体位姿定义，任务表示的选择并不明确。

Method: 给定任务相关区域的掩码和冻结的ViT特征，Cleanly-DINO拟合一个投影以分离出任务空间，并在该空间上拟合进展度量；模型在多样化清洁示例上训练。

Result: 评估了模型定位未完成清洁区域以及在视觉域间迁移的能力；通过将其用作强化学习中的观测编码器和奖励信号研究其用于机器人学习的效用；并在真实机器人擦拭流程中部署该模型以评估其引导任务执行的效果。

Conclusion: Cleanly-DINO可从少量示范中学习视觉任务空间与进展度量，具备区域定位与跨域迁移能力，并能作为RL观测编码器与奖励信号，且可部署于真实机器人擦拭流程中引导任务执行。

Abstract: Learning manipulation tasks from few demonstrations requires abstractions that capture the changes relevant to task completion, such as object poses in rigid-body manipulation. However, tasks such as cleaning are defined by changes in visual state rather than object pose, making the choice of task representation less clear. In this work, we propose Cleanly-DINO to learn visual task spaces from few demonstrations. Given a mask of a task-relevant region and frozen ViT features, Cleanly-DINO fits a projection to isolate a task space and fit a progression metric to it. We train the model on diverse cleaning examples and evaluate its ability to localize incomplete task regions and transfer across visual domains. We also study its utility for robot learning by using it as an observation encoder and reward signal in a reinforcement learning setting. Finally, we deploy our fitted model within a real-world robotic wiping pipeline to evaluate its use in guiding task execution.

</details>


### [57] [RoboKit: Accelerating Parallel Differentiable Optimization for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20185)
*Jianglong Ye,Xuanbin Peng,Conor Mc Gartoll,Changwei Jing,Lai Wei,Jiali Chen,Xueyan Zou,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboKit 是一个面向机器人并行可微优化的高性能通用工具包，遵循性能与灵活性、几何-渲染-物理、与学习集成三大原则，在七个任务上比专用基线快 2-60 倍，并可将优化蒸馏为策略、支持强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域缺乏兼顾性能与灵活性的通用可微优化工具：现有方法多为任务专用、串行或缺乏与学习的衔接，难以同时支持多解并行搜索、几何/渲染/物理计算以及与策略学习和强化学习的一体化流程。

Method: 工具包基于三个设计原则构建：(i) GPU 并行多种子求解器，将采样与多种优化器组合；(ii) 采用混合式与由粗到细的几何表示，加速碰撞检测、可微渲染与仿真；(iii) 保持代价可微、仿真并行，从而支持将优化蒸馏为策略并训练强化学习。

Result: 在七个任务上比任务专用基线快 2-60 倍；可在 2.97 秒内优化 4096 个抓取；蒸馏得到的策略在精度相当的情况下快 9-240 倍；在同一仿真器上，强化学习达到 GPU 基线分数所需时间最多缩短至 2.1 倍。

Conclusion: RoboKit 表明，将 GPU 并行可微优化、高效几何/渲染/物理表示与学习流程统一集成，可以同时获得显著的加速与可比的精度，为机器人优化与学习提供通用基础设施。

Abstract: We present RoboKit, a high-performance, general-purpose toolkit for parallel differentiable optimization in robotics, built on three principles: (i) Performance and flexibility: GPU-parallel multi-seed solvers pair sampling with versatile optimizers, running 2-60$\times$ faster than task-specific baselines across seven tasks; (ii) Geometry, rendering and physics: hybrid and coarse-to-fine representations accelerate collision, differentiable rendering and simulation, optimizing 4096 grasps in 2.97 s; (iii) Integration with learning: costs are differentiable and simulation is parallel, so RoboKit both distills optimization into policies and trains RL; distilled policies run 9-240$\times$ faster at comparable accuracy, and RL reaches a GPU baseline's scores up to 2.1$\times$ sooner on the same simulator.

</details>


### [58] [DAPPT: Domain-Aware Policies from Procedural Trajectories for Sim-to-Real Dexterous Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20202)
*Beining Han,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPPT，一个无需模拟非刚体材料的sim-to-real框架，用于端到端灵巧工具使用操作技能（如铲沙、揉面团滚棒），通过对过程轨迹的参数化追踪学习域感知RL策略并蒸馏为端到端传感器运动策略，在六个任务上实现1.5-3.8倍指标提升并零样本迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim-to-real强化学习在灵巧操作上成果显著，但局限于具备精确刚体仿真的任务；涉及可变形体和颗粒的技能仿真过慢，难以支撑大规模RL学习，因此需要一种不依赖非刚体仿真的方案。

Method: 1）构建DAPPT框架，回避对非刚体材料的仿真；2）通过追踪由域参数参数化的过程轨迹，学习域感知的RL策略；3）将该策略蒸馏为端到端的域感知传感器运动策略；4）利用显式域表示快速选择能最大化下游任务性能的域参数。

Result: 在六个灵巧操作任务（铲沙、面团滚棒、刮沙、鼠标滚轮滚动、分配器按压、喷雾触发）上进行端到端评估，通过最优域参数取得1.5-3.8倍的任务指标提升，并实现零样本迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: DAPPT表明，通过域参数化过程轨迹与策略蒸馏，可以在不仿真可变形体与颗粒的前提下获得可用的灵巧工具使用技能，并高效地通过域参数选择提升真实任务表现，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real RL has achieved impressive results in dexterous manipulation but remains limited to tasks with accurate rigid-body simulation. However, these methods cannot be applied to skills that involve deformable and particles, with which the simulation speed will become too slow for large-scale RL learning. We present DAPPT, a sim-to-real framework for end-to-end dexterous tool-use manipulation skills, e.g., sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, without simulating non-rigid materials. Our method learns a domain-aware RL policy by tracking procedural trajectories parameterized by domain parameters, and distills it into an end-to-end domain-aware sensorimotor policy. Furthermore, explicit domain represen-tation enables fast selection of domain parameters that can maximize downstream task performance. We evaluate DAPPT end-to-end on six dexterous manipulation tasks, including sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, sand scraping, mouse-wheel scrolling, dispenser pressing, and spray triggering. Our method achieves 1.5-3.8x in task metrics through the best domain parameter and transfers zero-shot to the real world.

</details>


### [59] [Right Goal, Wrong Body: Re-grounding Frozen VLAs under Actuation Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20244)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReGround：一种推理期方法，在不重新训练VLA的前提下应对部署时执行机构变化（重装、磨损、指令延迟），通过任务无关的电机探索学习部署机器人动力学，并在检测到偏移时跟踪策略意图运动、保持贴近原策略规划，恢复接近最优的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在理想条件下能产生期望的机器人行为，但部署时执行机构发生变化（如重新安装、机械磨损、指令延迟变化）常导致任务失败和不安全行为。这类执行偏移不可避免，而重新训练成本高且缺乏奖励与示范数据。

Method: ReGround保持VLA冻结，通过任务无关的motor babbling学习部署机器人的动力学；再跟踪策略的意图运动，同时约束其贴近策略自身的规划；仅在检测到偏移时激活，无需奖励、示范或重训练。

Result: 在两个VLA模型、八个LIBERO任务和五种偏移设置下验证，ReGround可恢复接近oracle的成功率，例如在400 ms延迟下将π_{0.5}的成功率从0.16提升到0.90，且比线性校准基线更安全。

Conclusion: ReGround是一种无需重训、即插即用的推理期重定位方案，能有效应对部署时执行机构偏移，兼顾成功率和安全性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are capable of producing desired robotic behaviors in ideal settings. However, they often fail when the robot's actuation changes at deployment. For example, remounting, mechanical wear, or command latency changes can lead to failed tasks and unsafe behaviors. To address the inevitable actuation shift, we propose ReGround, an inference-time method that keeps the VLA frozen and re-grounds its intended motion. ReGround learns the deployed robot's dynamics from task-agnostic motor babbling, and then tracks the policy's intended motion while staying close to the policy's own plan. ReGround activates only upon detecting a shift, and needs no rewards, demonstrations, or retraining. We show via two VLAs, eight LIBERO tasks, and five shift settings that ReGround can restore near-oracle success, such as raising $π_{0.5}$'s success from 0.16 to 0.90 under 400 ms latency, while remaining safer than a linear-calibration baseline. Project website and open-source code: https://umersiddique94.github.io/ReGround/.

</details>


### [60] [DexFormer 2.0: Towards a Cross-Embodiment Foundation Policy for Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20273)
*Lixin Xu,Ke Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Jie Tan,Senming Tan,Jiaju Yin,Yating Feng,Xiaoyi Lin,Xiyue Dong,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexFormer 2.0 是一个单一强化学习策略，可跨本体控制多种手臂-手组合，在未见配对与新手型上实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取策略通常针对单一“手臂+手”配对训练，每增加一种配对就需重新训练，策略数量随手臂与手的数量组合而膨胀。跨本体训练困难之处在于不同机器人的关节数量、布局与动力学各异，其观测与动作缺乏公共接口。

Method: 将每个机器人映射到带有效性掩码的规范关节接口；用受运动学树偏置的注意力编码各关节形态；用 Transformer-XL 记忆从近期交互中推断与本体相关的动力学。训练集为 4 种手臂×6 种手共 18 种组合，并与 GRU、DexFormer 1.0 基线对比，开展消融实验及真机蒸馏部署。

Result: 在已见组合上达到 89% 稳定成功率，在 6 组未见手臂-手配对上有 51%，优于 GRU 与 DexFormer 1.0 基线；无需微调即可抓取 4 种新手型，并可通过重定向用于平行夹爪；在实体 Flexiv 手臂上，蒸馏学生策略从仿真零样本迁移到两种手（含一组未见配对）。

Conclusion: 单一策略可同时控制多种手臂-手组合并泛化到未见配对与新手型；消融表明记忆模块、运动学树偏置与更广的配对覆盖均对迁移有贡献。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping policies are usually trained for one arm paired with one hand. Every new pairing then needs its own policy, and the number of policies grows with every arm and hand added. We ask whether a single policy can control many arm-hand combinations and transfer to pairings and hands it has never seen. This is hard because robots differ in the number, layout, and dynamics of their joints, so their observations and actions share no common interface. We present DexFormer 2.0, a single reinforcement learning policy for cross-embodiment dexterous grasping. It maps every robot to a canonical joint interface with validity masks, encodes each joint's morphology with attention biased by the kinematic tree, and uses a Transformer-XL memory to infer embodiment-dependent dynamics from recent interaction. Trained on 18 combinations of four arms and six hands, it reaches 89% stable success on seen combinations and 51% on six held-out arm-hand pairs, outperforming GRU and DexFormer 1.0 baselines. Without fine-tuning, it also grasps with four new hand types and, through retargeting, with parallel grippers. Ablations show that memory, the kinematic-tree bias, and broader pairing coverage each matter for transfer. On a physical Flexiv arm, distilled students transfer zero-shot from simulation with two hands, including a held-out pair. Project website: https://davidlxu.github.io/dexformer2-web/.

</details>


### [61] [Ontology-Compiled Control Barrier Functions for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20306)
*Peng Wu,Seyede Fatemeh Ghoreishi,Nathan Bastian,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OntoCBF：将领域本体（类层次、类型化关系、安全公理与各机器人类别的动力学画像）编译为控制屏障函数（CBF）安全过滤器，每个智能体求解一个二次规划（QP）；用描述逻辑推理在线导出异构团队的安全约束，并证明子类可复用父类制动能力的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队的安全过滤器因三类问题失效：约束缺失（新类别、新载荷无法被现有安全规则覆盖）、制动能力估计过高、以及单独可满足的约束之间发生冲突；成对屏障在三体及以上交互时存在必然碰撞的"不可避免"状态。

Method: 以本体建模（类分类、类型化关系、安全公理、每类动力学画像），用描述逻辑蕴含在线生成任意团队的约束，包括关系链与"按载荷定义的类"所推出的约束；每个智能体求解一个基于 CBF 的 QP 过滤器；理论分析给出子类复用父类制动能力的充分条件（能产生父类所有速度），并证明盒子约束双积分器下该条件亦为必要条件、安全损失均始于冲突。

Result: 在人类无视机器人的仿真仓库中：为设计类编写的过滤器在遇到未见类别时 89–100% 回合违反安全；OntoCBF 在所有场景（含 32 机器人）中违反安全率为 20–70%，且每次均发生在冲突之后，最深侵入中位数为禁入距离的 10–17%；以过滤器在环训练多智能体 PPO 可将不可行 QP（几乎全为冲突）比例降低 18–41%。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的约束编译使安全规则可泛化到新机器人类别与载荷组合；但冲突导致的不安全性无法靠固定能力削减消除，需通过感知/学习层面的策略调整来减少冲突，从而提升可行性。

Abstract: Safety filters for heterogeneous robot teams fail because of missing constraints, overestimated braking capabilities and conflicts between individually satisfiable constraints. We present OntoCBF, which compiles a domain ontology with a class taxonomy, typed relations, safety axioms and a dynamics profile per robot class into a control barrier function filter with one quadratic program per agent. Description-logic entailment supplies the constraints of any team online, including those that follow from chains of relations and from classes defined by what a robot carries, so the existing safety axioms cover new robot classes, payload classes and combinations. We prove that a subclass may reuse the braking capability of its parent whenever it can produce every velocity of the parent, a property separate from subsumption. For box-bounded double integrators this property is also necessary, and every loss of safety then begins with a conflict. Once three robots interact, the safe set of pairwise barriers contains states from which a collision is unavoidable, and such states persist in larger teams under any fixed capability reduction. In a simulated warehouse where humans ignore the robots, a filter written for the design classes violates safety in 89-100 % of the episodes with unseen classes. In all scenarios, including one with 32 robots, OntoCBF violates safety in 20-70 % of the episodes, each time after a conflict, with a median deepest intrusion of 10-17 % of the keep-out distance. A multi-agent PPO policy trained with the filter in the loop lowers the rate of infeasible quadratic programs, nearly all of them conflicts, by 18-41 %.

</details>


### [62] [NF-VLA: A Normalizing-Flow Baseline for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20314)
*Ruichen Deng,Qinyu Zhao,Yong-Hyun Park,Mutian Tong,Liang Zheng,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NF-VLA 用条件自回归归一化流以单一最大似然目标训练 VLA 策略，并在推理时用同一密度做一次 score 校正，在 LIBERO 上达 98.35% 成功率、LIBERO-Plus 上 72.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归归一化流能为连续机器人动作提供可计算的密度与采样，作者希望验证单一最大似然目标是否足以训练出与主流方法竞争的 VLA 策略，并避免额外训练独立去噪器。

Method: 提出 NF-VLA：深层-浅层架构中，大容量深层块集中处理视觉、语言与本体感知前缀条件，无条件的浅层块构成共享可逆动作变换；视觉-语言主干与流在平滑后的动作块上以精确最大似然联合训练；推理时用同一学习密度做一次 score 校正以弥补训练时的高斯平滑。

Result: 基于预训练视觉-语言主干（无机器人预训练）在 LIBERO 训练，四个标准套件平均成功率 98.35%，同一检查点零微调在 LIBERO-Plus 上达 72.6%；消融显示仅深层前缀条件化与 score 校正均提升闭环成功率。

Conclusion: 结构精心设计的自回归流是 VLA 控制的有竞争力方案，单一学习密度可同时支持策略训练、动作生成与动作精炼。

Abstract: Autoregressive normalizing flows (NFs) provide a tractable density for learning and sampling continuous robot actions. We show that a single maximum-likelihood objective can train vision--language--action (VLA) policies competitive with leading methods on manipulation benchmarks. We present NF-VLA, which combines conditional autoregressive normalizing flows with score-based action refinement. Its deep--shallow architecture concentrates visual, language, and proprioceptive prefix conditioning in a high-capacity deep block, while unconditioned shallow blocks provide a shared invertible action transformation. The vision--language backbone and flow are jointly trained by exact maximum likelihood on Gaussian-smoothed action chunks. The same learned density supplies a single score-based correction at inference, refining generated actions to account for training-time smoothing without a separately trained denoiser. Trained on LIBERO from a pretrained vision--language backbone without robot pretraining, NF-VLA achieves 98.35\% success across the four standard suites and 72.6\% on LIBERO-Plus using the same checkpoint without further fine-tuning. Ablations show that deep-only prefix conditioning and score-based refinement both improve closed-loop success. Together, these results establish carefully structured autoregressive flows as a competitive approach to VLA control, with one learned density supporting policy training, action generation, and refinement.

</details>


### [63] [High-Speed Forward and Reverse Tractor-Trailer Driving with Learned Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20324)
*Aaron Chen,David Shen,Taekyung Kim,Kaleb Ben Naveed,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于牵引挂车（tractor-trailer）控制的学得动力学模型，与包含Fiala轮胎力的降阶解析模型对比，并在MPPI控制器框架下进行闭环跟踪实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牵引挂车系统控制困难，倒车时尤其不稳定，且其动力学依赖路面摩擦、悬架系数等难以测量的因素，解析建模存在局限。

Method: 构建学习得到的动力学模型作为解析基线（含Fiala轮胎力的降阶模型）的替代；分别用两种模型驱动MPPI控制器，并在四种仿真环境中开展闭环跟踪实验，另与三种基线控制器比较。

Result: 在所有测试设定点上，基于学得动力学的MPPI平均速度更接近目标值，铰接角均方根（RMS）更低；在八种倒车配置中有七种完成率更高。

Conclusion: 学得动力学模型优于解析降阶模型，能显著提升牵引挂车在倒车等复杂工况下的跟踪精度与任务完成率。

Abstract: Tractor-trailer systems are difficult to control, especially unstable during reverse motion, and their dynamics depend on difficult-to-measure factors, including road friction and suspension coefficients. We introduce a learned dynamics model for tractor-trailer control and compare it with a reduced-order dynamics model with Fiala tire forces as an analytic baseline. Specifically, we compare a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller that uses the learned dynamics model to an MPPI controller that uses the analytic model, as well as to three additional baseline controllers. In the main closed-loop tracking experiments across four simulated environments, MPPI with learned dynamics achieves mean speeds closer to the target magnitudes and lower root-mean-square (RMS) hitch angles than MPPI with the analytic model at every tested setpoint, with higher completion rates in seven of eight reverse configurations.

</details>


### [64] [Making Koopman Control Guarantees Hold on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20402)
*Abulikemu Abuduweili,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出切线不变Koopman（TIK）方法，通过在嵌入切空间上强制一阶匹配，使学习的潜空间线性控制器能够真正镇定非线性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman控制器能在学到的潜空间线性模型上稳定，却无法稳定真实非线性对象：模型在潜空间传播预测，而部署时控制器反复重新编码物理状态，二者闭环动力学不一致导致失效。

Method: 论证当学习与真实闭环动力学在目标点嵌入切空间上一阶一致时，名义稳定性可迁移到非线性系统；给出近似匹配下的鲁棒裕度，指出稳定开环潜模型无法精确匹配不稳定平衡点。提出TIK：从离线迁移估计局部对象线性化，并强制一阶匹配该估计；目标点处恢复拟合LQR增益，双线性预测器捕捉大幅机动中的状态依赖驱动。另给出基于经验曲率估计的离线稳定性检验，以及在模型误差与曲率界下的不变终端集保证。

Result: 四个不稳定仿真基准中，全部40个标准Koopman控制器潜闭环稳定但部署后局部不稳定；全部40个TIK控制器均局部镇定对应非线性系统。离线检验在两个小车-杆系统上识别出稳定TIK控制器，高噪声下不确定。TIK在0.25 ms规划预算下实现88%带约束小车-杆摆起成功率，七关节臂每步控制耗时1–5 ms。

Conclusion: 一阶切空间匹配是Koopman控制器迁移到非线性对象稳定性的关键条件；TIK以可证裕度、离线检验与高效在线控制实现可靠镇定与约束满足。

Abstract: A Koopman controller can stabilize its learned latent-space linear model yet fail to stabilize the nonlinear plant. The learned model propagates predictions in latent space, while the deployed controller repeatedly re-encodes the measured physical state. We show that nominal closed-loop stability transfers to the nonlinear plant when the learned and true closed-loop dynamics agree to first order on the embedding's tangent space at the target. We derive a robustness margin for approximate matching and show that a stable open-loop latent model cannot exactly match an unstable equilibrium. We introduce Tangent-Invariant Koopman (TIK), which estimates a local plant linearization from offline transitions and enforces first-order matching to this estimate. TIK recovers the fitted LQR gain at the target, while a bilinear predictor captures state-dependent actuation during large maneuvers. Across four simulated unstable benchmarks, all 40 Standard Koopman controllers have stable latent closed loops, but their deployed nonlinear systems are locally unstable. In contrast, all 40 TIK controllers locally stabilize their respective nonlinear plants. An offline stability test using empirical curvature estimates identifies stable TIK controllers on two cart-pole systems, but is inconclusive under high noise. Under explicit model-error and curvature bounds, an invariant terminal set guarantees constraint satisfaction after entry under local feedback. TIK achieves 88\% constrained cart-pole swing-up success with a 0.25 ms planning budget and controls simulated seven-joint arms within 1-5 ms per step.

</details>


### [65] [XMimic and XSoma: Learning Humanoid Motion Tracking and Locomotion in Under a Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20452)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XMimic 和 XSoma 用 JAX 编译完整训练循环（含 MuJoCo Warp 模拟器）并将每步并行到最多四块 GPU，在不到一分钟训练内实现人形运动跟踪与崎岖地形行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SuperMimic 类方法每次训练迭代是大量小 GPU 操作的长串行链，无法利用更大规模 GPU；同时人形运动跟踪与崎岖地形移动的训练耗时较长，需要更高效的方案。

Method: 沿用 SuperMimic 的范式：学习机器人世界模型，并在模型想象的短 rollout 上用解析梯度优化策略。XMimic 与 XSoma 缩短串行链，把每次迭代的步骤同时放到最多四块 GPU 上，并用 JAX 编译整个训练循环（包括 MuJoCo Warp 模拟器）。XSoma 只把机器人动力学交给世界模型，其余（含脚下地形高度）精确计算，采用一秒时域且无 critic。

Result: 在四块 H100 上，XMimic 用 0.86 分钟学会 Unitree G1 跟踪全部 40 条 LAFAN1 片段，比单 GPU 的 BlinkMimic 快 3.7 倍；XSoma 用 0.77 分钟达到 Holosoma 为 FastSAC 设定的跟踪目标，比单 GPU 的 FastSAC 快 12.3 倍，且环境步数仅为其八分之一。

Conclusion: 通过端到端 JAX 编译与多 GPU 并行改写串行训练链，模型-based 解析梯度方法可在亚分钟级完成人形运动跟踪与崎岖地形移动训练，显著提升样本与计算效率。

Abstract: We present XMimic and XSoma, which learn humanoid motion tracking and locomotion on rough terrain in under a minute of training. Both follow SuperMimic, which learns a world model of the robot and optimizes the policy with analytic gradients through short rollouts imagined in this model. Existing implementations of this method do not make use of larger GPUs, as every training iteration is a long chain of small GPU operations that run one after another. XMimic and XSoma therefore shorten this chain and run the steps of every iteration at the same time on up to four GPUs. We compile the complete training loop with JAX, including the MuJoCo Warp simulator. For locomotion, XSoma leaves only the dynamics of the robot to the world model and computes everything else exactly, including the terrain height under the feet. It learns with a horizon of one second and without a critic. On four H100 GPUs, XMimic learns to track all 40 LAFAN1 clips with the Unitree G1 in 0.86 minutes, 3.7 times faster than BlinkMimic on one GPU. XSoma reaches the tracking target that Holosoma's tests set for FastSAC in 0.77 minutes, 12.3 times faster than FastSAC on one GPU, while needing only an eighth of its environment steps.

</details>


### [66] [Stochastic Self-Distillation for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20460)
*Jingru Guo,Alessandra Russo,Joey Bose,Christos Ziakas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-SSD：将预训练流匹配动作头自蒸馏为随机单步策略，降低推理延迟并提供可用的似然，从而实现高效的基于似然比的在线RL后训练，在LIBERO上以20.6%相对提升超越π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的VLA模型在模仿学习中表现优异，但用在线策略强化学习（on-policy RL）微调仍受限：GRPO等基于组的方法在每个rollout步骤都需多步ODE积分，导致推理延迟高；同时采样器随机性有限，组内样本方差低，削弱了组相对优势估计的效果。

Method: 提出VLA-SSD：把预训练流匹配动作头通过自蒸馏转化为随机单步策略——以布朗路径为条件训练单跳生成器，从而在一次前向中采样动作；并据此推导条件动作密度，使策略具备可计算的似然，进而支持基于似然比的RL算法（如GRPO）进行高效后训练。

Result: 从π0蒸馏得到VLA-SSD后，动作头前向时间缩短7.7倍，LIBERO成功率相对下降5.5%；但利用VLA-SSD在组生成中更强的样本多样性，对π0.5进行GRPO后训练可获得20.6%的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 单步随机化蒸馏可在很小的成功率代价下大幅降低延迟，并显著提升组采样多样性，使流式VLA的在线RL后训练变得实用；随机性而非仅仅是速度，是解锁组式RL收益的关键因素。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models achieve strong performance in robotics through imitation learning, yet fine-tuning them with on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is limited. In group-based RL methods like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), every rollout step requires multi-step ODE integration, and the sampler's limited stochasticity leads to rollout groups with low variance. We propose VLA-SSD, which addresses both high inference latency and limited stochasticity. In particular, our method self-distills a pretrained flow-matching action head into a stochastic one-step policy by conditioning a single-jump generator on a Brownian path. We derive a conditional action density via our stochastic one-step policy, enabling efficient post-training via likelihood-ratio RL algorithms. We show that distilling VLA-SSD from the $π_0$ VLA reduces the action head's forward-pass time by a factor of 7.7, at the cost of a 5.5% relative decrease in the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark. However, by leveraging the increased sample diversity of VLA-SSD during group generation, post-training a $π_{0.5}$ VLA using GRPO yields a 20.6% relative improvement.

</details>


### [67] [ReRead: Wrong Predictions, Useful Representations - Correcting Frozen Robot Policies by Re-reading Their Own Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20465)
*Xu Su,Shunlong Xiao,Qintao Yi,Mingkuan Zhao,Xinmiao Yu,Yizhe Li,Zihan Wang,Bokui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReRead：冻结骨干网络，用仅 4.17% 额外延迟的轻量校正器重读当前与两帧历史缓存来预测动作残差，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上显著提升多个 VLA 策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在困难任务上失败时，常见做法是重训骨干、加外部视觉编码器或加记忆模块，成本高。作者主张"错误预测不等于错误表征"：策略可能已保留所需信息，只是没能正确读出为动作，因此只需改进读出方式而非重建表征。

Method: 骨干参数冻结，训练一个不接收图像的校正器，读取当前及前两帧的骨干缓存特征，输出动作残差；仅用 50 个 RoboTwin 2.0 任务的离线示教数据训练。通过消融与干预实验（替换四种外部视觉编码器、替换为其他轨迹的历史缓存）验证信息来源。

Result: 在 11 个最难的 RoboTwin 2.0 任务上，LingBot-VLA、π0.5、Fast-WAM 成功率分别提升 23.45、13.09、8.00 个百分点；全 50 任务配对评估中 Fast-WAM 平均成功率由 90.73% 升至 92.66%；Flexiv 双臂机器人上一阶段歧义任务成功率从 0% 升至 36%；额外延迟仅 4.17%。

Conclusion: 外部视觉编码器未带来额外收益，且替换为其他轨迹缓存会显著增大动作误差，说明校正增益来自骨干自身表征的有效读出。该结果支持"表征足够、读出不足"的诊断视角，并给出低成本的通用校正方案。

Abstract: When a pretrained robot policy fails on challenging tasks, common remedies include retraining the backbone, adding an external visual encoder, or introducing a memory module. We argue that wrong predictions do not imply wrong representations: a policy can retain the required information yet fail to read it out as the correct action. To test this view, we propose ReRead: with the backbone frozen, a corrector re-reads the current and two previous caches to predict an action residual. The corrector sees no images, is trained solely on offline demonstrations from all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, and adds only 4.17% latency. Ablations and interventions show that the correction relies on the backbone's own representations: none of four external visual encoders adds further gain, and swapping in past caches from other trajectories sharply increases action error. On the 11 hardest RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, ReRead improves the success rates of LingBot-VLA, $π_{0.5}$, and Fast-WAM by 23.45, 13.09, and 8.00 percentage points, respectively. In paired evaluation on all 50 tasks, it raises Fast-WAM's average success rate from 90.73% to 92.66%. On a Flexiv dual-arm robot, it raises success on a phase-ambiguous task from 0% to 36%.

</details>


### [68] [Lift, See, Act: Hierarchical Robot Policy Pretraining with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20481)
*Yiyuan Ge,Changxing Ding,Ziyu Hao,Zijie Zheng,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LSA（Lift-See-Act）三阶段层级式机器人策略预训练框架，充分利用3D基础模型提取过程中的稠密信息，从无标注人类视频中学习显式动作相关表征，在仿真与真实任务上显著超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人类视频的机器人策略预训练对提升泛化能力至关重要，但无标注视频缺乏显式的动作相关表征。现有方法用3D基础模型估计手部运动轨迹时，仅把稀疏轨迹当作监督信号，丢弃了轨迹提取过程中产生的丰富细粒度信息（如中间特征、深度信息、手部区域等），造成信息浪费。

Method: LSA为三阶段框架：1）Lift——将2D视频观测提升为稠密3D表征，通过自适应对齐策略网络浅层特征与多个3D基础模型的中间表征实现；2）See——在提升后的表征上通过双重引导赋予策略显式几何与交互感知能力，包括深度图重建以理解3D空间布局，以及利用手部区域线索显式监督编码器注意力聚焦于任务相关交互热点；3）Act——基于稠密3D表征学习和精确空间引导，执行稳健且准确的机器人操作。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，LSA显著优于当前最先进方法，机器人操作表现更稳健、更准确；项目主页已公开。

Conclusion: 不应仅将3D手部轨迹作为稀疏监督信号，而应充分利用3D基础模型提取过程中的稠密中间信息；通过Lift-See-Act的层级预训练设计，可有效弥补无标注人类视频中动作相关表征缺失的问题，为机器人策略预训练提供更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot policy pretraining based on human videos is crucial for improving the policy's generalization ability. One main challenge for this task is the lack of explicit action-relevant representations in such unlabeled data. Recent works tend to estimate 3D hand motion trajectories from videos using 3D foundation models (3D FMs). However, they keep solely the sparse trajectories as supervision, ignoring the rich, fine-grained information produced during the trajectory extraction process. To address this issue, we propose LSA, a hierarchical robot policy pretraining framework that includes three phases: Lift, See, and Act. Specifically, the Lift phase elevates 2D video observations into dense 3D representations by adaptively aligning features in the policy's shallow layers with the intermediate representations extracted from multiple 3D FMs. Building upon these lifted representations, the See phase equips the policy with explicit geometric and interactive awareness through dual guidance. It introduces depth-map reconstruction to help the model comprehend 3D spatial layouts and utilizes hand region cues to explicitly supervise the encoder's attention toward task-relevant interaction hotspots. Empowered by the dense 3D representation learning and precise spatial guidance, our policy achieves robust and accurate robotic manipulation in the Act phase. Extensive experiments on diverse simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that LSA significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is available at: https://yiyuan00.github.io/Lift-See-Act.

</details>


### [69] [WTR-Drive: World-Task Decomposition with Structured Risk Supervision for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20510)
*Haoyu Xing,Shaobing Ding,Guanglei Zhao,Guanhua Zhao,Nawu Peng,Mingyang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WTR-Drive将共享场景估计与候选轨迹条件推理分离：用提议无关的World Memory估计物体状态、地图结构与未来运动，用残差Task分支承载候选反馈，并推导出结构化风险目标（事件存在性、对象关联、首次违规时间bin）。NAVSIM navtest上达94.66 PDMS（v1）/88.24 EPDMS（v2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提议条件的场景预测缺乏在候选轨迹间共享的对象级参照；聚合式规划打分又使违规所涉及的对象与时间信息隐含不显。作者因此希望同时解决"共享参照缺失"和"风险不可解释"两个问题。

Method: 提出Drive-JEPA框架下的WTR-Drive（World-Task-Risk）规划器：1）提议无关的World Memory在每轮refinement中重新读取原始视觉特征，估计物体状态、地图结构及未来运动；2）提议反馈仅更新残差Task分支、不回写World Memory，Task分支通过候选特定检索与门控视觉反馈引导细化，末端的Task readout输出候选条件特征用于打分；3）从已有候选打分rollout中直接推导结构化风险目标（无需额外仿真调用），对自车责任碰撞与TTC违规编码事件存在性、与共享对象槽的关联、以及首个违规时间bin。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest split（expert-frame sequential evaluation）上取得94.66 PDMS（v1）与88.24 EPDMS（v2），分别比已发表的Drive-JEPA高0.96和0.44分。候选级分析发现：最优候选分数更高时，被选中轨迹的分数反而可能更低，说明候选质量与选择有效性之间存在gap。

Conclusion: 共享场景估计与候选条件推理的解耦能有效提升规划性能，并让风险后果具备对象与时间层面的可解释性；同时指出现有选择机制是进一步提升的瓶颈，候选打分高并不等于选得对。

Abstract: Proposal-conditioned scene predictions can lack a common object-level reference across candidate trajectories. Aggregate planning scores also leave the objects and times associated with violations implicit. We present WTR-Drive (World-Task-Risk Driving), a Drive-JEPA-based planner that separates shared scene estimation from proposal-conditioned reasoning. A proposal-independent World Memory estimates object states, map structure, and future motion by re-reading the original visual features at each refinement round. Proposal feedback updates a residual Task branch without writing back to World Memory. This branch guides refinement through candidate-specific retrieval and gated visual feedback. A final Task readout supplies candidate-conditioned features for scoring. We derive structured risk targets from existing candidate-scoring rollouts without additional simulator calls. For at-fault collision and time-to-collision violations, the targets encode event existence, association with shared object slots, and the first violation time bin. On the NAVSIM navtest split, WTR-Drive achieves 94.66 PDMS on v1 and 88.24 EPDMS on v2 (expert-frame sequential evaluation). These scores are 0.96 and 0.44 points above the published Drive-JEPA values, respectively. Candidate-level analysis shows that higher best-candidate scores can coexist with lower selected-trajectory scores, revealing a gap between candidate quality and selection effectiveness.

</details>


### [70] [AsymEEF: Bimanual Manipulation with Asymmetric End Effectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20557)
*Xiaokang Sun,Ningyue Wang,Kunqi Xu,Guangqi Jiang,Jianglong Ye,Yong Wu,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AsymEEF 框架，将少量源示范数据迁移到不同运动学与动作空间的异构双臂末端执行器组合上，生成可执行的跨末端示教，并支持统一的双臂视触觉策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作示教数据采集成本高，且难以在不同运动学与动作空间的末端执行器之间复用；双臂操作中每种末端执行器组合都可能需要重新采集数据，负担进一步加重。

Method: 以少量源数据集为输入：用运动学教师锚点提供几何监督，用稀疏真实世界 X-Calib 锚点纠正抓取执行偏差；共享编码器将腕部位姿与执行器状态映射到与形态无关的潜空间，形态条件化解码器重建目标指令；生成轨迹经重放过滤以保证任务级可执行性；该表示同时用于训练跨多种末端组合的统一双臂视触觉策略。

Result: 在 4 个仿真任务和 6 个真实任务上，重放成功率优于适配迁移基线；仿真中平均获得 16.6 倍的重放有效数据扩展增益；并在真实机器人上完成下游策略训练评估。
