Conclusion: AsymEEF 无需额外遥操作即可实现跨末端执行器的示教生成与统一双臂策略学习，为异构双臂操作的数据复用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are costly to collect and rarely reusable across end-effectors with different kinematics and action spaces. This burden grows in bimanual manipulation, where each end-effector pair may require new data. We present AsymEEF, a framework for executable cross-end-effector demonstration generation and unified bimanual policy learning. Given a small source dataset, AsymEEF transfers demonstrations to heterogeneous combinations of two-, three-, and five-fingered end-effectors without additional teleoperation. Kinematic teacher anchors provide geometric supervision, while sparse real-world X-Calib anchors correct discrepancies in grasp execution. A shared encoder maps wrist poses and actuator states to a morphology-agnostic latent space, and a morphology-conditioned decoder reconstructs target commands. Generated trajectories undergo replay filtering for task-level executability. The representation also supports a unified bimanual visuomotor policy trained across multiple pairs. Across four simulated and six real-world tasks, AsymEEF improves replay success over adapted transfer baselines, achieves a mean replay-valid expansion gain of 16.6 times in simulation, and is evaluated through downstream policy training on real robots. Project website: https://asymeef.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [71] [Vision-Language Grounded Semantic Navigation for Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20558)
*Ravindran Vasoor Saravanan,Chittin Varunav Ravi,Madhumita Singh,Thirulok Sundar Mohan Rasu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完整的ROS2系统，将前沿探索、基于Grounding DINO的开放词表目标检测、基于深度的3D定位与语义建图相结合，在Gazebo仿真中实现语言引导的端到端视觉-语言接地导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导导航需让移动机器人在非结构化环境中理解自然语言指令并到达指定目标，但将开放词表视觉感知与经典机器人建图/导航流程集成仍具挑战。

Method: 系统接受"go to the yellow box"类自然语言指令，用文本提示从RGB图像检测候选目标，估计其世界坐标系位置，生成导航目标交由Nav2执行；同时结合前沿探索、深度3D定位与语义建图，无需任务专用训练，基于CPU轻量架构，在TurtleBot3+Gazebo室内环境验证。

Result: 机器人自主探索房间，检测到5个目标物体中的3个，并成功执行了两条测试的自然语言导航指令（无需任务特定训练）。

Conclusion: 开放词表接地模型可集成进经典导航流程，但性能受检测不确定性、深度定位误差与探索覆盖不完整的限制，说明轻量CPU架构部署视觉-语言接地导航兼具可行性与现存局限。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation enables mobile robots to interpret natural language commands and navigate toward user-specified targets in unstructured environments. However, integrating open-vocabulary visual perception with classical robotics pipelines for mapping and navigation remains a challenging problem. In this work, we present a complete ROS2-based system that combines frontier-based exploration, open-vocabulary object detection using Grounding DINO, depth-based 3D localization, and semantic mapping to enable end-to-end vision-language grounded navigation in simulation. The system accepts natural language commands such as ``go to the yellow box,'' detects candidate objects from RGB images using text prompts, estimates their positions in the world frame, and generates navigation goals executed by the Nav2 stack. In a Gazebo-based indoor environment with a TurtleBot3 robot, the system autonomously explored the room, detected three of the five target objects, and successfully executed both natural language navigation commands tested, without task-specific training. The results indicate that open-vocabulary grounding models can be integrated into classical navigation pipelines, though performance is limited by detection uncertainty, depth-based localization error, and incomplete exploration coverage. These findings highlight both the feasibility and current limitations of deploying vision-language grounded navigation systems using lightweight, CPU-only architectures.

</details>


### [72] [sqrtVINS-Based 360 Visual-Inertial SLAM Systems: Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026 Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20568)
*Jungwoo Lee,Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jinyong Jeong,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套用于2026 Hilti-Trimble SLAM挑战赛的360相机视觉惯性流水线，仅用双鱼眼图像与IMU：前端为预处理（低光增强+动静掩码）加√VINS局部里程计（SuperPoint+ELSed+LightGlue），后端用GTSAM紧耦合因子图优化、SALAD回环全局描述子与视觉BA，并做局部到全局无结构BA、严格正则化的内外参精化及基于已知相机间基线的全局尺度调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向长时室内序列中暗光区域、重复走廊、动态物体与几何视差受限等难点，在给定ROS2工作空间内构建可复现的高鲁棒位姿估计系统。

Method: 双鱼眼图像低光增强与静态/动态掩码预处理；以√VINS为前端，融合SuperPoint深度学习特征、ELSD/ELSed线段与LightGlue匹配；后端基于GTSAM进行紧耦合因子图优化，结合SALAD全局描述子做回环检测与视觉BA；执行局部到全局的无结构BA、强正则化相机内外参精化，并利用已知相机间基线与跨视角约束进行全局尺度校正。

Result: Fig.1显示所提方法在多个室内序列上的建图/轨迹结果与楼层平面图对齐良好。

Conclusion: 该360视觉惯性系统通过深度学习特征、线段、鲁棒回环与紧耦合BA的组合，能在暗光与重复结构室内场景中给出与平面图一致的轨迹，且保持整体流程在ROS2环境下可复现。

Abstract: This report describes our submission to the 2026 Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge. The system is a 360-camera visual-inertial pipeline that uses only the provided dual-fisheye image streams and IMU measurements. First, raw images are preprocessed with low-light enhancement and static/dynamic masking, and then fed into a $\sqrt{\text{VINS}}$ frontend as the local odometry, with deep learning-based SuperPoint features, ELSED line segments, and LightGlue matching. The resulting trajectory is refined using a GTSAM-based backend that performs tightly-coupled factor graph optimization with SALAD global descriptor and visual bundle adjustment (BA). The backend performs local-to-global structureless BA, tightly regularized intrinsic and extrinsic refinement, and global scale adjustment supported by the known inter-camera baseline and cross-view constraints. The design targets long indoor sequences with dark regions, repetitive corridors, dynamic objects, and limited geometric parallax, while keeping the pipeline reproducible in the provided ROS2 workspace. Fig. 1 demonstrates that our proposed method aligns well with the floorplan across several indoor sequences.

</details>


### [73] [OrthoMark: Place Recognition with Point-Anchored Orthographic Height Mark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20570)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的LiDAR地点识别方法，核心原则是"两帧扫描只能通过从相同几何表面计算出的特征来比较"：检索阶段使用BEV的极坐标傅里叶谱（平移旋转不变，每帧仅1.9KB），验证阶段用相位相关恢复平面位姿，并用点锚定的高度列（每格16位高度掩码）打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR地点识别为SLAM提供回环闭合。现有方法对"高度"信息的利用缺乏一致的几何解释：沿Z轴俯视时，重力对齐的扫描是场景的正交地图，重访只是该地图在平面内的刚性运动；而沿垂直方向，观测到哪些高度取决于距离（传感器垂直视场角会截断近处结构），因此高度作为全局关键帧描述子会被误导，但在两帧对齐后逐格比较时又非常有效。作者希望厘清何时该用高度、何时不该用。

Method: 免训练方法：1）检索：用鸟瞰图（BEV）的极坐标傅里叶谱作为平移与旋转不变的描述子，每关键帧仅1.9KB；2）验证：对每个候选用相位相关估计平面位姿；3）打分：采用"点锚定的高度列"，每个BEV格保留自身回波高度的16位掩码，在两帧重叠区域内逐格比较；4）会话内按两帧距离推算的期望感测范围重叠度对得分加权。

Result: 实验表明垂直（高度）链接在验证阶段有收益、在检索阶段反而有害。单机器人场景下，在KITTI开发序列上，该方法在无3D配准的免训练方法中AP最高；跨机器人场景下，在所有多机器人序列上达到或超过RING++，而描述子尺寸仅为其1/350；并且在全部测试中比学习型方法BEVPlace++闭合了更多跨机器人回环。

Conclusion: 高度的作用具有二重性：作为全局检索键时，因高度切片内容随距离变化而不可靠；而一旦两帧在平面上对齐、并按格在重叠处比较，高度就成为决定性的判别信息。据此设计的检索-验证分离架构，在免训练、低存储、跨机器人条件下取得了领先的回环检测性能。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition provides the loop closures of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping(SLAM). Perspective from Z-axis, a gravity-aligned scan is an orthographic map of the scene, and a revisit from another position and heading moves this map rigidly in the plane, a motion that a polar Fourier spectrum ignores and phase correlation recovers. Along the vertical, the heights of the returns describe the structures, but which heights are observed depends on the range, because the sensor's vertical field of view cuts nearby structures low; height thus misleads a global key, yet is decisive once two scans are aligned and compared cell by cell where they overlap. We present \method, a training-free method built on one principle: two LiDAR scans can only be compared through features computed from the same geometric surfaces in both scans. \method retrieves candidates with a translation- and rotation-invariant polar spectrum of the bird's-eye view (BEV), 1.9,KB per keyframe, and verifies each candidate by estimating its planar pose through phase correlation and scoring it with \emph{point-anchored height columns}: every BEV cell keeps a 16-bit mask of the heights of its own returns; within a session, the score is weighted by the expected overlap of the two scans' sensing ranges, computed from their distance. We show that this vertical link pays off in verification but hurts in retrieval, where what a height slice contains depends on the range to the structure. For single robot, \method has the highest AP of the training-free methods without 3D registration on the KITTI development sequences; across robots, on all multi-robot sequences, it matches or exceeds RING++ at 1/350 of its descriptor size and closes more inter-robot loops than the learned BEVPlace++ on all tests.

</details>


### [74] [ExtremeDrive: A Real-World Dataset and Benchmark for Critical Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20574)
*Xinhan Li,Yuhong Jiang,Shiyue Zhao,Junzhi Zhang,Chengkun He,Ruihai Ma,Shijie Zhao,Wenxuan Cheng,Jinbo Lv,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ExtremeDrive，一个覆盖紧急机动、复杂交通交互与恶劣环境（湿滑/积雪路面）的真实世界自动驾驶数据集与基准，含589个驾驶场景，并提出基于仿真执行评估规划轨迹的基准，实验表明加入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差并提升关键成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集与基准多针对常规驾驶，虽资源丰富，但覆盖安全关键场景与极端环境条件的真实世界数据仍然有限，难以支撑相关学习与评测需求。

Method: 从更大的真实驾驶记录池中筛选并整理589个驾驶场景，涵盖紧急机动、复杂交通交互及湿滑/积雪等恶劣路况，并提供标定对齐的多传感器数据与经审核的道路结构和交通参与者标注；同时构建基准，通过仿真车辆执行来评估规划轨迹，度量安全性相关指标与驾驶表现；在相同训练集规模下进行对比实验。

Result: 等量训练集实验中，纳入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差，并提升关键场景下的平均关键成功率。

Conclusion: ExtremeDrive为安全关键场景与极端环境条件下的自动驾驶学习与评估提供了统一资源。

Abstract: Existing autonomous driving datasets and benchmarks provide extensive resources for routine driving, yet real-world data covering safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions remain limited. To address this gap, we present ExtremeDrive, a real-world dataset and benchmark covering emergency maneuvers, complex traffic interactions, and adverse conditions such as slippery or snow-covered roads, alongside routine driving. The dataset comprises 589 driving scenes curated from a larger pool of real-world driving recordings, with calibrated and aligned multisensor data and reviewed annotations of road structure and traffic participants. We further establish a benchmark to evaluate planned trajectories through simulated vehicle execution, measuring safety-related metrics and driving performance. Experiments at equal training set sizes show that incorporating critical driving data reduces trajectory prediction errors and improves mean critical-success rates in critical scenarios. ExtremeDrive provides a unified resource for learning and evaluating autonomous driving in safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Stability Principle Inherent in Wheel Gait of Planar X-shaped Walker Generated Using Constant Torque Drive and Mechanical Stoppers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20587)
*Fumihiko Asano,Cong Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究髋关节机械限位对平面X形步行机器人轮式步态稳定性的作用，将单步运动分为四个阶段，用线性化模型推导状态误差转移矩阵的近似解析解，并以最大奇异值等指标分析误差范数的增减，数值仿真表明两阶段不稳定、两阶段稳定，总体呈渐近稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自19世纪末以来，人们已知髋关节机械限位对产生稳定的被动动态行走至关重要，近期关于被动动态与极限环行走器的研究也确认了髋、膝关节限位有助于稳定行走；然而，关于这种机械约束为何能提升整体步态稳定性的理论研究仍不充分。

Method: 引入一个在髋关节装有机械限位的平面X形步行器，研究机械约束对下坡恒力矩驱动所产生轮式步态稳定性的影响；将一步运动划分为四个阶段，基于线性化模型推导各阶段状态误差转移函数矩阵的近似解析解，并利用最大奇异值等指标分析状态误差范数的增减。

Result: 借助限位将自身约束在目标冲击姿态、单纯前倾即可产生高度稳定的轮式步态；数值仿真显示，就状态误差范数的增长而言有两个阶段不稳定，而另外两个阶段稳定，从而整体上实现渐近稳定。

Conclusion: 机械限位通过分阶段地抑制与放大状态误差，使整体步态获得渐近稳定性，为机械约束增强步态稳定性的机理提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Since the late 19th century when the first walking toys were developed, it has been known that mechanical stoppers at the hip joint are crucial for generating stable passive dynamic walking. Recent research on passive-dynamic and limit-cycle walkers has also confirmed that mechanical stoppers at the hip and knee joints are effective for generating stable walking motion, but theoretical research on how this mechanical constraint enhances the overall gait stability remains insufficient. This paper introduces a planar X-shaped walker equipped with mechanical stoppers at the hip joint, and investigates the effect of the mechanical constraint on the stability of the wheel gait generated by constant torque drive on a downslope. By simply falling forward while using the stoppers to constrain itself to the target impact posture, the robot can generate a highly stable wheel gait. We divide the motion of one step into four phases, derive approximate analytical solutions for the state error transition function matrix in each phase using a linearized model, and analyze the increase or decrease in the state error norm using metrics such as its maximum singular value. Numerical simulations demonstrate that while two phases are unstable in terms of the increase in the state error norm, the remaining two phases are stable, resulting in overall asymptotic stability.

</details>


### [76] [JEPTERA: A Platform Agnostic JEPA-Assisted Model Predictive Planner for Urban UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20593)
*Gibran Fathoni Belva,Mansur Arief,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEPTERA导航架构，将JEPA预测、MPC、PID与安全层集成，在仿真城市环境中验证了成功率与跨仿真器可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市无人机导航需同时处理环境几何、机体动力学与移动障碍物，现有单一方法难以兼顾预测、控制与安全性，且规划核心与仿真接口耦合度高、移植困难。

Method: 架构集成四部分：JEPA预测候选指令下无人机运动响应以辅助MPC选动作；PID用于底层控制；安全层检查轨迹与占据空间及预测障碍运动的一致性；并通过仿真器适配器与平台专属YAML配置将规划核心与仿真接口解耦。

Result: 在三个gym场景、六种配置、共180次运行的消融实验中，完整架构聚合成功率最高，30次运行完成28次；在三个城市地图上完成一条路线且无碰撞；在Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo、CARLA中集成测试成功，核心规划逻辑无需修改。

Conclusion: 结果支持该集成架构用于仿真城市无人机导航的可行性及其跨仿真器接口的可移植性。

Abstract: Urban navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) requires planning that accounts for environmental geometry, vehicle dynamics, and moving obstacles. This paper presents JEPTERA, a navigation architecture that integrates a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), Model Predictive Control (MPC), PID control, and a safety layer. JEPA predicts the UAV's motion response to candidate commands for MPC action selection, while the safety layer checks trajectories against occupied space and predicted obstacle motion. Simulator adapters and platform-specific YAML configurations separate the planning core from simulator interfaces. An ablation study evaluated six configurations across three gym scenarios, totaling 180 runs. The complete architecture achieved the highest aggregate success rate, completing 28 of 30 runs. Additional tests completed one route across three city maps without collisions. Separate integration tests demonstrated operation in Gazebo/ROS 2, MuJoCo, and CARLA without modifying the core planning logic. These results support the feasibility of the integrated architecture for simulated urban UAV navigation and its portability across simulator interfaces.

</details>


### [77] [Learning and Verifying Progress: Closed-Loop Agentic Planning with Progress-Enhanced Execution for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20676)
*Kangjie Zhou,Sukai Wang,Yuhan Xiao,Qinghang Liu,Zhejia Wen,ChunXuan Jiao,Wei Shan,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LeaP-VLN，将"进度感知执行"与"独立进度验证"结合，通过"计划-执行-验证-恢复"闭环提升长程视觉语言导航的鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程VLN需要智能体跟踪指令进度，并在局部错误跨子任务传播前进行纠正。但基于专家轨迹训练的被执行器（executor）在部署时会遇到策略诱导的状态漂移，且其停止信号不能可靠指示子任务完成，导致误差累积。

Method: 构建agentic系统LeaP-VLN，采用plan–execute–verify–recover循环：显式地引入进度验证器，在推进前核验子任务完成度，并在路线偏离时触发双阶段恢复；隐式地通过将进度推理与动作预测联合训练，增强执行器的进度感知与纠正控制。为适配闭环接口，设计两阶段进度增强课程：先对专家示范做行为克隆，再在示范、专家动作纠正与进度推理监督上联合训练；并用策略生成的轨迹暴露子任务内漂移与子任务间状态偏移。

Result: 在R2R-CE与RxR-CE上，在未使用额外导航数据集训练的单目方法中取得SOTA成功率；真实世界实验进一步验证了LeaP-VLN在长程导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式验证与隐式进度感知的协同，并借助闭环对齐的课程训练，可有效缓解执行器的状态漂移与停止信号不可靠问题，提升长程VLN的可靠性与可恢复性。

Abstract: Long-horizon vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to track instruction progress and recover from local errors before they propagate across subtasks. However, executors trained on expert trajectories encounter policy-induced state shifts during deployment, and their stop signals do not reliably indicate subtask completion. We introduce Learning and Verifying Progress for VLN (LeaP-VLN), an agentic VLN system that couples progress-aware execution with independent progress verification through plan--execute--verify--recover cycles. LeaP-VLN leverages progress both explicitly, through a progress verifier that verifies subtask completion before the agent advances and triggers dual-phase recovery upon route deviation, and implicitly, by co-training the executor on progress reasoning and action prediction to enhance the executor's progress awareness and corrective control. To adapt the executor to this closed-loop interface, we propose a two-stage progress-enhanced curriculum: behavior cloning on expert demonstrations, followed by joint training on demonstrations, expert action corrections, and progress-reasoning supervision. Policy-generated trajectories expose the executor to both intra-subtask drift and inter-subtask state shifts, aligning training with the deviations encountered during closed-loop execution. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate state-of-the-art success rates among monocular methods trained without additional navigation datasets, while real-world experiments further validate the effectiveness of LeaP-VLN in long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [78] [ARMFUL: Learning Whole-Arm Grasp Configurations for Single-Arm Pickup of Large Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20692)
*Xinyi Yu,Yunshuang Li,Daniel Seita,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向单臂拾取大物体的大规模数据集 WAG（170 万关节配置、793 个物体），并基于该数据集构建整体臂抓取流水线 ARMFUL，仿真训练物体举升成功率 85.4%、新物体 69.9%，真机 Franka 上 100 次试验成功 70 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收纳箱、篮子、椅子等日常物体尺寸大、重量重，传统夹爪无法夹取，但机械臂可以通过"环绕并支撑"的方式整体抱起。这类包裹式动作难以遥操作采集，现有数据集几乎不含整体臂拾取数据，导致该能力缺乏可学习的数据基础。

Method: 构建 WAG 数据集：直接在关节空间采样配置，对 793 个物体共 1.7M 关节配置，在仿真中通过 squeeze 与 lift 两个基本动作执行，并对存活样本进行主动抖动与外力扰动压力测试以筛除不稳定抓取。随后提出 ARMFUL 流水线：用 WAG 训练扩散模型，从物体点云生成整体臂抓取姿态，再由运动规划器无碰撞地到达该姿态，最后复用同样的 squeeze-and-lift 执行流程。

Result: 仿真中 ARMFUL 在训练物体上达到 85.4% 举升成功率，在留出物体上为 69.9%；在真实 Franka 机械臂上对 10 件 IKEA 物体进行测试，100 次试验中成功举升 70 次。

Conclusion: 整体臂包裹式抓取是大物体拾取的有效途径；关节空间直接采样配合抖动/外力压力测试可生成高质量抓取数据，且基于该数据训练的扩散策略能较好地泛化到未见物体与真实平台，验证了数据驱动整体臂拾取的可行性。

Abstract: Many everyday objects, such as storage boxes, baskets, and chairs, are too large or heavy for a gripper to grasp, yet a single arm can still pick them up by wrapping around and supporting them. Such grasps are hard to learn: wrapping motions are difficult to teleoperate, and existing datasets contain little whole-arm pickup data. We introduce WAG (Whole-Arm Grasp Dataset), the first large-scale dataset for single-arm pickup of large objects, with 1.7~million joint configurations across 793 objects. We sample configurations directly in joint space, execute each through squeeze and lift primitives in simulation, and stress-test survivors under active shaking and external forces. To demonstrate the utility of WAG and accomplish the whole-arm pickup task, we develop ARMFUL, a whole-arm pickup pipeline that pairs a diffusion model trained on WAG, generating whole-arm grasps from the object's point cloud, with a motion planner that reaches them collision-free before the same squeeze-and-lift execution. In simulation, ARMFUL reaches 85.4\% lift success on training and 69.9\% on held-out objects, and it lifts the object in 70 of 100 trials on a physical Franka arm across 10 IKEA objects.

</details>


### [79] [Design of the Secure Smart Scooter with Automatic Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20700)
*Hamid Reza Ghaeini,Nils Ole Tippenhauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者提出了一款利用计算机视觉识别行驶车道并自动调整车辆配置的电动滑板车原型，并讨论了该系统的安全与信息安全问题及对应原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动滑板车会在人行道、自行车道或机动车道上行驶，而政府对不同车道设有不同限速。为让滑板车自动适配所在车道的限速等配置，需要车道识别能力，因此作者关注基于计算机视觉的车道判定系统；但这类系统的安全性与信息安全仍存在隐患，需要专门研究。

Method: 作者搭建了车载硬件与软件设置（vehicular setup），实现了一台带安全约束（safety constraints）的电动滑板车原型，其中计算机视觉用于判定行驶车道并自动修改车辆配置；随后在此基础上构建了一个面向该类滑板车的安全（security）原型。

Result: 摘要给出了三部分工作：车辆设置介绍、带安全约束的滑板车实现，以及相应的安全原型；但摘要中未给出定量实验数据或评估结果。

Conclusion: 基于计算机视觉的电动滑板车车道识别与自动配置方案可行，但其安全与信息安全问题不容忽视，需要并行开展安全约束设计与安全防护原型研究。

Abstract: The authors present a prototype of an electric scooter that uses computer vision to determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. Scooters steer on sidewalks, bike paths, or car lanes. The government imposes speed limits on the lanes in which electric scooters drive. Featuring computer vision, electric scooters can determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. However, such systems' safety is still a concern, and research into such vehicle systems' safety and security aspects is proceeding. First, we will present our vehicular setup. Then, we will present our implemented scooter with safety constraints. Finally, we present our security prototype for such an electric scooter.

</details>


### [80] [SG-ODOM: Event-Driven Proprioceptive State Estimation with Stability-Gated Contact Updates for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20710)
*Francesco Iotti,Chiyun Noh,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-ODOM 提出一种基于稳定性门控接触选择的本体感知里程计框架，仅在足端达到稳定支撑条件后才用运动学信息校正状态，在多种四足与人形平台上取得有竞争力或更优的精度，户外序列 RPE 最高降低 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人本体感知状态估计通常融合 IMU 与腿部编码器的运动学信息，而后者假设支撑相内足端静止。但物理触地并不等于提供静止里程计参考，尤其在冲击与载荷转移阶段；在同一接触相位内反复施加运动学校正会引入冗余或不可靠信息。

Method: 提出 SG-ODOM 框架，采用稳定性门控的接触选择来调度运动学更新：物理触地后，稳定性门仅在足端达到足够稳定的支撑条件时才接受该接触用于状态估计；通过校验的触地事件触发一次估计器校正，另外设置超时更新以限制 IMU 连续递推的最长时间。

Result: 在多种四足与人形机器人平台上与代表性 SOTA 本体感知估计器对比，估计精度具竞争力或更优；在 GrandTour 数据集的户外序列上，相对基线 RPE 最多降低 25%。

Conclusion: 并非所有触地都应立即用于运动学校正，通过稳定性门控筛选有效接触并结合超时机制限制递推长度，可有效提升足式机器人本体感知里程计的可靠性；代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Reliable proprioceptive state estimation for legged robots fuses inertial sensing with kinematic sensing from leg encoders. The latter assumes a foot is stationary during the stance phase; however, physical foot touchdown does not necessarily provide a stationary reference for odometry, particularly during impact and load transfer. Repeatedly applying kinematic corrections throughout the same contact phase can introduce redundant or unreliable information. We present SG-ODOM, a proprioceptive odometry framework that uses stability-gated contact selection to schedule kinematic updates. Following physical touchdown, a stability gate accepts the contact for state estimation only after the foot reaches a sufficiently stable support condition. The resulting validated touchdown event triggers an estimator correction, while additional timeout updates bound the maximum duration of uninterrupted IMU propagation. The framework is evaluated on multiple legged platforms, both quadrupedal and humanoid robots, against representative state-of-the-art proprioceptive estimators. Across the evaluated sequences, SG-ODOM demonstrates competitive or superior estimation accuracy, reducing Relative Pose Error (RPE) by up to 25% compared to the baseline estimator on outdoor sequences from the GrandTour dataset. Source code: https://github.com/ori-drs/SG-ODOM. Video: https://youtu.be/-583_RyuhqQ

</details>


### [81] [TaF-Foundation: Wrench-Grounded Cross-Sensor Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20718)
*Jiaming Jiang,Yuzhe Huang,Baokun Yang,Hao Liang,Li Gao,Haoran Guo,Pei Lin,Shengcheng Luo,Chenxi Xiao,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文以六维力/力矩（wrench）为共同物理锚点，构建跨传感器触觉表示，采集超1亿条配对触觉-力数据，训练共享触觉编码器，并提出TaF-Policy用于操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对接触密集型操作至关重要，但不同传感器的传感机制与观测格式异构，导致跨硬件迁移困难。作者认为物理接触由牛顿力学支配，因此可用统一的物理量作为跨传感机制的共同参照。

Method: 1）将所有传感器的触觉观测与wrench测量配对，实现共享物理监督，无需传感器间直接触觉-触觉配对；2）搭建专用采集平台，从涵盖四类传感原理的24种传感器收集超1亿条配对数据；3）框架结合传感器特定输入适配与共享触觉编码器，通过重构wrench使编码器把不同传感器观测映射到基于接触物理的统一连续特征空间；4）提出TaF-Policy，将触觉特征与视觉观测、机器人状态融合以生成动作块（action chunks）。

Result: 在核心基准上，单帧力RMSE较最强基线降低17.1%，跨传感器检索R@1提升13.11个百分点；操作实验中，TaF-Policy在三个仿真任务上平均成功率51.33%，四个真实任务上46.25%，而最强基线在相同任务集上分别为47.33%和23.75%。评估涵盖wrench估计、跨传感器检索、对未见传感器的少样本适应与机器人操作。

Conclusion: 以wrench为物理基础的触觉表示可实现跨传感机制的统一特征空间，并在力估计、跨传感器检索与少样本适配上取得提升；结合视觉与状态后，仿真提升有限而真实任务成功率显著提高，表明该表示对真实操作更具价值。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for contact-rich manipulation, yet heterogeneous sensing mechanisms and observation formats hinder cross-hardware transfer. Since physical contact is governed by Newtonian mechanics, we ground tactile representations in six-dimensional wrenches, establishing a common physical reference across sensing mechanisms. Specifically, we pair tactile observations from all sensors with wrench measurements, enabling shared physical supervision without requiring direct tactile-to-tactile pairing between sensors. To support learning at scale, we develop a dedicated acquisition platform and collect over 100 million paired tactile observations and wrench measurements from 24 sensors spanning four families of sensing principles. Building on this dataset, our framework combines sensor-specific input adaptation with a shared tactile encoder. By learning to reconstruct wrenches, the encoder maps observations from different sensors into a shared, continuous feature space grounded in contact physics. To leverage these representations for manipulation, we introduce TaF-Policy, which integrates tactile features with visual observations and robot states to generate action chunks. We evaluate the learned representations through wrench estimation, cross-sensor retrieval, few-shot adaptation to unseen sensors, and robotic manipulation. On the core benchmark, our method reduces single-frame force RMSE by 17.1% and improves cross-sensor retrieval R@1 by 13.11 percentage points over the strongest baseline. In manipulation experiments, TaF-Policy achieves average success rates of 51.33% across three simulated tasks and 46.25% across four real-world tasks, compared with 47.33% and 23.75%, respectively, for the strongest baselines evaluated on the same task sets. Project page: https://mrjiangjm.github.io/TaF-Foundation/.

</details>


### [82] [ACiT: Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20719)
*Haixiang Wu,Zhihao Guan,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACiT，将历史本体感觉关节耦合信息作为主条件、视觉作为后期校准，通过三个模块在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实任务上提升成功率，并可迁移至VLA主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略将本体感觉序列与高维视觉信息等同对待进行融合，导致富含动态先验的本体感觉信号被淹没；作者据此追问"等权融合中丢失了什么"，发现关节间强耦合是决定动作的关键结构，并与神经感觉运动环路中"本体感觉先形成身体中心先验、视觉后经顶叶重映射校准"的机制相呼应。

Method: 提出ACiT（Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers），以历史关节耦合为主条件、视觉为后期校准，由三个模块实现：1）Inverted State Encoding，用逐关节token与关节间自注意力构建本体感觉先验；2）State-Visual Grounding，让关节token查询视觉证据，使视觉仅承担目标设定与校准作用；3）Inverted Action Chunking，对每个关节轨迹独立解码。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实世界任务的大多数场景上提升成功率，在需要动力学与协调的任务上增益最大；将同样的条件设计迁移到VLA主干得到pi_0-ACiT，取得显著提升并具备更好的执行效率。

Conclusion: 合理区分本体感觉与视觉的条件角色（先验 vs 校准）而非等权融合，是提升视觉运动策略性能的关键设计原则，且该设计具备跨架构的可迁移性。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies condition action prediction on visual and proprioceptive observations. While historical proprioceptive sequences carry rich dynamic priors, current methods treat them as equal to high-dimensional vision, causing the proprioceptive signal to be overwhelmed. To identify what is lost in equal-conditioning fusion, we analyze proprioceptive data and find that strong inter-joint coupling is the key action-determining structure. This finding aligns with the neural sensorimotor loop, in which proprioception first forms a body-centered prior and vision is later calibrated via parietal remapping. Inspired by this, we propose ACiT, Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers, which reinforces historical joint-coupling as the primary condition with vision as a later calibration. This design is instantiated through three modules: Inverted State Encoding builds a proprioceptive prior via per-joint tokens and inter-joint self-attention. State-Visual Grounding lets joint tokens query visual evidence, making vision goal- and calibration-only. Inverted Action Chunking decodes per-joint trajectories independently. ACiT improves success rates on most LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world tasks, with the largest gains on those that require dynamics and coordination. The same conditioning design transferred to the VLA backbone pi_0-ACiT yields significant gains with better execution efficiency.

</details>


### [83] [AVD: A Multi-Sensor Teleoperated Wheelchair Platform with Safety-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20720)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Boby George*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVD（Autonomous Vehicle Drive）遥控辅助出行架构，用低成本商用硬件统一感知、通信、嵌入控制和远程监督，并以安全层级设计为核心，通过实测量化传感器在恶劣环境与倾斜条件下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动轮椅主要依赖手动摇杆交互，对严重运动和视觉障碍者构成独立出行的关键障碍，而现有低成本平台缺乏对安全权限分配与传感器可靠性缺失程度进行实测验证的系统性工程框架。

Method: 构建由商用硬件组成的低成本遥控原型：采用静态权限层级（手动急停与乘员摇杆优先于远程指令，车载感知层仅具"只停"权限）；计算上划分主功能与安全功能，并设计心跳驱动的故障安全状态切换；对感知栈进行实测表征，包括逐通道采集延迟、覆盖五种目标配置共2025次扫描的LiDAR退化实验（含水雾、扬尘与直接遮挡孔径）以及至47度静态坡度的姿态测量。

Result: 最差环境条件下合并平均仍保留清晰基线前向扇区产出的76.6%，且无任何扫描完全丢失该扇区；记录到一次振动导致惯性单元掉线而LiDAR与相机记录仍持续，体现通道互补性。

Conclusion: 研究给出了可复现的原型与明确验证路径，为可部署的轮椅实现奠定安全设计与证据基础，但当前仍属原型阶段，尚未完成实车部署验证。

Abstract: Electric wheelchairs predominantly rely on manual joystick interfaces, which present critical barriers to independent mobility for individuals with severe motor and visual impairments. This paper presents AVD (Autonomous Vehicle Drive), a dependable teleoperation architecture for assistive mobility that unifies perception, communication, embedded control, and remote supervision in a single low-cost platform built entirely from commercially available hardware. The central engineering contribution is a safety-oriented design framework: control authority follows a static hierarchy, a manual emergency stop and the occupant's joystick pre-empt remote commands, and the on-board sensing layer has stop-only authority. The compute design partitions primary and safety functions and specifies a heartbeat-driven safe-state transition for the fault cases considered. The sensing stack this framework depends on is characterized against measured data: per-channel acquisition latency, a 2,025-scan LiDAR degradation campaign spanning five target configurations and a control under water mist, airborne dust, and direct aperture occlusion, and attitude measurements across static inclines to 47 degrees. The lowest pooled environmental-condition mean retained 76.6% of the clear-baseline forward-sector yield, and no scan lost the sector completely. A logged vibration-induced inertial dropout occurred while LiDAR and camera logging continued, illustrating complementary channel behavior. The result is a documented prototype and an explicit validation path for a deployable wheelchair implementation.

</details>


### [84] [Selective Sensor Acquisition for Multimodal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20730)
*Margaux Edwards,Tobias Fischer,Michael Milford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"选择性、可控的传感器采集"框架：用户给定第二传感器的目标使用率，系统仅在主传感器估计性能足够差时才采集附加传感器，并通过在线阈值调整满足该使用率，无需针对模态/方法/环境标定。多数据集实验表明，使用第二传感器25%查询可恢复55%的可用性能增益，50%查询可恢复81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态地点识别（PR）虽能提升鲁棒性，但现有系统对每个查询都刚性读取并处理所有传感器，即使主传感器已足够。这带来能耗、第二传感器更新率受限及被动隐身等特定领域问题，因此需要按需、可控制的第二传感器采集机制。

Method: 提出选择性可控传感器采集：以主传感器匹配分数的估计性能为判据，仅当性能足够差时采集附加传感器；采用在线阈值调整，根据近期主传感器表现自适应调节，使实际使用率逼近用户指定值。方法只要求PR方法输出匹配分数，适用于单帧与序列式PR，且与传感器模态、PR方法、环境无关。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种PR描述子与传感器组合上，使用第二传感器于25%查询时恢复55%的可用PR性能增益，50%查询时恢复81%。此外还分析比较了其他采集决策机制与使用率控制方法，并考察了第二传感器强于、相当于或弱于主传感器时的表现。

Conclusion: 通过对第二传感器进行按需、可控制的使用率调度，可在显著降低采集开销的同时获得大部分多模态融合带来的性能增益，且该框架具有良好的通用性，无需针对具体模态、方法或环境进行标定。

Abstract: Multimodal Place Recognition (PR) can improve robustness, but most systems rigidly read and process every sensor for every query, even when the primary sensor is sufficient, with implications for energy consumption, second sensor update rate, and domain-specific considerations such as passive stealth. We introduce selective, controllable sensor acquisition, where a user specifies a desired second sensor usage rate and the system acquires the additional sensor only when the primary sensor's estimated performance is sufficiently poor. An online threshold adjustment achieves the requested usage rate by adapting to recent primary sensor performance, directing additional sensing towards queries where fusion is most likely to help without calibration specific to sensor modality, PR method, or environment. The approach requires only a PR method that produces a match score and applies to both single-frame and sequence-based PR. Our system, across multiple datasets, PR descriptors, and sensor combinations, recovers 55% of the available PR performance gain while using the second sensor on only 25% of queries, and 81% while using it on 50%. We further analyse alternative acquisition decision mechanisms and usage-rate control methods, and examine performance when the second sensor is stronger than, comparable to, or weaker than the primary sensor.

</details>


### [85] [Robotic Reward Models in the Foundation-Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20741)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Shangchen Zhou,Wenzheng Zeng,Yanzhe Chen,Zechen Bai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述将114种机器人奖励模型方法按输出所回答的问题分为五类（结果、绝对进展、相对、时间价值、混合），并提出RRM-Bench-1K基准（1117段真实机器人闭环视频及四类对照套件），统一评测27个评测器，指出奖励模型保真度需求应取决于其驱动的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型（RRM）用基础模型替代逐任务奖励工程，实现可复用、以任务为条件的评估。但同名方法下输出实际回答的问题各不相同（任务是否成功 vs 哪次执行更好），且现有评测实践（与示范顺序的一致性、下游策略增益）无法说明奖励是否真正跟踪任务完成度，缺乏统一的语义分类与可靠的评测手段。

Method: 1）按原生输出回答的问题构建五类语义分类体系，梳理114种方法；2）在此框架下分析模型设计如何决定评测器的可判断范围、原始经验如何转化为监督信号、现有评估实践如何检验结果；3）构建RRM-Bench-1K：1117段真实机器人闭环执行视频，带任务状态与相机证据标签，并设计四个对照套件以将"被记功的条件"与其常见线索分离；4）对27个评测器进行统一研究；5）开源RRM-Hub工具箱，支持14种现有RRM的即用与新评测器在同一能力维度上的测试。

Result: 最强评测器的最终得分足以支持对整段执行进行排序（用于选择与过滤）；但稠密信用（dense credit）更贴近执行的时间进程而非任务状态；且没有任何评测器能在决定性视角缺失时发出信号。

Conclusion: 奖励模型所需的保真度取决于其所驱动的决策。未来方向包括从真实失败中获取监督信号，以及设计能将任务状态与时间顺序分离的度量指标。

Abstract: Robotic reward models (RRMs) use foundation models to replace per-task reward engineering with reusable, task-conditioned evaluation. Yet under one name, their outputs answer different questions, from whether a task succeeded to which execution is better. This survey organizes 114 methods into five semantic families by the question their native outputs answer: Outcome, Absolute Progress, Relative, Temporal Value, and Hybrid. Within this taxonomy, we review how model design determines what an evaluator can judge, how raw experience becomes supervision, and how existing evaluation practice tests the result. The review exposes a gap: agreement with demonstration order and downstream policy gains do not show whether a reward tracks task fulfillment. We therefore introduce RRM-Bench-1K, 1,117 real-robot videos of closed-loop executions with task-state and camera-evidence labels, and four contrast suites that separate the credited condition from its usual cues. A unified study of 27 evaluators shows that the strongest final scores rank whole executions well enough for selection and filtering. Dense credit, however, follows the progression of an execution more closely than its task state, and no evaluator signals when the decisive view is missing. How much fidelity a reward model needs therefore depends on the decision it drives. We outline directions from real-failure supervision to metrics that separate task state from chronology, and open-source RRM-Hub, a toolbox in which 14 existing RRMs can be readily applied and new evaluators tested on the same capabilities.

</details>


### [86] [Non-Imaging Optical Tactile Sensors for Robotics: Transduction Principles, System Integration, and Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20770)
*Hao Wu,Yu Feng,Haotian Guo,Huichan Zhao,Changsheng Wu,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述提出一个统一框架，把机械载荷传递、光学编码、读出与触觉推断同机器人任务需求联系起来，系统梳理了反射式与波导式器件、微/纳米光纤、光纤光栅、干涉与谐振结构以及力致发光复合材料等非成像光学触觉传感器，并指出高光学灵敏度只有在目标变量可辨识时才有效、空间覆盖与反馈时序须在系统层面评估、器件表征/离线识别/闭环性能属于不同层级的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非成像光学触觉传感器通过光强、波长、光谱或光子计数变化编码接触，架构多样；但其实用性取决于能否在有耦合机械与环境效应的情况下可靠区分接触变量，以及能否及时输出信息以指导动作。现有研究分散，缺少将物理机制与机器人任务要求贯通的统一评估视角。

Method: 综述性分析：构建覆盖"机械载荷传递—光学编码—读出—触觉推断—任务需求"的统一框架；按器件类型分类考察反射式与波导器件、微/纳米光纤、光纤光栅、干涉与谐振结构、力致发光复合材料；并从变量可辨识性、系统级空间覆盖与时序（复用、采集、解调、推断）、以及证据层级（器件表征、离线识别、闭环机器人性能）三个维度进行对比与评判。

Result: 归纳出三点核心认识：其一，高光学灵敏度并不能单独保证准确触觉估计，需在相关接触与标定条件下保证目标变量可辨识；其二，空间覆盖与反馈时序必须系统级评估，涵盖复用、采集、解调与推断全链路；其三，器件表征、离线识别与闭环机器人性能提供不同强度的证据，需采用不同评价标准。代表性研究展示了这些考量如何影响力估计、接触定位、滑移修正与分布式交互等任务表现。

Conclusion: 未来优先方向包括标定迁移、可靠接口、可扩展读出与基于任务的验证；该框架为面向机器人应用选择和开发非成像光学触觉架构提供了依据。

Abstract: Non-imaging optical tactile sensors encode contact through changes in optical intensity, wavelength, spectra, or photon counts, offering diverse architectures for robotic touch. Their usefulness depends on whether contact variables can be reliably distinguished from coupled mechanical and environmental effects, and whether the resulting information can be delivered in time to guide action. This review develops a unified framework linking mechanical load transmission, optical encoding, readout, and tactile inference to robotic task requirements. It examines reflective and waveguide-based devices, micro/nanofibers, fiber gratings, interferometric and resonant structures, and mechanoluminescent composites. Three considerations emerge across these approaches. First, high optical sensitivity supports accurate tactile estimation only when target variables remain identifiable under the relevant contact and calibration conditions. Second, spatial coverage and feedback timing must be assessed at the system level, accounting for multiplexing, acquisition, demodulation, and inference. Third, device characterization, offline recognition, and closed-loop robotic performance provide distinct levels of evidence and require different evaluation criteria. Representative studies illustrate how these considerations shape force estimation, contact localization, slip correction and distributed interaction. This review concludes with priorities for calibration transfer, reliable interfaces, scalable readout, and task-based validation, providing a basis for selecting and developing non-imaging optical tactile architectures for robotics.

</details>


### [87] [QTOP: Scaling Humanoid Motion Tracking with Quantile Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20791)
*Markus Peschl,Dana Kianfar,Daniel Dijkman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantile Tracking Optimization（QTOP），通过引入以状态-参考对为条件的分布式优势估计来缓解多样动作带来的学习干扰，从而提升动作追踪强化学习的样本效率，并在仿真和真实场景中性能领先基线最高 10%，同时给出其缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 RL 的人体动作追踪是人形全身控制近期的核心能力，但要高保真泛化到未见动作，需要同时把训练规模扩展到数千 GPU 小时与数据集小时，代价高昂。作者假设动作追踪本质上是计算受限（compute-bound）的，因为多样化动作训练会带来类似多任务 RL 的学习干扰。

Method: 提出 QTOP（Quantile Tracking Optimization）。核心是引入分位数（分布式）优势估计，并将其条件化于状态与参考动作对，以更精细地区分不同参考下的价值分布，从而提升样本效率。

Result: 在多个数据集的仿真与真实世界追踪场景中，QTOP 在 on-policy 与 off-policy 的 RL 基线上均有提升，最高约 10%；同时建立了 QTOP 的缩放规律，发现除可预测的缩放之外，在中等 GPU 小时范围内数据集规模对性能影响很小。

Conclusion: 将动作追踪视为计算受限问题并以分布式、条件化优势估计应对学习干扰是有效的；QTOP 兼具更高样本效率与可预测的缩放行为，且在中规模算力下数据规模并非主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning human motion tracking policies through reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a core capability that underpins a majority of recent progress in humanoid whole-body control. However, achieving generalization to unseen motions at high fidelity requires jointly scaling training to thousands of GPU and dataset hours. This work hypothesizes that motion tracking is inherently compute-bound because training on a diverse set of motions introduces learning interference, similar to multi-task RL. To tackle this, we propose Quantile Tracking Optimization (QTOP), which improves sample efficiency by introducing a distributional advantage estimate conditioned on state and reference pairs. QTOP outperforms on and off-policy RL baselines both in simulation and real-world tracking scenarios on multiple datasets by up to 10\%. Furthermore, we establish a scaling law for QTOP and find that, aside from scaling predictably, dataset size has minimal impact on performance for moderately sized GPU hour regimes.

</details>


### [88] [Driving the Style: Velocity-Conditioned Whole-Body Control from Motion Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20888)
*Murad Dawood,Oezge Drama Berkenkamp,Antonio Gonzales Marin,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将全身跟踪奖励用于训练人形机器人策略，但把actor限制为紧凑的locomotion命令与机器人观测输入；参考动作仅用于监督奖励和特权critic，无需在运行时提供参考轨迹，也无需教师蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：对抗模仿（AMP）能提供风格信号但难以迁移到硬件；全身跟踪可可靠部署并忠实模仿，但actor以未来参考为条件，运行需参考轨迹，且当动作偏离训练数据时易失败。

Method: 用全身跟踪奖励直接训练人形策略；actor输入仅为紧凑locomotion命令和机器人观测；参考动作仅监督奖励函数与特权critic，不向actor提供参考关节轨迹，也不使用教师蒸馏，使示范风格通过跟踪奖励隐式编码进策略。

Result: 仅用一条随机行走动作训练、并用留出动作的速度曲线下达命令：仿真中成功率至少98%，在Unitree G1上全部试验完成。留出慢速行走测试中，全身跟踪成功率降至2%，命令条件蒸馏为56%，二者在真机均失败。在训练动作上，本方法关节位置误差保持在全身跟踪的8%以内，风格覆盖率高于AMP，而AMP在无安全吊架的真机上倒地。

Conclusion: 参考动作只用于监督奖励与critic即可让actor在紧凑命令下复现全身风格，兼顾了全身跟踪的部署可靠性与AMP的风格表现，并在真机上实现了更鲁棒的泛化。

Abstract: Achieving human-like behavior on humanoid robots has advanced rapidly by leveraging motion-capture demonstrations rather than hand-crafted cost functions. Adversarial imitation supplies a style signal that lets a policy fulfill a task while adhering to a desired style, but is difficult to transfer to hardware. Whole-body tracking through reinforcement learning instead deploys reliably and imitates demonstrations faithfully, but the resulting policies are conditioned on the future reference, so they require a reference trajectory at runtime and can fail when the motion differs from the training data. We train a humanoid policy directly with whole-body tracking rewards while restricting its actor to compact locomotion commands and robot observations. Reference motion supervises the reward and privileged critic, without supplying reference joint trajectories to the actor or requiring teacher distillation. The demonstrated style is thereby encoded in the policy through the tracking reward, so the robot reproduces the whole-body style under commanded velocities. Trained on a single random-walk motion and commanded with velocity profiles from held-out motions, our policy reaches at least 98\% success in simulation and completes every trial on the Unitree G1. On held-out slow walking, whole-body tracking drops to 2\% success and command-conditioned distillation to 56\%, and both fail on the real robot. On the trained motions, the joint position error of our policy stays within 8\% of whole-body tracking, and it has a higher style coverage than adversarial motion priors (AMP), which fell on the real robot without a safety crane.

</details>


### [89] [Transferable Neural CBFs via Generalized Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20929)
*Nishant Jayesh Bhave,Parthib Khound*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究单个神经控制屏障函数（CBF）能否通过把状态表示为相对各障碍物的坐标而跨环境复用，并提出广义闭包条件、提升定理与秩检验，用于判定相对坐标是否足以支持安全集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CBF需手工设计，对欠驱动载具困难且安全集过于保守；神经CBF可从数据学习更有信息量的屏障，但通常仅针对某一固定障碍物构型验证，难以复用。相对坐标有望实现跨障碍物迁移，然而其可能丢失预测自身演化所需的信息，这一缺口缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 提出广义闭包条件，将相对坐标中缺失的信息显式表示为残差；给出提升定理，把经单环境验证的相对坐标屏障迁移到一族障碍物上；并设计秩检验，识别仅靠对称性无法为迁移提供依据的情形。在双积分器、平面飞行器与12状态四旋翼上进行验证。

Result: 不满足秩检验的坐标会导致障碍物穿透，或在悬停附近使屏障条件失效；而机体坐标系对四旋翼精确满足闭包条件。所得屏障无需重新训练即可迁移到新地图，并在所报告的部署与快速起飞实验中有效。

Conclusion: 相对坐标下的神经CBF可跨障碍物复用，但前提是满足所提出的广义闭包条件并通过秩检验；机体坐标系对四旋翼是合适选择，为安全约束的跨环境迁移提供了可验证的理论依据。

Abstract: Classical control barrier functions (CBFs) provide safety constraints for controllers, but designing them by hand is difficult for underactuated vehicles and the resulting safe sets can be overly conservative. Neural CBFs can learn more informative barriers from data, yet a learned barrier is usually verified only for one fixed obstacle configuration. We study whether one neural barrier can be reused across environments by expressing the state relative to each obstacle. The key gap is that these relative coordinates may discard information needed to predict their own evolution. We give a generalized closure condition that represents this missing information as a residual and a lifting theorem that transfers a verified relative-coordinate barrier to every obstacle in a family. A rank test also identifies when symmetry alone cannot justify the transfer. We validate the conditions on a double integrator, a planar aircraft, and a 12-state quadrotor. Coordinates that fail the test lead to obstacle penetration or to a barrier condition that fails near hover, whereas body-frame coordinates close exactly for the quadrotor. The resulting barrier transfers to new maps without retraining and is effective in the reported deployment and fast-start experiments.

</details>


### [90] [TacBridge: Learning Unified Tactile Representations through Cross-Sensor Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20939)
*Yongji Fu,Yijiong Lin,Yi Zhou,Yi Qi,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacBridge 通过跨传感器对应关系学习多传感器触觉视频的统一表征，以缓解不同光学触觉传感器间的观察差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GelSight、DIGIT、GelSlim、TacTip 等光学触觉传感器在成像几何、背景外观、光照、分辨率和形变响应上存在差异，导致同一接触事件产生传感器相关的观测结果，从而限制了表征迁移与多传感器数据的联合使用。

Method: 提出 TacBridge 框架：先进行多传感器掩码触觉视频预训练，学习跨传感器的时空表征；再利用匹配接触事件的成对源/目标视频，将同一掩码模型联合训练于同传感器重建与跨传感器重建任务上。

Result: 在跨传感器转换与下游感知任务上的实验表明，所学触觉表征具备跨传感器泛化能力与任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于跨传感器对应的掩码视频预训练可有效构建统一触觉表征，为多传感器触觉数据的联合利用与表征迁移提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Optical tactile sensors record tactile videos that capture local deformation and its temporal evolution during contact, supporting robot manipulation, force estimation, and tactile representation learning. Differences in imaging geometry, background appearance, illumination, resolution, and deformation response lead to sensor-dependent observations of the same contact across GelSight, DIGIT, GelSlim, TacTip, and other optical tactile sensors. This variability limits representation transfer and the joint use of multi-sensor data. We introduce TacBridge, a framework for learning unified representations from multi-sensor tactile videos through cross-sensor correspondence. Specifically, multi-sensor masked tactile-video pre-training learns spatiotemporal representations across sensors. We then train the same masked model jointly on same-sensor and cross-sensor reconstruction, using paired source and target videos of matched contact events. Experiments on cross-sensor translation and downstream perception demonstrate the cross-sensor generalization and task transferability of the learned tactile representations.

</details>


### [91] [ROSE: A Shared Cross-Embodiment Facial-Motion Latent Space for Humanoid Robot Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20950)
*Yongji Fu,Man Cao,Yi Zhou,Fei Lin,Mark F. Hansen,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROSE框架，将仿生硬件、跨形态运动表征学习与多模态运动生成整合，使紧凑仿人头部机器人实现自然连贯的全脸动态表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人机器人表情系统通常只能实现有限的唇部运动或预设脚本动作；物理上紧凑头部难以容纳密集独立的驱动单元，算法上"渲染-反演"式视觉重定向存在延迟与伪影，且难以可靠迁移到实体机器人。

Method: 硬件上采用柔性腱鞘传动结构，在19.5 cm仿生头部内集成34个驱动器，通过解耦驱动布置与面部动作点实现紧凑体积内的全脸协调表情；算法上ROSE-VAE从人类、数字人和机器人观测中学习身份无关的共享面部运动隐空间，ROSE-Flow直接将文本、音频或其组合映射到该运动空间轨迹（不以视频作为生成输入），ROSE-ACT再将轨迹转换为受约束的驱动器指令。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅说明系统展示了高密度仿生硬件与共享可执行面部运动表征如何共同统一跨形态学习、多模态生成与实体机器人表情。

Conclusion: 高密度仿生硬件与共享可执行面部运动表征的结合，为跨形态学习、多模态生成和实体机器人表情提供了统一框架，避免了渲染-反演路径的延迟与伪影。

Abstract: Natural, coherent full-face dynamic expressions are central to making humanoid robots socially approachable, yet existing systems commonly realize only limited lip motion or scripted expressions. The limitation is both physical and algorithmic. A compact head leaves little room for densely packed, independently controllable actuation; meanwhile, learned systems often generate an avatar video, landmarks, or facial parameters and then infer actuator commands through visual retargeting. This render-and-invert path adds latency and artifacts, and it does not reliably transfer generated behavior to a physical robot. We present ROSE (RObot facial expression through Shared motion Embeddings), a framework that integrates biomimetic hardware, cross-embodiment motion representation learning, and multimodal motion generation. On the hardware side, we build a flexible tendon-sheath transmission structure that integrates 34 actuators into a 19.5 cm biomimetic head. By decoupling actuator placement from facial action points, it enables coordinated full-face expression within a compact volume. On the algorithmic side, ROSE-VAE learns an identity-independent shared facial-motion latent space from human, digital-human, and robot observations. ROSE-Flow directly maps text, audio, or their combination to trajectories in this motion space without using video as a generator input. ROSE-ACT then converts these trajectories into constrained actuator commands. Our system demonstrates how high-density biomimetic hardware and a shared executable facial-motion representation can jointly unify cross-embodiment learning, multimodal generation, and physical robot expression.

</details>


### [92] [Inverse Kinematics in Endoscopic Scenarios using MoveIt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20978)
*Marc Bestmann,Timon Engelke,Rebecca Rodeck,Gerko Wende*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对现有逆运动学（IK）求解器进行基准测试，并比较多种实用改进方案；将 IK 问题拆分为两部分可获得最佳性能，在内窥镜水箱场景中实现 100 ms 内 100% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学在许多机器人应用中至关重要，但基于 MoveIt 的标准求解器在狭窄受限场景中表现不佳，因此需要系统评估现有求解器并寻找实用的改进手段。

Method: 对现有可用 IK 求解器开展基准测试与对比；在此基础上评估多种实用修改方案，其中包括将 IK 问题拆分为两个子问题进行求解。

Result: 将 IK 问题拆分为两部分的方法性能最佳，在内窥镜水箱场景中于 100 ms 内达到 100% 的成功率。

Conclusion: 针对受限场景下的 IK 求解，问题分解式策略优于其他实用修改方案，可显著提升求解成功率与实时性，适合狭窄环境中的应用。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics are critical in many robotic applications, yet standard MoveIt-based solvers struggle in confined scenarios. This paper provides a benchmark of the available solvers and compares multiple practical modifications for improving the performance of the solvers. Splitting the IK problem into two parts results in the best performance, leading to a 100% success rate under 100 ms in an endoscopic tank scenario.

</details>


### [93] [FlowRow: Learning Navigation Behaviors in Agricultural Environments with Long-Horizon Goal-Conditioned Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21035)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FlowRow——基于流匹配的局部规划器，将半结构化农业环境中的跨行导航建模为目标条件引导的 SE(2) 路径分布生成，并发布 RowNavBench 基准（900 个 Isaac Sim 片段，覆盖葡萄园、柠檬园和苹果园）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园、葡萄园等半结构化农业环境中的自主监测与干预需要机器人在行内行驶、行端转向与跳行、跨行穿越等多种行为间自由切换。现有基于学习的行内导航虽已较鲁棒，但跨行切换通常依赖先验的田间几何信息来指定和调参行间过渡动作，无法实现任意行间转移。

Method: 将问题重构为以相对机器人表达的长时域目标为条件的目标导向导航任务：由规划器结合该目标与机器人感知观测生成本地路径。FlowRow 采用流匹配（flow matching）建模多样化农业导航行为在 SE(2) 上的路径分布，输入为长时域目标与深度感知。同时构建 RowNavBench 基准，基于 Isaac Sim，包含 900 个回合，覆盖葡萄园、柠檬园与苹果园，并变化行长、行距与株距、植株位置及杂草生长。

Result: FlowRow 成功率达 87%，而在相同演示数据上重新训练的最强生成式行为克隆基线仅为 44%。

Conclusion: 以长时域相对目标为条件、用流匹配生成 SE(2) 局部路径的方式可有效实现任意行间转移的农业导航，显著优于生成式行为克隆基线；基准、数据与代码将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: Autonomous monitoring and intervention in semi-structured agricultural environments, such as orchards and vineyards, require mobile robots to navigate to locations across diverse field configurations. This entails performing navigation behaviors including in-row driving, row turning and skipping, and inter-row traversal. Recent learning-based approaches have enabled robust in-row navigation from onboard perception, but navigation across rows typically requires a priori knowledge of field geometry to specify and tune row-transition maneuvers, preventing arbitrary transitions between rows. We address this challenge by framing navigation in these environments as a goal-directed navigation task, where a long-horizon goal is expressed relative to the robot and a planner generates local paths based on the goal and the robot's perceptual observations. We present FlowRow, a flow-matching-based local planner which models the distribution of diverse agricultural navigation behaviors as $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ paths using the long-horizon goal and depth perception. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce RowNavBench, a navigation benchmark for semi-structured agricultural environments developed in Isaac Sim, comprising 900 episodes across grape vineyards, lemon orchards, and apple orchards with varying row lengths, row and plant spacings, plant positions, and weed growth. FlowRow achieves 87% success, compared to 44% for the strongest generative behavior cloning baseline retrained on the same demonstrations. We will release our benchmark, data, and code upon acceptance.

</details>


### [94] [WiTac: Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21057)
*Haotian He,Yiyang Chen,Congsheng Xu,Wenchao Yue,Mingze Ye,Shuai Chen,Yi Yang,Yaping Li,Haonan Wang,Weinan E,Wenzhao Lian,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WiTac提出一种融合触觉信号、手部运动与腕部视角的预训练触觉编码器，将接触表征注入下游VLA策略后，在四项富接触操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似的触觉接触因手部、物体与目标构型不同而需要不同纠正，因此仅赋予触觉+腕部视觉分别输入的方式不足；只有在策略注入前把二者深度融合，才能让触觉特征扎根于视觉语境，获得更准确的接触表征。

Method: 提出Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations（WiTac）：在动作生成之前先学习的预训练编码器，在近300小时SharpaDex数据上预训练，将触觉信号与手部运动、腕部视角深度联合融合为综合接触表征；随后以该表征作为条件注入下游VLA策略（GR00T N1.7与π0.5）。

Result: 在四项富接触操作任务上，相比纯视觉基座策略平均成功率提升+28.8%（GR00T N1.7）与+15.0%（π0.5）；相比无腕部视觉的触觉编码器分别再提升18.8%与7.5%。离线分析显示：腕部视觉的引入带来更准确的接触描述子、更强的任务—接触联合判别能力、更强触觉噪声下的特征稳定性，以及从相似接触中读出更清晰的动作方向。

Conclusion: 在策略注入前将触觉与腕部视觉深度融合是有效路径：WiTac证明腕部语境能显著提升接触表征质量与下游操作性能，且离线指标与在线成功率变化相互印证。

Abstract: Similar tactile contacts can call for different corrections depending on the configuration of the hand, object and target. Rather than supplying touch and wrist vision separately, fusing them before policy injection better grounds tactile features in visual context, yielding more accurate contact representations. We introduce Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations (WiTac), a pretrained encoder that learns such representations before action generation. Pretrained on nearly 300 hours of SharpaDex data, WiTac deeply fuses tactile signals with hand motion and wrist views into a comprehensive contact representation. Conditioning downstream VLA policies (GR00T N1.7 and $π_{0.5}$) on WiTac substantially improves task success over vision-only base policies across four contact-rich manipulation tasks (+28.8% and +15.0% on average), and further outperforms a wrist-free tactile encoder by 18.8% and 7.5%, respectively. Offline analyses further show that incorporating wrist vision yields more accurate contact descriptors, stronger joint task-contact discrimination, greater feature stability under tactile noise, and clearer action-direction readout from similar contacts.Project page: https://wrist-informed-tactile-representations.github.io/.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-CONDOR: Learning Multistable Motion Primitives from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21059)
*Gabriele Giuseppe Di Marzo,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Maximilian Stölzle,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 multi-CONDOR 框架，用于学习具有多个离散吸引子或连续吸引目标轮廓的动力学运动基元，并给出收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作任务存在多个不同目标或连续可接受结果集，现有基于动力系统的运动策略难以在统一、连续且自主的动力学框架中同时兼顾这两种目标结构。

Method: 以 CONDOR 为基础构建 multi-CONDOR，将模仿学习与损失函数结合，促使学习到的任务动力学与编码目标吸引结构的潜在参考系统保持一致；并对所学运动动力学的收敛性进行基于 Lyapunov 的分析。

Result: 仿真中相比由状态机选择的单目标运动基元，轨迹 RMSE 降低约 72%；在双目标和三目标任务中，将初始状态落在目标容差外的比例从 StableNODE 的 0.31% 和 7.29% 均降至 0%；Franka Emika 机械臂实验实现了从示教学习、经任一容器把手成功抓取。

Conclusion: multi-CONDOR 在保持运动复现能力的同时，具备收敛到多种有效结果的灵活性，统一支持离散多目标与连续目标轮廓两类结构。

Abstract: Dynamical-system-based motion policies provide a framework for learning reactive robot motions with stability and convergence properties. Many manipulation tasks, however, admit several distinct goals or a continuous set of acceptable outcomes, requiring motion primitives that preserve these alternatives. Accommodating both goal structures within a unified framework with continuous, autonomous dynamics remains an open challenge. We propose a CONDOR-based framework, multi-CONDOR, for learning motion primitives with either multiple discrete attractors or a continuous attracting goal contour. The approach combines imitation learning with losses that encourage agreement between the learned task dynamics and latent reference systems encoding the desired attraction structure. We provide a Lyapunov-based analysis of the convergence properties of the learned motion dynamics. We evaluate the framework on simulated benchmarks with discrete goals, qualitative examples with continuous goal contours, and robotic grasping experiments. In simulation, multi-CONDOR reduces trajectory RMSE by approximately 72% compared with separately learned single-goal motion primitives selected by a state machine. For tasks with two and three goals, respectively, it reduces the fraction of evaluated initial states ending outside the goal tolerance from StableNODE's 0.31% and 7.29% to 0% in both settings. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot arm demonstrate successful grasping of a container by either of its two handles using motions learned from demonstrations. Together, these results show multi-CONDOR combines motion reproduction with the flexibility to converge to alternative valid outcomes.

</details>


### [96] [CoopDistill: Equilibrium-Guided Multi-Agent Policy Distillation for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21069)
*Shiyu Fang,Sheng Zhao,Jiarui Zhang,Chengkai Xu,Xiaocong Zhao,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoopDistill——一种由势博弈(PG)引导的多智能体策略蒸馏框架，将PG固定路径纵向参考与残差MARL策略结合，并配以硬案例课程与对比式滚动选择器，在汇入、环岛、交叉口场景中显著优于PG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的协同驾驶方法为简化多车规划常固定路径，牺牲空间灵活性；MARL虽通过交互探索替代方案，但协同学习与安全执行仍困难。二者融合面临探索效率、经验分配与执行可靠性三方面挑战。

Method: 1) 以势博弈(PG)给出固定路径的纵向参考；2) 残差MARL策略在其周围学习纵向加速度、冲突接近时机与横向路径修正；3) 硬案例课程将经验集中于PG未能解决或显著延误的交互；4) 对比式滚动选择器在执行前评估学习修正与PG的联合后果并择优执行。

Result: 在汇入、环岛、交叉口三类场景及四种网联自动驾驶渗透率下与六个基线对比：相对PG成功率提升2.1–27.1个百分点，延误降低7.3–27.3%，未观察到碰撞。

Conclusion: 将优化式势博弈与残差MARL蒸馏结合，可在保持安全性的同时提升协同驾驶的成功率与时延表现，验证了"结构化参考+学习修正+滚动比较选择"这一融合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Cooperative driving extends autonomous driving from individual vehicle decisions to joint maneuvers that improve traffic safety and efficiency. Optimization-based methods impose safety constraints but often fix paths to simplify multi-vehicle planning, limiting spatial flexibility. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) explores alternatives through interaction, but coordinated learning and safe execution remain challenging. Their integration nevertheless faces challenges in exploration efficiency, experience allocation, and execution reliability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes CoopDistill, an equilibrium-guided multi-agent policy distillation framework. A potential-game (PG) formulation provides a fixed-path longitudinal reference, around which a residual MARL policy learns longitudinal acceleration, conflict-approach timing, and lateral path corrections. A hard-case curriculum concentrates experience on interactions that PG leaves unresolved or substantially delayed. A comparative rollout selector evaluates the joint consequences of learned corrections against PG before choosing the executed action. Experiments in merging, roundabout, and intersection scenarios compare CoopDistill with six baselines at four connected automated vehicle penetration levels. Relative to PG, success improves by 2.1--27.1 percentage points and delay decreases by 7.3--27.3\%, with no observed collisions. Code is available at https://github.com/FanGShiYuu/CoopDistill

</details>


### [97] [ForceHarness: Turning Force into Action and Physics for Frozen Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21099)
*Ke Sun,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yike Zhang,Yanzhe Chen,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceHarness 让冻结的多模态模型无需演示或微调即可使用接触力完成机器人操作：通过自终止力原语在模型之下闭合力环，并用物理量翻译层把力轨迹转成带不确定性的物理量供模型推理，在真实机械臂上把 GPT-6 Astra 的零样本成功率从 3.3% 提升到 90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像可驱动零样本机器人控制，但许多操作任务依赖图像无法体现的接触力。把力引入仅提供 API 的冻结模型有两大难题：(1) 接触动态远快于 API 模型的决策速度，需在模型下层闭合力环；(2) 冻结模型无法理解原始力信号，需把力转化为可推理的形式。

Method: 提出 ForceHarness 框架：先仅凭腕部传感器或关节力矩校准手部受力；用"自终止力原语"在底层自主调节力并在接触事件时返回，解决快速闭环问题；再用物理翻译层把每条力轨迹转换为带不确定性的物理量作为工具返回值，解决模型不可解释原始力的问题。同时构建 HiddenPhys——含隐藏物理变量的七任务 MuJoCo 基准，用于与专家力控制器、示教学习策略同条件对比；并做消融。

Result: 真实机械臂（无腕部力传感器）上零样本成功率由 3.3% 升至 90.0%；仿真中在公开 ForceBench 上提升 17.5 个百分点，在 HiddenPhys 上提升 34.3 个百分点，两个基准上均超过示教训练的力感知策略，在 HiddenPhys 上与手写专家控制器持平。消融显示较小模型在接收物理量而非原始力数值时决策明显更好。

Conclusion: 力可以通过"模型下层闭环 + 物理量抽象"两个设计被冻结的多模态模型有效利用，无需演示或微调即可获得接近乃至达到专家力控制水平的零样本操作性能；向模型提供物理量而非原始力值是关键。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models can now control robots zero-shot from images, yet many manipulation tasks depend on contact forces that images cannot show. Bringing force to an API-only frozen model raises two challenges. First, contact evolves much faster than an API model can decide, so the system must close the loop on force beneath the model. Second, a frozen model cannot interpret raw force streams, so force must be returned in a form the model can reason about. Therefore, we present ForceHarness, a harness that lets frozen models use force without demonstrations or finetuning. After calibrating hand force from a wrist sensor or joint torques alone, ForceHarness addresses the first challenge with self-terminating force primitives that regulate force on their own and return at a contact event. To address the second challenge, a physics translation layer turns each force trace into physical quantities with uncertainty as the tool result. To evaluate ForceHarness against expert force controllers and learned policies under identical conditions, we also build HiddenPhys, a seven-task MuJoCo benchmark with hidden physical variables. On a real arm without a wrist force sensor, ForceHarness raises the zero-shot success rate of GPT-6 Astra from 3.3% to 90.0%. In simulation, it adds 17.5 percentage points on the public ForceBench suite and 34.3 percentage points on HiddenPhys, surpassing force-aware policies trained on demonstrations on both benchmarks and matching hand-written expert controllers on HiddenPhys. Ablations further show that smaller models make significantly better decisions when given physical quantities rather than raw force values.

</details>


### [98] [ReuseMem: One Memory, Multiple Goals in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21115)
*Yoonseok Kang,Dayeon Jung,Seunghoon Jeon,Wonjib Kim,Dongchul Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReuseMem 学习用同一份视觉记忆支持多种后续操作目标：记录与未来目标无关的视觉特征，按请求检索互补证据并经轻量动作接口与当前场景结合，检索由候选证据对专家动作预测的支持度监督，并在四个仿真任务与两个真实场景中验证了记忆复用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在与人类协作时，观测到的经验往往在观察时用途未知，同一段经历之后可能用于识别物体，也可能用于把物体放回原位。现有方法通常为特定任务构建记忆，难以让一段记忆在场景变化后服务多种新的操作请求，因此需要研究如何用一份共享视觉记忆支撑多样化的后续操作目标。

Method: 方法包含三点：一是以与未来目标无关的方式记录视觉特征，保证记忆的通用性；二是针对每个请求检索互补证据，并通过轻量动作接口与当前场景观测结合；三是用候选证据对不同当前状态下专家动作预测的支持程度来监督检索，把记忆选择与其在操作中的实际用途挂钩。此外，对重复出现的视觉特征只存储一次，并复用动作生成过程中保持不变的内存计算以提升效率。

Result: 在四个仿真任务和两个真实物理场景上的实验表明，同一次记录可被复用于不同请求。在动作适配条件一致的情况下，ReuseMem 在基准任务上的联合目标完成率比均匀使用记忆的基线高出 24.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，单一视觉记忆能够在场景变化时支持多种后续操作目标，无需为每个新请求重新采集或重建记忆。将记忆检索与动作预测用途绑定、并配合存储与计算复用，是实现这一目标的有效途径。

Abstract: Robots that work alongside people should be able to use what they have seen when new requests arise. The same experience may later support identifying an object or returning it to its original location, although these uses were unknown during observation. ReuseMem learns to use one visual memory for multiple later manipulation goals. It records visual features independently of future goals, retrieves complementary evidence for each request, and combines it with the current scene through a lightweight action interface. Retrieval is supervised by how well candidate evidence supports expert-action prediction across different current states, linking memory selection to its use in manipulation. Repeated visual features are stored once, and memory computations that remain unchanged during action generation are reused. Experiments on four simulation tasks and two physical scenarios demonstrate reuse of a common recording across different requests. With matched action adaptation, ReuseMem improves joint goal completion on the benchmark tasks by 24.5 percentage points over uniform memory. These findings show that one memory can support multiple later manipulation goals as the scene changes.

</details>


### [99] [Linglong Agent Service: A Runtime for Centralized Agent Reasoning and Distributed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21129)
*Hua Zhang,Min Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Linglong Agent Service（agentsvc），一个自托管 HTTP 服务与可嵌入 Python 运行时，将"执行站点"与模型、工具、上下文并列为可配置维度；父服务负责推理编排与状态，每次推理请求可选一个子环境执行文件、Shell、终端与 MCP 操作。另提出 online_debugger 作为执行站点分离的进程级特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 已能使用远程工具与云端沙箱，但如何统一整合推理、会话状态与异构执行站点仍是运行时设计难题。同时，进程内的在线调试场景需要让被探测程序自身提交异常证据并在同一会话中完成诊断与恢复。

Method: 将执行站点抽象为可配置维度：父服务保留推理编排、工具描述与对话历史，每个推理请求选择一个子环境承载文件、Shell、终端与 MCP 操作；直接 HTTP 与子端发起的 WebSocket 隧道共享同一应用接口，包括可移植请求上下文与集中管理更新；核心路径不依赖第三方 Python 包（Windows 伪终端为可选依赖）。online_debugger 则让插桩程序提交异常证据，在事故进程内执行会话级检查工具并恢复同一诊断会话，默认提示要求源码修改具备"先失败后通过"的复现证据。设计通过受约束的期望效用推理进行解释，并与 Hermes Agent、Pi、Codex、Claude Code 的具体机制做对比。

Result: 报告了实现与回归测试证据，验证了可执行契约层面的正确性；运行时补丁激活与失败调用恢复尚未实现。作者明确指出当前证据仅确立可执行契约，尚不构成性能优势、修复准确率或生产级恢复能力的证明。代码以 MIT 许可证开源。

Conclusion: 该工作把执行站点提升为一等可配置维度，为跨异构环境的 agent 运行时提供了一种轻量、自托管、无重依赖的设计方案，并通过 online_debugger 展示其在进程内在线调试中的特例形态。其贡献目前限于架构与契约层面，性能与修复效果仍需后续实证。

Abstract: LLM agents can use remote tools and cloud sandboxes, but integrating reasoning, session state, and heterogeneous execution sites remains a runtime design problem. We present Linglong Agent Service (agentsvc), a self-hosted HTTP service and embeddable Python runtime that treats execution site as a configurable dimension alongside model, tools, and context. A parent service retains inference orchestration, tool descriptions, and conversation history; each inference request selects one child environment for file, shell, terminal, and MCP operations. Direct HTTP and child-initiated WebSocket tunnels carry the same application interfaces, including portable request context and centrally managed updates. The normal core path requires no third-party Python packages, with an optional Windows pseudo-terminal dependency. We further present online_debugger as the process-scale special case of execution-site separation: an instrumented program submits exception evidence, executes session-scoped inspection tools inside the incident process, and resumes the same diagnostic session. Its default prompt requires failing-then-passing reproduction evidence for source changes, while runtime patch activation and failed-call recovery remain unimplemented. We explain the design through constrained expected-utility reasoning, compare concrete mechanisms with Hermes Agent, Pi, Codex, and Claude Code, and report implementation and regression-test evidence. The current evidence establishes executable contracts, not performance advantages, repair accuracy, or production recovery. The source code is open source under the MIT license at https://github.com/mz24cn/agents.

</details>


### [100] [WinDEX: Metric 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild for Dexterous Teleoperation and Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21143)
*Hongyu Ma,Hairong Qu,Shiqi Zhao,Yongsong Yang,Kai Shi,Jiahao Ai,Xin You,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WinDEX 提出一套从硬件采集、几何-时序优化标注、端到端鱼眼原生网络到蒸馏部署的完整人手指数据流水线，实现野外大规模度量尺度手部重建，离线 43.61 FPS、因果流式 171 FPS，并显著提升下游强化学习与遥操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类操作数据被广泛用于训练机器人获得类人灵巧性，但低成本、大规模、野外（in the wild）采集仍很困难。现有方法在几何上可靠的度量尺度重建、真实世界泛化能力与推理速度三方面均存在不足。

Method: 工作包含四部分：(1) 几何-时序优化，从野外第一人称立体视频获得度量 3D 手部标注，并结合视觉-惯性 SLAM 做世界坐标系重建；(2) WinDEX-Net，端到端鱼眼原生网络，融合标定立体、局部细节与时序上下文，并配套"模型辅助标注—重训练"的数据-模型飞轮；(3) 蒸馏模型，分别支持离线重建与因果流式推理，服务强化学习与遥操作；(4) 低成本 CyberCap 立体-惯性采集硬件，构建 WinDEX-Hand 数据集，含 1000 小时训练标注及留出的人工标注野外 2D 评测集与独立光学动捕评测集。

Result: 离线重建 43.61 FPS，因果流式 171 FPS。在留出数据上达到最优精度：野外重投影误差 14.5 像素；结合视觉-惯性里程计时世界坐标系关节误差 37.29 mm。以 WinDEX 动作参考训练的强化学习策略任务成功率分别达到 WiLoR 与 HaWoR 参考的 7.6 倍和 3.6 倍，并实现实时手臂-手部遥操作。

Conclusion: WinDEX 通过硬件、标注优化、网络设计与蒸馏部署的协同，首次在低成本条件下打通野外大规模手部数据采集与度量尺度重建，兼顾精度、泛化与实时性，并直接转化为下游机器人学习与遥操作的性能提升。

Abstract: Human manipulation data are widely used to train robots for human-like dexterity. However, low-cost, large-scale collection in the wild remains challenging. Existing methods remain limited in geometrically grounded metric-scale reconstruction, real-world generalization and inference speed. We present WinDEX, integrating (1) geometric-temporal optimization for metric 3D hand annotation from in-the-wild egocentric stereo videos, combined with visual-inertial SLAM for world-frame reconstruction; (2) WinDEX-Net, an end-to-end, fisheye-native network combining calibrated stereo, local detail and temporal context, with a data-model flywheel of model-assisted annotation and retraining; (3) distilled models for offline reconstruction and causal streaming, supporting reinforcement learning and teleoperation; and (4) low-cost CyberCap stereo-inertial capture, forming the WinDEX-Hand dataset with 1,000 hours of training annotations and held-out human-annotated in-the-wild 2D and independent optical motion-capture evaluation sets. Offline reconstruction runs at 43.61 FPS and causal streaming at 171 FPS. On held-out data, offline reconstruction achieves state-of-the-art accuracy: 14.5 pixels in-the-wild reprojection error and, with visual-inertial odometry, 37.29 mm world-frame joint error. We achieve 7.6 and 3.6 times the task success rates of reinforcement learning policies trained with WiLoR and HaWoR motion references, respectively, and demonstrate real-time arm-hand teleoperation. Project website: https://fruitrabbit.github.io/WinDEX/.

</details>


### [101] [Polygraph: Large-Scale Multi-Session Trajectory Optimization using Pose Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21163)
*Petar Lukovic,Tian Yi Lim,Marc Pollefeys,Pablo Speciale,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Polygraph 是一个在线的多会话轨迹优化流程，逐关键帧、逐空间簇地处理各次通行轨迹，不构建三维地图，通过分层共识门控与增量/周期批量优化融合成全局一致轨迹，在多平台数据上比同类方法更快、边更少且精度相当或更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、车辆与可穿戴设备会多次经过同一环境，每次通行由视觉或视觉惯性里程计产生局部准确但随时间漂移、且处于互不相关坐标系的轨迹。将这些独立轨迹融合为全局一致集合即大规模多会话轨迹优化问题，而现有方法多为离线：需预先具备全部会话、固定单个会话为参考系，并优化单一融合地图，难以适应持续在线运行的场景。

Method: 提出 Polygraph 在线流水线：关键帧按局部聚类分组，簇闭合时通过学习式图像检索与其他所有会话匹配；候选对用稀疏特征匹配、单目度量深度和鲁棒位姿估计验证；采用两层一致性门控，仅当独立证据相互印证时才将跨会话边加入共享位姿图；以低成本增量求解保持图最新，并周期性执行鲁棒批量求解剔除离群边；全程不构建三维地图。

Result: 在立体视觉户外驾驶、HoloLens 与 iPad 室内行走、Boston Dynamics Spot 足式室内运动，以及大规模户外车辆与手持路线等多平台数据上验证。在与最接近的先前工作使用相同 GPU 型号、沿用其报告耗时的条件下，Polygraph 运行更快、提交的回环边更少，精度达到或优于对方。

Conclusion: Polygraph 使多会话轨迹优化可由离线转为在线增量处理，通过严格证据门控与分层优化在减少回环边的同时保持或提升精度，为持续运行的多次通行场景提供更高效的全局一致轨迹融合方案。

Abstract: Robots, vehicles, and wearable devices often traverse the same environment many times. Each pass produces its own trajectory from onboard visual or visual-inertial odometry. These trajectories are locally accurate but drift over time and live in unrelated coordinate frames. Turning many such independent trajectories into one globally consistent set is the large-scale multi-session trajectory optimization problem. Existing solutions largely work offline. All sessions must be available up front, one session is fixed as the reference frame, and a single fused map is then optimized. This paper presents Polygraph, a multi-session trajectory optimization pipeline that processes sessions online, one keyframe and one spatial cluster at a time, and never builds a 3D map. Keyframes are grouped into local clusters. When a cluster closes, it is matched against every other session through learned image retrieval. Candidate pairs are verified with sparse feature matching, monocular metric depth, and robust pose estimation. A two-tier consensus gate admits a cross-session edge into the shared pose graph only once independent evidence corroborates it. A cheap incremental solve keeps the graph current, and a periodic robust batch solve prunes outliers. We evaluate Polygraph on stereo outdoor driving, HoloLens and iPad indoor traversals, legged indoor locomotion with a Boston Dynamics Spot, and large-scale outdoor vehicle and handheld routes. Against the closest prior work on the same GPU model, using its reported timings, Polygraph runs faster, commits fewer loop-closure edges, and matches or improves on accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Cooperative Mobile Manipulation using Learned Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21198)
*Saray Bakker,Wendelin Böhmer,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALDS框架，将单智能体学到的动力系统行为适配为多移动操作机器人的协同执行，实现全身避碰并保持任务相关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同移动操作需要机器人在协调运动的同时保证全身避碰，并保留与任务相关的行为。现有从示教中学到的单智能体行为难以直接用于多机协同。

Method: 基于从示教学习的个体动力系统（DS），设计两个关键组件：协同策略选择（Cooperative Policy Selection，识别相容的学习策略组合）与时间适配（Temporal Adaptation，在保持原始行为空间结构与收敛性质的同时确保无碰撞协同）。

Result: 在辅助穿衣、餐厅清洁与上菜、抽屉开启与物品放置三个场景中评估，定量结果显示其能提升任务成功率，实现安全协调的同步执行，并保持对示教行为的保真度。

Conclusion: CALDS可将单智能体示教行为有效扩展至多移动操作机器人的协同场景，兼顾安全性、协调性与行为保真度。

Abstract: Cooperative mobile manipulation requires robots to coordinate their motions while ensuring whole-body collision avoidance and preserving task-relevant behaviors. We propose CALDS (Cooperative Adaptation of Learned Dynamical Systems), a framework that enables mobile manipulators to adapt learned single-agent behaviors for coordinated execution. Building on individual dynamical systems (DSs) learned from demonstrations, CALDS enables cooperative adaptation through two key components: Cooperative Policy Selection, which identifies compatible combinations of learned policies, and Temporal Adaptation, which ensures collision-free cooperation while preserving the spatial structure and convergence properties of the original behaviors. We evaluate CALDS across three diverse scenarios: assistive dressing, restaurant cleaning and serving, and drawer opening and object placement. Quantitative results demonstrate its effectiveness in improving task success and enabling safe, coordinated simultaneous execution while maintaining fidelity to demonstrated behaviors.

</details>


### [103] [BoBDeLaN: Bounded-Bandwidth Context Adaptation for Deep Lagrangian Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21209)
*Lucas Schulze,Sucheth Shenoy,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BoBDeLaN，用收缩、增益有界的REN上下文编码器配合稳定线性滤波，理论上保证对测量扰动的频率选择性敏感度，并在MPC中验证其降低高频敏感度、提升力矩预测精度与运动平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文感知的深度拉格朗日网络虽能通过隐上下文适应不确定负载与未建模动态，但仅靠拉格朗日结构无法约束模型对测量扰动的敏感度，鲁棒性缺乏保证。

Method: 将收缩且增益有界的循环均衡网络（REN）上下文编码器与稳定线性滤波相结合，塑造跨频率的扰动敏感度；理论上给出循环初值的指数遗忘性，以及有限时域、频率加权的情境扰动界，并在上下文条件动力学模型满足Lipschitz连续假设下推广到残余力矩预测误差界。

Result: 在仿真与真实硬件操作任务中采用低通实现并嵌入模型预测控制，并进行真实机器人数据的频率敏感度分析；与使用相同输入滤波的LSTM编码器相比，BoBDeLaN高频情境敏感度更低、残余力矩预测误差更小、运动更平滑，在注入扰动下性能退化更小。

Conclusion: 将稳定性/收缩性结构引入上下文编码器可为物理启发学习模型提供可证扰動敏感度界，并带来实际控制性能与鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Learned physics-based models provide structure for robot control, but uncertain payloads and unmodeled dynamics limit their predictive accuracy. Context-aware Deep Lagrangian Networks enable adaptation through a latent context inferred from measurement histories while retaining Lagrangian structure for each fixed context. However, this structure alone does not constrain sensitivity to measurement disturbances. We introduce BoBDeLaN, which combines a contracting, gain-bounded Recurrent Equilibrium Network context encoder with stable linear filtering to shape disturbance sensitivity across frequencies. This architecture guarantees exponential forgetting of the recurrent initialization and admits a finite-horizon, frequency-weighted bound on context perturbations, which extends to residual torque prediction under a Lipschitz continuity assumption on the context-conditioned dynamics model. We evaluate a low-pass realization within model predictive control on simulated and real hardware manipulation tasks, together with frequency-sensitivity analysis on real-robot data. Compared with an LSTM encoder using the same input filter, BoBDeLaN exhibits reduced high-frequency context sensitivity, lower residual torque prediction error, and smoother motion, with less degradation under injected disturbances.

</details>


### [104] [ThinkWAM: Rethinking Latent Futures for Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21258)
*Haoyun Tang,Baqiao Deng,Enjin Zhou,Songwen Tian,Keyao Xu,Bowen Jing,Kun Wang,Ke Ma,Zhandong Mei,Huibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ThinkWAM，一种在潜在空间中通过"提案-重思-精炼"循环实现执行前双向反馈的世界-动作模型，在多个仿真与真实基准上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）中，初始动作块可能有缺陷，而不以该动作块为条件预测的未来状态无法反映所提动作将引起的视觉表征变化，从而限制了其在执行前用于动作精炼的价值。

Method: 设计提案-重思-精炼闭环：策略先提出完整动作块；该提案通过学习的残差修正多时域未来潜在表征；修正后的潜在表征注入同一动作头的第二次前向，生成可执行动作块。辅以受预测编码启发的可供性引导的观测-动作记忆，以及"近细远粗"的多时域预测方案，全部预测均在潜在空间进行。

Result: LIBERO 98.70%；无 OOD 微调下 LIBERO-Plus 84.76%；RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random 85.75%/65.85%；RoboCasa24 65.17%；真实世界三类 OOD 条件平均成功率 64.4%。两遍推理时延：LIBERO 153.48 ms、RoboTwin 164.61 ms、Galaxea A1Z 101.2 ms。消融显示重思与可供性引导收益互补；诊断表明提案条件化的修正同时改变预测未来与动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 执行前以动作提案为条件修正未来潜在表征，可有效精炼动作；修正后的未来表征确实提升了最终动作质量，且以较低的两遍推理开销实现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple action generation with predictions of future states. An initial action chunk may be flawed. A future prediction that is not conditioned on the chunk may fail to reflect the changes that the proposed actions would induce in visual representations, limiting its usefulness for refinement before execution. We introduce ThinkWAM, a latent WAM that establishes bidirectional feedback before execution through a proposal-rethink-refine loop: the policy first proposes a complete action chunk, the proposal corrects multi-horizon future latents through a learned residual, and the corrected latents are injected into a second pass of the same action head, which generates the executable chunk. An affordance-guided observation-action memory and a near-fine, far-coarse multi-horizon prediction scheme, both motivated by predictive coding, support this loop, and all prediction stays in latent space. ThinkWAM achieves 98.70% success on LIBERO, 84.76% on LIBERO-Plus without OOD fine-tuning, 85.75%/65.85% on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random, and 65.17% on RoboCasa24. ThinkWAM achieves 64.4% mean success across three real-world OOD conditions. The mean two-pass inference latency per action chunk is 153.48 ms on LIBERO, 164.61 ms on RoboTwin, and 101.2 ms on the Galaxea A1Z. Ablations show complementary gains from rethinking and affordance guidance. Diagnostics indicate that proposal-conditioned corrections change both predicted futures and action trajectories, showing that the corrected future representations improve the final action.

</details>


### [105] [Hybrid Optimization-Generative Motion Planning for Multiagent Affine Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21264)
*Zhonggang Li,Hongyu Zhou,Raj Thilak Rajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合优化-生成式框架用于仿射编队运动规划：将规划任务建模为约束最大后验估计，解耦多智能体目标以复用预训练单智能体扩散先验，并与投影梯度下降交替迭代以严格满足仿射几何约束和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编队机动依赖高质量、动态可行的先验参考轨迹，因此编队运动规划（FMP）不可或缺；但传统基于约束优化的方法在高维联合轨迹上面临严重计算挑战。

Method: 聚焦仿射编队以提供空间灵活性；将规划表述为约束最大后验（MAP）估计，解耦多智能体目标，利用预训练单智能体扩散先验保证动态可行性、避障与平滑性；再将生成过程与投影梯度下降（PGD）交替进行，严格施加仿射几何约束并提供安全保证。

Result: 仿真表明该框架能生成高度平滑、无碰撞的规划轨迹，可被常见分布式控制器直接跟踪，并在多项指标上优于现有先进规划器。

Conclusion: 通过优化与生成式方法的混合，可在避免高维联合优化计算负担的同时严格保证编队几何与安全约束，为编队运动规划提供了有效且易跟踪的方案。

Abstract: Formation maneuver control enables a multiagent system to cooperatively navigate through constrained environments in collective motion. While various formation controllers exist, they rely heavily on high-quality and dynamically feasible reference trajectories provided a priori, making formation motion planning (FMP) essential.
