To address the computational challenges for high-dimensional joint trajectories faced by conventional constrained optimization-based solutions, this paper introduces a hybrid optimization-generative framework with a focus on affine formations, which provide the necessary spatial flexibility. By formulating the planning task as a constrained Maximum-a-Posteriori estimation, we decouple multiagent objectives to leverage pre-trained single-agent diffusion priors for dynamic feasibility, obstacle avoidance, and smoothness. We then interleave the generative process with projected gradient descent (PGD) to strictly enforce affine geometric constraints with safety guarantees. Simulations demonstrate that our framework generates highly smooth, collision-free plans that are readily trackable by common distributed controllers and outperforms state-of-the-art planners in various metrics.

</details>


### [106] [Safe Physical Interaction of All-Electric Heavy-Duty Robotic Manipulators via Control Barrier Functions and Variable Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21309)
*Amir Hossein Barjini,Mohammad Bahari,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向EMLA驱动重型机械臂的安全感知物理交互架构，将变阻抗控制(VIC)与控制屏障函数(CBF)叠加在虚拟分解控制(VDC)框架之上，实现接近结构作业时的柔顺交互与硬约束安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全电重型机械臂需在隧道壁、设备等结构附近作业，同时保持大负载所需力能力；仅靠精确运动控制无法满足由此产生的安全与交互需求。

Method: 在VDC框架基础上融合VIC与CBF：VIC依据预测的接近与接触风险连续调节任务空间刚度和阻尼；关节速度CBF滤波器施加笛卡尔工作空间边界及闭链升降/俯仰机构引起的物理行程限制；并利用实测关节速度跟踪误差界对CBF不等式进行收紧，以补偿内层VDC/EMLA动态对滤波速度的非理想实现。

Result: 在四种配置(VDC、VIC+VDC、CBF+VDC、VIC+CBF+VDC)下于工作空间、执行器极限与物理交互场景评估：VIC使环境交互力峰值和RMS分别降低92.59%和96.33%；两种鲁棒CBF配置均保持正的工作空间与EMLA安全裕度；VIC+CBF+VDC相较CBF+VDC使CBF干预指标降低10.30%。

Conclusion: VIC与CBF可在VDC架构上互补集成，在保证执行器与工作空间安全约束的同时显著降低交互冲击力并减少不必要的屏障干预，适用于受限空间中的重型机械臂作业。

Abstract: All-electric heavy-duty robotic manipulators are increasingly expected to operate close to tunnel walls, equipment, and other structures while retaining the force capability required for large-payload tasks. Accurate motion control alone does not address the resulting safety and interaction requirements. This paper develops a safety-aware physical-interaction architecture that combines variable impedance control (VIC) with control barrier functions (CBFs) on top of an established virtual decomposition control (VDC) framework for electromechanical linear actuator (EMLA)-driven manipulators. VIC continuously adapts the task-space stiffness and damping according to predicted proximity and contact risk, whereas a joint-velocity CBF filter enforces Cartesian workspace boundaries and the physical stroke limits induced by the closed-chain lift and tilt mechanisms. To account for nonideal realization of the filtered velocity by the inner VDC/EMLA dynamics, the CBF inequalities are tightened using a measured bound on the joint-velocity tracking error. Four configurations, namely VDC, VIC+VDC, CBF+VDC, and VIC+CBF+VDC, are evaluated under workspace, actuator-limit, and physical-interaction scenarios. VIC reduces the peak and RMS environment-interaction forces by 92.59% and 96.33%, respectively; both robust CBF configurations maintain positive workspace and EMLA safety margins, while VIC+CBF+VDC reduces the CBF intervention metric by 10.30% compared with CBF+VDC.

</details>


### [107] [GHySSeg: Real-Time Geometric Ground and Road Segmentation for Hybrid Solid-State LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21507)
*Martín Palos,Fernando García*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出GHySSeg，一种纯几何方法，用于从混合固态LiDAR单帧中点云中分割地面与可行驶道路，并发布首个面向车载混合固态LiDAR地面/道路分割的公开基准HySoLiD-Drive。方法在无训练、无GPU条件下实时运行（约22ms/帧单核），在基准上全面超越几何与学习类SOTA，道路IoU 0.907、地面IoU 0.896，精度均超0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合固态LiDAR正进入量产车，但其数据无环结构、稠密、杂乱且集中于前向视场，与现有自动驾驶数据集依赖的360度旋转式机械LiDAR的有序环-列结构不同。传统几何方法（用于可行驶空间计算与导航）假设旋转式结构，在此类数据上退化或失效；学习方法计算开销大、难以认证，且几乎只基于旋转式LiDAR数据训练。作者认为这种稠密的逐曲线几何本身是一种机会，可弥合产业偏好传感器与自动驾驶研究之间的鸿沟。

Method: GHySSeg为纯几何方法：对单帧混合固态LiDAR数据逐条扫描曲线进行追踪，读取两种几何特征——高度上的台阶（step in height）与距离上的回折（fold-back in range），据此分割地面与可行驶道路；无需训练，也不依赖GPU。为评估方法，作者构建并发布HySoLiD-Drive基准，包含两辆车真实行驶中采集的100帧人工标注数据与7000帧无标注数据。

Result: 在HySoLiD-Drive基准上，GHySSeg在地面与道路分割任务上均超越几何与学习类SOTA基线，道路IoU达0.907、地面IoU达0.896，且精度均超过0.96；单核运行速度约22毫秒/帧，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 面向混合固态LiDAR的无环、稠密、前向集中点云，简单而高效的逐曲线几何特征（高度台阶与距离回折）足以实现高精度、可认证、实时的地面与道路分割；配套发布的HySoLiD-Drive基准填补了该传感器形态下评测数据的空白，有助于推动产业主流传感器与自动驾驶研究的对接。

Abstract: Hybrid solid-state LiDAR sensors are making their way into production vehicles, yet the data they produce differs structurally from that of the established spinning mechanical sensors behind today's autonomous-driving datasets: it is ring-free, dense, cluttered, and concentrated in a frontal field of view. The classical geometric methods that autonomous vehicles rely on for drivable space computation and navigation assume the ordered ring-and-column structure of a 360-degree spinning LiDAR and degrade or fail on this data, while learned alternatives are computationally heavy, hard to certify, and trained almost exclusively on spinning-LiDAR data. We demonstrate that the same dense, per-curve geometry is instead an opportunity to close the divide between the industry's preferred sensors and autonomous-driving research. We present GHySSeg, a purely geometric method that segments ground and drivable road from a single hybrid solid-state LiDAR frame by tracing each scan curve and reading two geometric signatures, a step in height and a fold-back in range, with no training and no graphics processing unit. To evaluate it, we release HySoLiD-Drive, the first public benchmark dedicated to ground and road segmentation on an automotive hybrid solid-state LiDAR, comprising 100 manually labeled and 7000 unlabeled frames recorded by two vehicles in real driving. On this benchmark, GHySSeg surpasses both geometric and learned state-of-the-art baselines across ground and road segmentation, reaching an intersection-over-union of 0.907 for road and 0.896 for ground at over 0.96 precision, while running in real time at about 22 milliseconds per frame on a single core.

</details>


### [108] [Lazy-MOSAIC: Accelerating Skill-Centric Manipulation Planning with Learned Skill-Effect World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21517)
*Itamar Mishani,Mukund Krishnakumar,Oren Salzman,Maxim Likhachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Lazy-MOSAIC：一种"懒惰"的长时程操作规划框架，在假设的技能效果空间中搜索，用毫秒级的学得技能效果世界模型预筛候选，把昂贵仿真集中在有希望的技能上，并在假设失效时只修复受影响的局部搜索，同时保证最终计划中每个技能都经过仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务需组合多种参数化技能，解空间是技能序列与参数的巨大混合空间；用高保真物理仿真评估每个候选技能可行性与效果代价极高。已有以技能为中心的MOSAIC方法虽能引导非序列化搜索指向成功区域，但对每个参数化技能都付出完整仿真代价，效率低下。

Method: 1) 在假设的技能结果（skill outcomes）上而非逐个参数化技能上搜索；2) 用廉价的学习型技能效果世界模型在不到一毫秒内预测技能可行性与产生的世界状态；3) 以预测结果聚焦仿真，仅对有希望的候选技能执行昂贵仿真；4) 当假设被仿真证伪时，仅修复搜索中受影响的部分；5) 通过仿真验证返回计划中的每一个技能，保证正确性。

Result: 在难度递增的杂乱货架取物任务上评测，并在实体机器人上演示：相比基线规划速度显著更快、仿真查询次数更少，且在每种设置下成功率持平或更高。

Conclusion: 将技能效果预测与仿真验证解耦（惰性假设+局部修复+全程验证）可在保持甚至提升成功率的同时大幅降低长时程技能规划的计算与仿真开销，并在真实机器人上可行。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks require a robot to perform a sequence of physical interactions with its environment, typically combining several different skills. Planning such behaviors efficiently is challenging: given a library of parameterized skills, solutions lie in a vast hybrid space of skill sequences and their parameters, and evaluating each candidate skill's feasibility and effect with a high-fidelity world model such as a physics simulator is computationally expensive. MOSAIC, a recent skill-centric approach, lets the skills guide a non-sequential search toward the regions of this space where they are likely to succeed; however, it pays the full cost of simulation for every parameterized skill it considers. In this work, we introduce Lazy-MOSAIC, a lazy planning framework that instead searches over hypothesized skill outcomes. A cheap learned skill-effect world model predicts, in a fraction of a millisecond, a skill's feasibility and the world state it produces. Lazy-MOSAIC then uses these predictions to focus expensive simulation on promising candidate skills and, when a hypothesis is invalidated, repairs only the affected part of the search, while ensuring that every skill in a returned plan is validated in simulation. We evaluate Lazy-MOSAIC on cluttered-shelf retrieval tasks of increasing difficulty and demonstrate it on a physical robot, showing that it plans substantially faster than baselines with fewer simulator queries, while matching or exceeding their success rate in every setting. Visit skill-mosaic.github.io.

</details>


### [109] [SADL: On the Demand-Supply Geometry of Representation Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21596)
*Junhan Sun,Yigui Feng,Mingzhe Liu,Haoyan Xu,Lei Ye,Ziqi Han,Hanru Bai,Yanzhao Zhang,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出JEPA类架构中表示坍缩的根源在于编码器从一开始就被输入共享成分主导，投影器的批归一化会消除该成分，导致投影器之后的正则无法纠正。为此提出SADL损失，直接约束编码器，在"供给"（编码器能保留的差异）不足于"需求"（投影器输出要求的差异）时才施加约束。在LeWM与INTACT多任务实验中，坍缩率从50%降至0%，首轮训练后有效秩提升3.4–6.4倍，潜空间预测误差降至1/20–1/303，并在控制与图像分类任务上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法依靠投影器输出上的正则化来防止表示坍缩，但作者发现这并不足够：从初始化起，编码器表示就被一个跨输入共享的成分主导，而批归一化投影器会把这个成分直接去掉，因此投影器之后的正则化无法修正编码器层面的这一问题，表示差异会持续缩小直至坍缩。

Method: 提出SADL（Supply And Demand Loss）。它比较"需求"——投影器输出所要求的表示差异，与"供给"——编码器能够保留并传递给投影器的差异；仅当供给不足于需求时，才对编码器直接施加约束。SADL保留原有训练目标，可与多种Demand Regularizer配合使用，且不引入辅助网络、额外可训练参数或架构改动。

Result: 在单任务及四任务联合训练的LeWorldModel（LeWM）与INTACT实验中，SADL把十次随机种子下的坍缩率从50%降到0%；训练一个epoch后，编码器有效秩为无SADL基线的3.4–6.4倍，潜空间预测误差降至基线的1/20–1/303。无需额外调参，损失权重跨多个数量级仍保持稳健；配合多种Demand Regularizer时，INTACT的无搜索平均控制成功率超过95%。在四个图像数据集上，SADL也稳定提升了不同随机种子下的准确率与稳定性。

Conclusion: 让正则化器所要求的表示差异与编码器所能提供的差异相匹配，可在控制与图像分类任务中带来更快、更稳定的表示学习，并从根本上缓解JEPA类架构的表示坍缩问题。

Abstract: In Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), a well-constrained projector output does not by itself prevent representation collapse. We find that, from initialization, the encoder representation is dominated by a component shared by different inputs. A batch-normalized projector removes this component, so regularization after the projector cannot correct it, and the differences between representations shrink until they collapse. We propose SADL (Supply And Demand Loss), which keeps the original training objective and constrains the encoder directly when needed. SADL compares the representation differences that the projector output demands with the differences the encoder can retain and pass on to the projector, and adds a constraint on the encoder only when supply falls short of demand. It works with different Demand Regularizers and introduces no auxiliary networks, additional trainable parameters, or architectural changes. In single-task and jointly trained four-task LeWorldModel (LeWM) and INTACT experiments, SADL lowers the collapse rate over ten random seeds from 50% to 0%. After one epoch of training, encoder effective rank is 3.4-6.4 times that of the corresponding baseline without SADL, and latent prediction error falls to 1/20-1/303 of it. Without additional tuning, SADL stays robust as its loss weight spans orders of magnitude, and with several Demand Regularizers INTACT exceeds 95% mean search-free control success. On four image datasets, SADL also consistently improves accuracy and stability across random seeds. These results show that matching the representation differences a regularizer demands with the differences the encoder can supply leads to faster and more stable representation learning in control and image classification.

</details>


### [110] [ScaleVLA: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models to 552 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21607)
*Ruixi Yu,Dairu Liu,Xuchuan Chen,Zekun Qi,Zekai Li,Zhuo Cao,Luyang Niu,Yingsheng Zhang,Sikai Liang,Ke Fan,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文ScaleVLA将552B的DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash视觉语言模型接入可训练的动作DiT，在统一训练设置下系统比较2B至552B不同规模VLM主干用于机器人策略学习的效果，发现前沿规模表征在样本效率、泛化和精细操作上全面占优，且仍能保持216ms的中位推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视觉语言模型（如GPT-6 Astra）已展现机器人控制能力，说明大规模预训练知识对物理动作有价值；但现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略仍普遍使用小得多的VLM主干，这一潜力未被充分利用。论文因此追问：前沿规模的表征能否为机器人学习提供更强的基座。

Method: 提出ScaleVLA：把552B参数的DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash主干与可训练的动作DiT连接，并在共享训练配置下对2B到552B的多个VLM进行系统对比；从样本效率、泛化、精细操作三项机器人基础模型能力进行评估（含LIBERO-PRO与真实世界充电器插入任务）。

Result: 样本效率：仅10条示范即超过用50条示范训练的2B主干（87.8% vs 85.6%）；泛化：LIBERO-PRO成功率45.1%，小规模主干最高仅31.3%；精细操作：真实充电器插入从4/10提升到9/10，增益集中在接触敏感的对齐环节；效率：尽管规模巨大，每个动作块中位延迟216ms。

Conclusion: 前沿VLM不仅能作为推理智能体，也可作为高效、可泛化机器人策略的强大表征基础；扩大VLM主干规模是提升VLA性能的有效路径，且大模型带来的延迟开销在实际部署中可接受。

Abstract: Frontier Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robot control, suggesting that broad pretraining provides knowledge valuable for physical action. Yet most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies still rely on much smaller VLM backbones, leaving this potential largely untapped. We ask whether frontier-scale representations can provide a stronger foundation for robot learning. We introduce ScaleVLA, which connects a 552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone to a trainable action DiT, and systematically compare VLMs from 2B to 552B under a shared training setup. We evaluate three fundamental capabilities of robot foundation models. In sample efficiency, just 10 demonstrations outperform a 2B backbone trained with 50 (87.8% vs. 85.6%). In generalization, LIBERO-PRO success reaches 45.1%, compared with at most 31.3% for smaller backbones. In precise manipulation, real-world charger insertion improves from 4/10 to 9/10 successes, with gains concentrated in contact-sensitive alignment. Despite its scale, ScaleVLA achieves 216 ms median latency per action chunk. Our findings suggest that frontier VLMs can serve not only as reasoning agents, but also as powerful representational foundations for efficient and generalizable robot policies.

</details>
